# SSVDAG — Symmetry-aware Sparse Voxel DAGs

**Real-time GPU raycasting of gigavoxel scenes from a losslessly compressed, symmetry-aware sparse voxel structure.**

This is the reference implementation of the SSVDAG papers by
[Alberto Jaspe-Villanueva](https://albertojaspe.net), Fabio Marton and Enrico Gobbetti
(CRS4 Visual Computing):

- **[SSVDAGs: Symmetry-aware Sparse Voxel DAGs](https://albertojaspe.net/publications/2016-I3D-ssvdags.html)**
  *ACM SIGGRAPH Symposium on Interactive 3D Graphics and Games (i3D), 2016* — Best Papers Selection
- **[Symmetry-aware Sparse Voxel DAGs (SSVDAGs) for compression-domain tracing of high-resolution geometric scenes](https://albertojaspe.net/publications/2017-JCGT-ssvdags.html)**
  *Journal of Computer Graphics Techniques (JCGT), 2017* — extended journal version

## What it does

Voxelized geometry grows cubically with resolution, which makes high-resolution
scenes explode in memory. A Sparse Voxel Octree (SVO) removes empty space; a
Sparse Voxel DAG additionally merges *identical* subtrees. SSVDAGs go one step
further and merge subtrees that are identical **up to a reflective symmetry**,
encoding the mirror transform in the parent pointer — cutting the structure
roughly in half again, losslessly, while remaining directly traversable on the
GPU in the compressed domain:

```
OBJ mesh ──► SVO ──► DAG ──► SSVDAG (symmetry-aware DAG)
                               │
                               ▼
                 real-time GPU raycasting (GLSL)
```

Typical numbers (see the papers for full benchmarks): a scene at 8K³–64K³
resolution traced at hundreds of FPS from a structure of ~0.1–1 bits per voxel.

## Tools

| Tool           | Purpose |
|----------------|---------|
| `builder`      | Full pipeline: mesh → SVO → DAG → all encoded formats (`.svo`, `.dag`, `.dag4`, `.sdag1`…`.sdag4`), with statistics |
| `builder_lite` | Minimal builder producing just the final encoded SDAG |
| `viewer`       | Interactive GPU raycaster for encoded `.dag` / `.dag4` / `.sdag1` / `.sdag4` files |
| `demo`         | Demo renderer (deferred shading, hard shadows, SSAO) on top of the same traversal |

## Encoded formats

The builder can emit the same voxelized scene at several compression stages,
each directly traversable by the GPU raycaster:

| Extension | Structure | Compression |
|-----------|-----------|-------------|
| `.svo`    | Raw sparse voxel octree | None — baseline. Empty space is skipped, but every subtree is stored explicitly |
| `.dag`    | Sparse Voxel DAG | Identical subtrees merged, as in the original DAG paper by [Kämpe, Sintorn & Assarsson, *High Resolution Sparse Voxel DAGs*, SIGGRAPH 2013](https://doi.org/10.1145/2461912.2462024) |
| `.dag4`   | DAG + compact node encoding | DAG with entropy-oriented node layout: variable-size pointers and tagged leaves |
| `.sdag1`  | **Symmetry-aware DAG** | Subtrees identical *up to reflection* merged too — the core SSVDAG contribution, plain node encoding |
| `.sdag4`  | **SSVDAG + compact node encoding** | Symmetry-aware merging *and* the variable-size entropy encoding — **the highest compression**, and the format benchmarked in the papers |

As a reference point, a 1024³ sphere gives 21.3 bits/voxel encoded SVO →
4.3 (`.dag`) → 2.4 (`.dag4`) → 0.69 (`.sdag1`) → **0.35 (`.sdag4`)**.
The remaining variants (`.sdag2`, `.sdag3`, `.sdag5`) are intermediate
experimental encodings kept for completeness.

## Building

Requirements:

- CMake ≥ 3.24
- A C++17 compiler (tested: MSVC 2022, GCC, Clang)
- A GPU + driver supporting OpenGL 4.4

All library dependencies (GLM, GLFW) are fetched automatically by CMake; the
OpenGL loader (glad) is vendored under `external/`. Nothing to install manually.

```sh
cmake -S . -B build
cmake --build build --config Release
```

Binaries land in `build/Release/` (multi-config generators) or `build/`
(single-config). The viewer and demo load their GLSL shaders from the source
tree (`shaders/`), whose location is baked in at configure time — run them
from any directory.

## Usage

Voxelize a mesh at 2^N resolution and write encoded octrees:

```sh
# 10 levels = 1024^3, monolithic in-core build (step 0)
builder model.obj 10 0 model.sdag1 model.dag

# 13 levels = 8K^3, split build: top 5 levels + parallel per-subtree DAGs
builder model.obj 13 4 model.sdag4
```

The third argument (`nBuildStep`) controls the divide-and-conquer build: `0`
builds the whole SVO in memory; `k > 0` builds the top `k+1` levels, then
constructs and DAG-compresses each occupied subtree independently and in
parallel — required for resolutions whose full SVO would not fit in RAM.

View an encoded file:

```sh
viewer model.sdag1
```

**Viewer controls**: drag = orbit · right-drag = zoom · middle-drag = pan ·
arrows / `Q` `E` = walk & turn · `SPACE` = reset · `U` = toggle Y/Z-up ·
`1`/`2` = draw level · `R` = render mode.

**Demo extras**: `L` = place light at camera · `K` = shadows · `A` = SSAO
(`5`/`6` radius) · `M` = materials · `P` = screenshot (+ reloadable camera/light
info file).

## Repository layout

```
src/voxelator     core library: scene loading, octree build, DAG/SSVDAG
                  reduction, encoded formats
src/voxelator_gl  OpenGL support library: camera, GLSL raycaster, HUD
src/builder*      command-line building tools
src/viewer        interactive raycasting viewer
src/demo          demo renderer (deferred + SSAO)
src/common        small self-contained helpers (AABB, timing, formatting,
                  memory-mapped external array, paths)
shaders/          GLSL raycasting & shading kernels
external/glad     vendored OpenGL 4.4 loader
```

### shaders/demo_bilateral_pass.frag.glsl
```
#version 440 core

// define MSIZE

uniform sampler2D inputTex;
layout(location = 0) out vec3 filteredOutput;

uniform float sigma;
uniform float bsigma;

vec3 getSample(const in ivec2 coords) {
	return texelFetch(inputTex, coords, 0).xyz;
}

float normpdf(const in float x, const in float sig) {
	return 0.39894*exp(-0.5*x*x/(sig*sig))/sig;
}

float normpdf3(const in vec3 v, const in float sig) {
	return 0.39894*exp(-0.5*dot(v,v)/(sig*sig))/sig;
}

void main()
{
	ivec2 pixCoords = ivec2(gl_FragCoord.xy);
	vec3 c = getSample(pixCoords);
	
	if(c == vec3(0,0,0)) discard;
	
	const int kSize = (MSIZE-1)/2;
	float kernel[MSIZE];
		
	//create the 1-D kernel
	float Z = 0;
	for (int j = 0; j <= kSize; ++j) {
		kernel[kSize+j] = kernel[kSize-j] = normpdf(float(j), sigma);
	}

	const float bZ = 1.0/normpdf(0, bsigma);
	vec3 final_colour = vec3(0);

	for (int i=-kSize; i <= kSize; ++i) {
		for (int j=-kSize; j <= kSize; ++j) {
			const vec3 cc = getSample(pixCoords + ivec2(i,j));
			const float factor = normpdf3(cc-c, bsigma)*bZ*kernel[kSize+j]*kernel[kSize+i];
			Z += factor;
			final_colour += factor*cc;
		}
	}

	filteredOutput = normalize(final_colour/Z);
}
```

### shaders/demo_blur_pass.frag.glsl
```
#version 440 core

uniform sampler2D inputTex;
layout(location = 0) out float filteredOutput;

uniform int kSize = 1;

float getSample(const in ivec2 coords) {
	return texelFetch(inputTex, coords, 0).x;
}

void main() {
	const ivec2 pixCoords = ivec2(gl_FragCoord.xy);
	float result = 0.0;
	for (int i=-kSize; i <= kSize; ++i) {
		for (int j=-kSize; j <= kSize; ++j) {
			result += getSample(pixCoords + ivec2(i,j));
		}
	}
	filteredOutput = result / float((2*kSize+1) * (2*kSize+1));
}
```

### shaders/demo_material_pass.frag.glsl
```
#version 440 core

uniform sampler2D depth;
uniform mat4 viewMatInv;
uniform mat4 projMatInv;
uniform vec2 screenRes;
uniform usamplerBuffer matOctree;
uniform float matOctreehalfSide;
uniform vec3 matOctreeCenter;
uniform int matOctreeLevels;

layout(location = 0) out uint materialOutput;

#define NULLMAT 255

struct Ray {
	vec3 o;
	vec3 d;
};


vec3 fromHomog(in const vec4 v) {
	return vec3(v.x/v.w, v.y/v.w, v.z/v.w);
}

Ray computeCameraRay(in const vec2 pixelScreenCoords) {
	vec4 pixel_s0 = vec4(pixelScreenCoords.x, pixelScreenCoords.y, 0, 1);
	vec4 pixel_s1 = vec4(pixelScreenCoords.x, pixelScreenCoords.y, 1, 1);

	vec3 pixel_w0 = fromHomog(projMatInv * pixel_s0);
	vec3 pixel_w1 = fromHomog(projMatInv * pixel_s1);

	Ray r;
	r.o = vec3(0,0,0);
	r.d = normalize(pixel_w1 - pixel_w0);

	vec3 o_prime = vec3(viewMatInv * vec4(r.o, 1));
	vec3 e_prime = vec3(viewMatInv * vec4(r.d, 1));
	r.o = o_prime;
	r.d = normalize(e_prime - o_prime);
	return r;
}

float getHalfSide(const in uint level) {
	return matOctreehalfSide / float(1U << level);
}

void main()
{
	const vec2 screenCoords = (gl_FragCoord.xy/screenRes) * 2.0 - 1.0;
	const Ray r = computeCameraRay(screenCoords);
	const float val = texelFetch(depth, ivec2(gl_FragCoord.xy), 0).w;
	if(val == 0) discard;
	const vec3 hitPos = r.o + val * r.d;

	// traverse material octree
	int nodeIdx = 0;
	int nodeLevel = 0;
	vec3 nodeCenter = matOctreeCenter;
	materialOutput = NULLMAT;
	
	while(true) {
		nodeLevel++;
		uvec2 node = texelFetch(matOctree, nodeIdx).xy;
		uint nodeMat = (node.x >> 16) & 0xFF;
		if ((nodeMat != NULLMAT) && (nodeLevel <= matOctreeLevels)) {
			materialOutput = nodeMat;
			//materialOutput = 0;//nodeLevel;
			return;
		} else {
			bvec3 gt = greaterThan(hitPos,	nodeCenter);
			uvec3 gti = uvec3(gt);
			uint selectedChild = 4 * gti.x + 2 * gti.y + gti.z;
			if(nodeLevel < matOctreeLevels) {
				nodeCenter += (vec3(gt) + (-1.0 * vec3(not(gt)))) * getHalfSide(nodeLevel);
				uint nodeMask = node.x & 0xFF;
				if((nodeMask & (1<<selectedChild)) == 0) {
					materialOutput = 254;
					return;
				}
				nodeIdx = int(node.y + bitCount(nodeMask >> selectedChild) - 1);
			} else {
				uvec2 leaf = texelFetch(matOctree, int(node.y)).xy;
				materialOutput = ((gti.x == 0 ? leaf.x : leaf.y) >> (8*(2 * gti.y + gti.z))) & 0xFF;
				//materialOutput = (leaf.y >> 0) & 0xFF;
				//materialOutput = NULLMAT;
				return;
			}
		}
	}
}
```

### shaders/demo_pos_norm_pass.frag.glsl
```
#version 440 core

uniform mat4 viewMatInv;
uniform mat4 projMatInv;
uniform vec2 screenRes;
uniform sampler2D depth;
uniform float projectionFactor;
uniform bool useDif;

#if MATERIALS
uniform usamplerBuffer materialOctree;
uniform int materialOctreeLevels;
#endif

layout(location = 0) out vec3 hitPosition;
layout(location = 1) out vec3 hitNormal;

#if MATERIALS
layout(location = 2) out int hitMaterial;
#endif

struct Ray {
	vec3 o;
	vec3 d;
};

vec3 fromHomog(in const vec4 v) {
	return vec3(v.x/v.w, v.y/v.w, v.z/v.w);
}

Ray computeCameraRay(in const vec2 pixelScreenCoords) {
	vec4 pixel_s0 = vec4(pixelScreenCoords.x, pixelScreenCoords.y, 0, 1);
	vec4 pixel_s1 = vec4(pixelScreenCoords.x, pixelScreenCoords.y, 1, 1);

	vec3 pixel_w0 = fromHomog(projMatInv * pixel_s0);
	vec3 pixel_w1 = fromHomog(projMatInv * pixel_s1);

	Ray r;
	r.o = vec3(0,0,0);
	r.d = normalize(pixel_w1 - pixel_w0);

	vec3 o_prime = vec3(viewMatInv * vec4(r.o, 1));
	vec3 e_prime = vec3(viewMatInv * vec4(r.d, 1));
	r.o = o_prime;
	r.d = normalize(e_prime - o_prime);
	return r;
}


vec3 getPos(const in float dx, const in float dy) {
	//vec2 coord = clamp(vec2(dx,dy), vec2(-100), vec2(100));
	vec2 coord = vec2(dx,dy);
	vec2 sc = (gl_FragCoord.xy +  coord) / screenRes;
	const Ray r = computeCameraRay(sc * 2.0 - 1.0);
	const float k = texture(depth, sc).x;
	//return (k>=0) ? r.o + k * r.d : vec3(1e30);
	return (k>=0) ? (r.o + k * r.d) - vec3(viewMatInv[3]) : vec3(1e30);
}

vec3 calcNormal(const in vec3 camDir, const in float dif) {
	
	vec3 dx, dy, dx1, dx2, dy1, dy2, n1, n2;
	const vec3 p = getPos(0,0);
	
	dx1 = getPos( dif,0) - p;
	dx2 = getPos(-dif,0) - p;
	dx  = (length(dx1)<length(dx2)) ? dx1 : -dx2;

	dy1 = getPos(0, dif) - p;
	dy2 = getPos(0,-dif) - p;
	dy  = (length(dy1)<length(dy2)) ? dy1 : -dy2;

	n1 = cross(dx,dy);
	if(dot(n1, camDir)>0) n1 = -n1;


	dx1 = getPos( dif, dif) - p;
	dx2 = getPos(-dif,-dif) - p;
	dx  = (length(dx1)<length(dx2)) ? dx1 : -dx2;
	
	dy1 = getPos(-dif, dif) - p;
	dy2 = getPos( dif,-dif) - p;
	dy  = (length(dy1)<length(dy2)) ? dy1 : -dy2;

	n2 = cross(dx,dy);
	if(dot(n2, camDir)>0) n2 = -n2;

	const float n1l = length(n1);
	const float n2l = length(n2);
	
	//vec3 n = normalize(n1+n2);
	vec3 n = normalize( (1.0-n1l/(n1l+n2l)) * normalize(n1) + (1.0-n2l/(n1l+n2l)) * normalize(n2));
	//vec3 n = normalize(n1l<n2l ? n1 : n2);

	if(dot(n, camDir)>0) n = -n;

	return n;
}

#if MATERIALS

int getMaterial(vec3 p) {
	unsigned int ptr = 0;
	vec3 nodeCenter = 
}

#endif

void main()
{
	vec2 val = texelFetch(depth, ivec2(gl_FragCoord.xy), 0).xy;
	if(val.x <= 0) discard;
	const vec2 screenCoords = (gl_FragCoord.xy/screenRes) * 2.0 - 1.0;
	const Ray r = computeCameraRay(screenCoords);
	hitPosition = r.o + val.x * r.d;

	float dif = 1.0;
	if (useDif) dif = max(0.001, 2.0*(val.y*projectionFactor) / val.x);
#define MINK 5.0
#define MAXK 20.0
	if(dif>MINK) dif = mix(dif, 1.0, min(dif-MINK, MAXK-MINK)/(MAXK-MINK));
	hitNormal = calcNormal(r.d, dif);
}
```

### shaders/demo_shading_pass.frag.glsl
```
#version 440 core

uniform int isYUp;
uniform vec3 camPos;
uniform mat4 projMatInv;
uniform mat4 viewMatInv;
uniform vec2 screenRes;
uniform vec3 lightPos;
uniform int renderMode;
uniform bool useMaterials;
uniform sampler2D hitPosTex;
uniform sampler2D depthTex;
uniform sampler2D hitNormTex;
uniform sampler2D shadowsTex;
uniform sampler2D aoTex;
uniform usampler2D materialTex;


#ifdef USE_MATERIALS
uniform vec3 materials[NUM_MATERIALS];
#endif

out vec3 color;

const float lightSize = .5;


vec3 getSkyColor(in float y) {
	y = max(0, ((((y/2.0)+0.5)*1.2)-0.2));
    vec3 c;
    const float oneMinusY = 1.0 - y;
    c.r = pow(oneMinusY,2.0) - 0.2;
    c.g = oneMinusY - 0.2;
    c.b = 0.3 + oneMinusY*0.5;
    return c;
}

vec3 fromHomog(in const vec4 v) {
	return v.xyz/v.w;
}


vec3 computeCameraRayDir(in const vec2 pixelScreenCoords) {
	const vec4 pixel_s0 = vec4(pixelScreenCoords.x, pixelScreenCoords.y, 0, 1);
	const vec4 pixel_s1 = vec4(pixelScreenCoords.x, pixelScreenCoords.y, 1, 1);
	
	const vec3 pixel_w0 = fromHomog(projMatInv * pixel_s0);
	const vec3 pixel_w1 = fromHomog(projMatInv * pixel_s1);
	
	vec3 ro = vec3(0,0,0);
	vec3 rd = normalize(pixel_w1 - pixel_w0);
	
	const vec3 o_prime = vec3(viewMatInv * vec4(ro, 1));
	const vec3 e_prime = vec3(viewMatInv * vec4(rd, 1));
	rd = normalize(e_prime - o_prime);
	return rd;
}

void main()
{
	const ivec2 pixCoords = ivec2(gl_FragCoord.xy);
	const vec2 screenCoords = (gl_FragCoord.xy/screenRes) * 2.0 - 1.0;
	const vec3 camRayDir = computeCameraRayDir(screenCoords);
	const vec3 pos = texelFetch(hitPosTex, pixCoords, 0).xyz;

#if 1 // draw light ball 
	if(dot(lightPos - camPos, camRayDir)>0) {
		float a = dot(camRayDir, camRayDir);
		float b = 2.0 * dot(camRayDir, camPos - lightPos);
		float c = dot(lightPos, lightPos) + dot(camPos, camPos) - 2.0 * dot(lightPos, camPos) - lightSize*lightSize;
		float test = b*b - 4.0*a*c;
		if(test >= 0.0 && length(lightPos - camPos) < length(pos - camPos)) { 
			color = vec3(1.0);
			return;
		}
	}
#endif

	if(pos == vec3(0,0,0)) { // background
		color = getSkyColor((isYUp==0) ? camRayDir.z : camRayDir.y);
	} else {
		const vec3  norm = texelFetch(hitNormTex, pixCoords, 0).xyz;
		const float shadow = texelFetch(shadowsTex, pixCoords, 0).x;
		const float ao = texelFetch(aoTex, pixCoords, 0).x;

		if(renderMode == 0) {
			vec3 diffuseColor = vec3(0.8, 0.8, 0.8);
#ifdef USE_MATERIALS
			if(useMaterials) {
				uint matID = texelFetch(materialTex, pixCoords, 0).x;
				if(matID<NUM_MATERIALS) {
					diffuseColor = materials[matID];
				} else {
					if(matID == 255) 
						color = vec3(1.0, 0.0, 1.0);
					else
						color = vec3(0.1, 1.0, 0.9);
					return;
				}
			}
#endif
			const vec3 ambientColor = vec3(0.6, 0.6, 0.6);
			const vec3 lightVec = normalize(lightPos-pos);
			const vec3 diffuseShading = 
				0.2 * max(0,dot(norm,-camRayDir)) * diffuseColor  // head light
				+ 0.9 * max(0,dot(norm,lightVec)) * diffuseColor * max(shadow,0.1) // actual light
				+ 0.2 * ambientColor; // ambient
				color = diffuseShading * max(ao,0.1);
				color = pow(color, vec3(0.9)); // a bit of gamma :-)
		}
		else if (renderMode == 1) {
			color = abs(norm);
		}
		else if (renderMode == 2) {
			color = vec3(shadow);
		}
		else if (renderMode == 3) {
			color = vec3(ao);
		} 
		else if (renderMode == 4) {
			const float steps = texelFetch(depthTex, pixCoords, 0).z;
			color = vec3(1. - (steps / 256.0));
		} else {
			discard;
		}
	}
}
```

### shaders/demo_ssao_pass.frag.glsl
```
#version 440 core

uniform mat4 projMat;
uniform mat4 viewMat;
uniform vec3 camPos;
uniform sampler2D hitPosTex;
uniform sampler2D hitNormTex;
uniform sampler2D depthTex;
uniform sampler2D noiseTex;
uniform vec3 samples[NUM_SAMPLES];
uniform float radius;

layout(location = 0) out float outVisibility;


void main() {
	const ivec2 coord = ivec2(gl_FragCoord.xy);
	vec3 hitPos = texelFetch(hitPosTex, coord, 0).xyz;
	if(hitPos == vec3(0,0,0)) discard;
	const vec3 hitNorm = texelFetch(hitNormTex, coord, 0).xyz;
	const vec2 hitDepth_cellSize = texelFetch(depthTex, coord, 0).xy;
	hitPos += hitNorm * hitDepth_cellSize.y/5.0;

	vec3 rvec = texelFetch(noiseTex, coord%SIDE_NOISE_TEX, 0).xyz;
	vec3 tangent = normalize(rvec - hitNorm * dot(rvec, hitNorm));
	vec3 bitangent = cross(hitNorm, tangent);
	mat3 tbn = mat3(tangent, bitangent, hitNorm);

	float occlusion = 0.0;
	float count = 0.0;
	for (int i = 0; i < NUM_SAMPLES; ++i) {
		// get sample position:
		vec3 samp = tbn * samples[i];
		samp = hitPos + samp * radius;
  
		// project sample position:
		vec4 offset = vec4(samp, 1.0);
		offset = projMat * viewMat * offset;
		offset.xyz /= offset.w;
		offset.xyz = offset.xyz * 0.5 + 0.5;
  
  		if(all(lessThanEqual(offset.xy, vec2(1,1))) && all(greaterThanEqual(offset.xy, vec2(0,0)))) {
  
			float sampleDepth = texture(depthTex, offset.xy).x;
			//float rangeCheck = smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, radius / abs(hitDepth_cellSize.x - sampleDepth ));
			float rangeCheck = (abs(hitDepth_cellSize.x - sampleDepth) < radius) ? 1.0 : 0.0;	
			occlusion += (sampleDepth < length(camPos - samp) ? 1.0 : 0.0) * rangeCheck;
			count += 1.0;
		}
	}
	outVisibility = (count > 0.0) ? 1.0 - occlusion / count : 1.0;
}
```

### shaders/octree_dda.frag.glsl
```
#version 440 core

// To define:
// DAG || SDAG1 || SDAG4 || DAG4
// SDAG4_TEX3D
// VIEWER_MODE || DEPTH_MODE || SHADOW_MODE || AO_MODE
// N_HS_SAMPLES

uniform mat4 viewMatInv;
uniform mat4 viewMat2Inv;
uniform mat4 projMatInv;
uniform vec2 screenRes;

uniform vec3 sceneBBoxMin;
uniform vec3 sceneBBoxMax;
uniform vec3 sceneCenter;
uniform float rootHalfSide;

uniform uint maxIters;
uniform uint drawLevel;
uniform float projectionFactor;

#define TEST_CHILD_INDIR_TEX 1

#if (DAG || SDAG1)
uniform usamplerBuffer nodes;
#elif (SDAG4 || DAG4)
#if SDAG4_TEX3D
uniform usampler3D innerNodes;
#else
uniform usamplerBuffer innerNodes;
#endif
uniform usamplerBuffer leafNodes;
uniform uint levelOffsets[INNER_LEVELS]; // INNER_LEVELS defined from app

#if TEST_CHILD_INDIR_TEX
uniform isamplerBuffer childIndir;
#endif 

#endif

#if VIEWER_MODE
uniform int viewerRenderMode;
uniform bool doStereo;
#elif SHADOW_MODE
uniform sampler2D hitPosTex;
uniform sampler2D hitNormTex;
uniform sampler2D depthTex;
uniform vec3 lightPos;
#elif AO_MODE
uniform sampler2D hitPosTex;
uniform sampler2D hitNormTex;
uniform sampler2D depthTex;
uniform vec3 hsSamples[N_HS_SAMPLES];
uniform int numAORays;
uniform float lengthAORays;
#endif

#if (VIEWER_MODE || DEPTH_MODE)
uniform sampler2D minDepthTex;
uniform bool useMinDepthTex = false;
#endif

#if VIEWER_MODE
layout(location = 0) out vec3 color; // a debug colour
#elif DEPTH_MODE
layout(location = 0) out vec4 output_t; // (t, level, iters, t_inside)
#elif (SHADOW_MODE || AO_MODE)
layout(location = 0) out float output_t;
#endif

struct Ray {
	vec3 o;
	vec3 d;
};

struct traversal_status {
	float t_current;
	int node_index;
	uint hdr;
	ivec3 mirror_mask;
	uvec2 leaf_data;
	
	ivec3 idx; 
	ivec3 local_idx;  uint child_linear_index; // Could be merged
	vec3 t_next_crossing; // exit of current voxel on x,y,z
	vec3 inv_ray_d;
	ivec3 delta_idx;
	int current_node_size;
	
	float cell_size;
	uint level;
};

//////////////////////////// STACK STUFF ////////////////////

#define MAX_STACK_DEPTH (INNER_LEVELS+1)

ivec3 stack[MAX_STACK_DEPTH];
uint stack_size = 0;

void stack_push(in const int node, in const uint hdr, in const ivec3 mirror_mask, in const uint level) {
	const int mask = mirror_mask.x | (mirror_mask.y << 1) | (mirror_mask.z << 2) | int(level << 3);
	stack[stack_size] = ivec3(node, hdr, mask);
	++stack_size;
}

void stack_pop_in(out int node, out uint hdr, out ivec3 mirror_mask, out uint level) {
	--stack_size;
	const ivec3 node_mask = stack[stack_size];
	node = node_mask.x;
	hdr = node_mask.y;
	const int mask = node_mask.z;
	mirror_mask = ivec3(mask & 1, (mask >> 1) & 1, (mask >> 2) & 1);
	level = uint(mask >> 3) & 255;
}

bool stack_is_empty() {
	return stack_size == 0;
}

uint voxel_to_linear_idx(in const ivec3 mirror_mask, in ivec3 idx, in const int sz) {
	idx = (ivec3(1) - 2 * mirror_mask) * idx + mirror_mask * (sz-1);
	return uint(idx.z + sz * (idx.y + sz * idx.x));
}

#if DAG // ----------------------------------------------------------------------

#define LEAF_SIZE 2

uint myFetch(in const int idx) {
	const uvec4 tmp = texelFetch(nodes, idx/4);
	const int selected = idx%4;
	uint result;
	if      (selected == 0) result = tmp.x;
	else if (selected == 1) result = tmp.y;
	else if (selected == 2) result = tmp.z;
	else if (selected == 3) result = tmp.w;
	return result;
}

bool fetch_voxel_bit(in const traversal_status ts) {
	return (ts.hdr & (1 << ts.child_linear_index)) != 0;
}

void fetch_data(inout traversal_status ts) {
	ts.hdr = myFetch(ts.node_index);
}

void fetch_child_index_in(inout traversal_status ts) {
	
	const int childPtrPos = bitCount((ts.hdr & 0xFF) >> ts.child_linear_index);
	ts.node_index = int(myFetch(ts.node_index + childPtrPos));
}

#elif SDAG1 // ------------------------------------------------------------------

#define LEAF_SIZE 2

uint myFetch(in const int idx) {
	const uvec4 tmp = texelFetch(nodes, idx/4);
	const int selected = idx%4;
	uint result;
	if      (selected == 0) result = tmp.x;
	else if (selected == 1) result = tmp.y;
	else if (selected == 2) result = tmp.z;
	else if (selected == 3) result = tmp.w;
	return result;
}

bool fetch_voxel_bit(in const traversal_status ts) {
	return (ts.hdr & (1 << ts.child_linear_index)) != 0;
}

void fetch_data(inout traversal_status ts) {
	ts.hdr = myFetch(ts.node_index);
}

void fetch_child_index_in(inout traversal_status ts) {
	
	const int childPtrPos = bitCount((ts.hdr & 0xFF) >> ts.child_linear_index);
	ts.node_index = int(myFetch(ts.node_index + childPtrPos));
	ivec3 m = ivec3(0,0,0); // FIXME optimize
	if((ts.hdr & (1 << (ts.child_linear_index +  8))) != 0) m.x = 1;
	if((ts.hdr & (1 << (ts.child_linear_index + 16))) != 0) m.y = 1;
	if((ts.hdr & (1 << (ts.child_linear_index + 24))) != 0) m.z = 1;
	ts.mirror_mask ^= m;
}

#elif SDAG4 // -----------------------------------------------------------------------

#define LEAF_SIZE 4

#if SDAG4_TEX3D

ivec2 myInnerFetch(in const int idx) {
	ivec2 result;
	result.x = int(texelFetch( innerNodes, 
		ivec3(
			idx % TEX3D_SIZE,
			(idx/TEX3D_SIZE) % TEX3D_SIZE,
			idx/(TEX3D_SIZE_POW2)
			),
		0).x);
	const int idx2 = idx + 1;
	result.y = int(texelFetch( innerNodes, 
		ivec3(
			idx2 % TEX3D_SIZE,
			(idx2/TEX3D_SIZE) % TEX3D_SIZE,
			idx2/(TEX3D_SIZE_POW2)
			),
		0).x);
	
	return result;
}

uint myInnerHeaderFetch(in const int idx) {
	return texelFetch( innerNodes, 
		ivec3(
			idx % TEX3D_SIZE,
			(idx/TEX3D_SIZE) % TEX3D_SIZE,
			idx/(TEX3D_SIZE_POW2)
			),
		0).x;
}
#else
ivec2 myInnerFetch(in const int idx) {
	const int realIdx = idx/4;
	const ivec4 tmp = ivec4(texelFetch(innerNodes, realIdx));
	const int selected = idx - realIdx * 4;
	ivec2 result;
	if      (selected == 0) result = tmp.xy;
	else if (selected == 1) result = tmp.yz;
	else if (selected == 2) result = tmp.zw;
	else if (selected == 3) {
		result.x = tmp.w;
		result.y = int(texelFetch(innerNodes, realIdx+1).x);
	}
	return result;
}

uint myInnerHeaderFetch(in const int idx) {
	const int realIdx = idx/4;
	const uvec4 tmp = texelFetch(innerNodes, realIdx);
	const int selected = idx - realIdx * 4;
	uint result;
	if      (selected == 0) result = tmp.x;
	else if (selected == 1) result = tmp.y;
	else if (selected == 2) result = tmp.z;
	else if (selected == 3) result = tmp.w;
	return result;
}

#endif
int get_child_mask(in const uint hdr, in const uint child_id) {
	return int((hdr >> (child_id<<1)) & 3U);
}

bool leaf_fetch_voxel_bit(in const traversal_status ts) {
	const uint word  = (ts.child_linear_index & 32);
	const uint bit_mask = 1 << (ts.child_linear_index & 31);
	const uint value = (word == 0 ? ts.leaf_data.x : ts.leaf_data.y) & bit_mask;
	return value != 0;
}

bool fetch_voxel_bit(in const traversal_status ts) {
	// for inner nodes, true if at least 1 of the 2 corresponding mask bits != 0
	// For leafs if corresponding voxel bit is not empty
	return (ts.level < INNER_LEVELS ?
		(get_child_mask(ts.hdr, ts.child_linear_index) != 0) :
		leaf_fetch_voxel_bit(ts));
}

void fetch_data(inout traversal_status ts) {
	if (ts.level < INNER_LEVELS)
		ts.hdr = myInnerHeaderFetch(ts.node_index + int(levelOffsets[ts.level]));
	else
		ts.leaf_data = texelFetch(leafNodes, ts.node_index).xy;
}

void fetch_child_index_in(inout traversal_status ts) {
	// Compute offset to get child pointer information
	int node_offset = 1 + int(levelOffsets[ts.level]) + ts.node_index;
#if TEST_CHILD_INDIR_TEX
	node_offset += texelFetch(childIndir, int(ts.hdr + 65536 * ts.child_linear_index)).x;
#else
	for (uint i = 7; i > ts.child_linear_index; --i) {
		node_offset += min(get_child_mask(ts.hdr, i), 2);
	}
#endif
	// Read child pointer and child mask
	//int child_index = int(texelFetch(innerNodes, node_offset).x);
	ivec2 child_index = myInnerFetch(node_offset);
	const int child_mask = get_child_mask(ts.hdr, ts.child_linear_index);
	
	// read mirror info and remove this info from childidx
	ivec3 mirror;
	mirror.x = ((child_index.x >> 13) & 1);
	mirror.y = ((child_index.x >> 14) & 1);
	mirror.z = ((child_index.x >> 15) & 1);
	child_index.x &= -57345; // ~(7 << 13);
	
	if (child_mask > 1) {
		//int tmp = int(texelFetch(innerNodes, node_offset+1).x);
		//const int tmp = int(myInnerFetch(node_offset+1));
		child_index.x = (child_index.x << 16) | child_index.y;
		const int x_bit = child_mask & 1;
		child_index.x |= (x_bit << 29);
	}
	
	// Update accumulated mirror mask, node and its header from next level
	ts.mirror_mask ^= mirror;
	ts.node_index = child_index.x;
}

			idx/(TEX3D_SIZE_POW2)

		ivec3(
			idx2 % TEX3D_SIZE,
			(idx2/TEX3D_SIZE) % TEX3D_SIZE,
			idx2/(TEX3D_SIZE_POW2)
			),
		0).x);
	
	return result;
}

		ivec3(
			idx % TEX3D_SIZE,
			(idx/TEX3D_SIZE) % TEX3D_SIZE,
			idx/(TEX3D_SIZE_POW2)
			),
		0).x;
}
#else
ivec2 myInnerFetch(in const int idx) {
	const int realIdx = idx/4;
	const ivec4 tmp = ivec4(texelFetch(innerNodes, realIdx));
	const int selected = idx - realIdx * 4;
	ivec2 result;
	if      (selected == 0) result = tmp.xy;
	else if (selected == 1) result = tmp.yz;
	else if (selected == 2) result = tmp.zw;
	else if (selected == 3) {
		result.x = tmp.w;
		result.y = int(texelFetch(innerNodes, realIdx+1).x);
	}
	return result;
}

uint myInnerHeaderFetch(in const int idx) {
	const int realIdx = idx/4;
	const uvec4 tmp = texelFetch(innerNodes, realIdx);
	const int selected = idx - realIdx * 4;
	uint result;
	if      (selected == 0) result = tmp.x;
	else if (selected == 1) result = tmp.y;
	else if (selected == 2) result = tmp.z;
	else if (selected == 3) result = tmp.w;
	return result;

#endif
int get_child_mask(in const uint hdr, in const uint child_id) {
	return int((hdr >> (child_id<<1)) & 3U);
}

bool leaf_fetch_voxel_bit(in const traversal_status ts) {
	uint word  = (ts.child_linear_index & 32);
	uint bit_mask = 1 << (ts.child_linear_index & 31);
	uint value = (word == 0 ? ts.leaf_data.x : ts.leaf_data.y) & bit_mask;
	return value != 0;
}

bool fetch_voxel_bit(in const traversal_status ts) {
	// for inner nodes, true if at least 1 of the 2 corresponding mask bits != 0
	// For leafs if corresponding voxel bit is not empty
	return (ts.level < INNER_LEVELS ?
		(get_child_mask(ts.hdr, ts.child_linear_index) != 0) :
		leaf_fetch_voxel_bit(ts));
}

void fetch_data(inout traversal_status ts) {
	if (ts.level < INNER_LEVELS)
		ts.hdr = myInnerHeaderFetch(ts.node_index + int(levelOffsets[ts.level]));
	else
		ts.leaf_data = texelFetch(leafNodes, ts.node_index).xy;
}

void fetch_child_index_in(inout traversal_status ts) {
	// Compute offset to get child pointer information
	int node_offset = 1 + int(levelOffsets[ts.level]) + ts.node_index;
	for (uint i = 7; i > ts.child_linear_index; i--) {
		node_offset += min(get_child_mask(ts.hdr, i), 2);
	}
	// Read child pointer and child mask
	//int child_index = int(texelFetch(innerNodes, node_offset).x);
	ivec2 child_index = myInnerFetch(node_offset);
	const int child_mask = get_child_mask(ts.hdr, ts.child_linear_index);
	
	// read mirror info and remove this info from childidx
	
	//ivec3 mirror;
	//mirror.x = ((child_index.x >> 13) & 1);
	//mirror.y = ((child_index.x >> 14) & 1);
	//mirror.z = ((child_index.x >> 15) & 1);
	
	//child_index.x &= -57345; // ~(7 << 13);
	
	if (child_mask > 1) {
		//int tmp = int(texelFetch(innerNodes, node_offset+1).x);
		//const int tmp = int(myInnerFetch(node_offset+1));
		child_index.x = (child_index.x << 16) | child_index.y;
		const int x_bit = child_mask & 1;
		child_index.x |= (x_bit << 29);
	}
	
	// Update accumulated mirror mask, node and its header from next level
	//ts.mirror_mask ^= mirror;
	ts.node_index = child_index.x;
}

///////////////////////////// DDA PRIMITIVES

bool in_bounds(in const ivec3 local_idx, in const int sz) { 
	const bvec3 cond0 = lessThan(local_idx, ivec3(sz));
	const bvec3 cond1 = lessThanEqual(ivec3(0,0,0), local_idx);
	return cond0.x && cond0.y && cond0.z && cond1.x && cond1.y && cond1.z;
}

vec2 intersectAABB(in const Ray r, in const vec3 aabbMin, in const vec3 aabbMax) {
  const vec3 t1 = (aabbMin - r.o)/r.d;
  const vec3 t2 = (aabbMax - r.o)/r.d;
  const vec3 tMin = min(t1, t2);
  const vec3 tMax = max(t1, t2);

  vec2 t = vec2(max(max(tMin.x, 0.0), max(tMin.y, tMin.z)), min(tMax.x, min(tMax.y, tMax.z)));
  return t;
}

bool resolution_ok(float t, float cell_size, float projection_factor) {
  return (cell_size * projection_factor) < t;
}

// ==========================================================================

void dda_init(in const Ray r, inout traversal_status ts) {
	// Init dda FIXME USE OCTREE POINT LOCATION
	const float voxel_eps = 1.0f/(256.*1024.);
	const vec3 p_a = r.o + (ts.t_current + voxel_eps) * r.d; // find current pos
	ts.idx = ivec3(p_a / ts.cell_size); // current global grid voxel
	
	// During initialization do not step back for dir < 0, because it would move of more than once cell
	const ivec3 delta_idx_conservative = max(ivec3(0), ts.delta_idx);
	const ivec3 idx_next = ts.idx + delta_idx_conservative;
	const vec3 p_next_a = idx_next * ts.cell_size;	// this should be the plane
	
	ts.t_next_crossing = (p_next_a - r.o) * ts.inv_ray_d;
	ts.local_idx = ts.idx % 2;
}


void dda_next(inout traversal_status ts) {
	const bvec3 b1 = lessThan(ts.t_next_crossing.xyz, ts.t_next_crossing.yzx);
	const bvec3 b2 = lessThanEqual(ts.t_next_crossing.xyz, ts.t_next_crossing.zxy);
	//const bvec3 mask = b1 && b2;
	const bvec3 mask = bvec3(b1.x && b2.x, b1.y && b2.y, b1.z && b2.z);
	const vec3 mask_v3 = vec3(mask); 			
	
	//All components of mask are false except the one components to the shortest t_next_crossing
	// which is the direction in which the step have to be done
	const ivec3 delta = ivec3(mask) * ts.delta_idx;
	ts.idx += delta;
	ts.local_idx += delta;
	
	ts.t_current = dot(mask_v3, ts.t_next_crossing);
	ts.t_next_crossing += mask_v3 * ts.cell_size * abs(ts.inv_ray_d);
}
 	     
ivec3 dda_next_delta_index(in const traversal_status ts) {
	const bvec3 b1 = lessThan(ts.t_next_crossing.xyz, ts.t_next_crossing.yzx);
	const bvec3 b2 = lessThanEqual(ts.t_next_crossing.xyz, ts.t_next_crossing.zxy);
	//const bvec3 mask = b1 && b2;
	const bvec3 mask = bvec3(b1.x && b2.x, b1.y && b2.y, b1.z && b2.z);
	return ivec3(mask) * ts.delta_idx;
}

void up_in(in const Ray r, inout traversal_status ts) {
	uint delta_level = ts.level;
	stack_pop_in(ts.node_index, ts.hdr, ts.mirror_mask, ts.level);
	delta_level -= ts.level;
	
	ts.idx >>= delta_level; // always delta_level >= 1
	ts.cell_size *= (1 << delta_level);  
	ts.current_node_size = ts.level < INNER_LEVELS ? 2 : LEAF_SIZE;
	ts.local_idx = ts.idx & 1; 
	
	const ivec3 delta_idx_conservative = max(ivec3(0), ts.delta_idx);
	const ivec3 idx_next = ts.idx + delta_idx_conservative;
	const vec3 p_next_a = idx_next * ts.cell_size;	// this should be the plane
	ts.t_next_crossing = (p_next_a - r.o) * ts.inv_ray_d;
}

void go_down_one_level(in const Ray r, inout traversal_status ts) {
	++ts.level;
	ts.cell_size*=0.5;
	
	// Init ts idx, t_next_crossing, local_idx using octree point location
	const vec3 p_a = r.o + ts.t_current * r.d;		
	const vec3 p_center = (ts.idx * 2 + 1) * ts.cell_size;
	const bvec3 child_pos = lessThan(p_center, p_a);
	const ivec3 delta = ivec3(child_pos);
	ts.idx = ts.idx*2 + delta;
	
	const ivec3 delta_idx_conservative = max(ivec3(0), ts.delta_idx);
	const ivec3 idx_next = ts.idx + delta_idx_conservative;
	const vec3 p_next_a = idx_next * ts.cell_size;	// this should be the plane
	
	ts.t_next_crossing = (p_next_a - r.o) * ts.inv_ray_d;
	ts.local_idx = ts.idx & (ts.current_node_size-1);
}

void down_in(in const Ray r, inout traversal_status ts) {
	// Check/push next
	const ivec3 local_idx = ts.local_idx;
	const ivec3 delta = dda_next_delta_index(ts);    
	
	if (in_bounds(local_idx + delta, 2)) { 
		stack_push(ts.node_index, ts.hdr, ts.mirror_mask, ts.level); 
	}
	  
	// Go down to next level: Fetch child index (store in node_idx)
	// and update accumulated_mirror_mask
	fetch_child_index_in(ts);
	
	go_down_one_level(r, ts);
	
	if (ts.level == INNER_LEVELS) {
		// GO TO LEAVES
		ts.current_node_size = LEAF_SIZE;
		int voxel_count = LEAF_SIZE / 2;
		while (voxel_count > 1) {
			go_down_one_level(r, ts);
			voxel_count >>= 1;
		}
	}
}


bool transform_ray(inout Ray r, inout vec2 t_min_max)  {
	const float epsilon = 1E-4f;
	const vec3 sign_rd = sign(r.d);
	
	// Move ray to LOCAL box
	const float scale = 1.0/(2.0 * rootHalfSide);
	const vec3 octree_min = sceneCenter - vec3(rootHalfSide);
	const vec3 octree_max = sceneCenter + vec3(rootHalfSide);
	r.o = r.o - octree_min;
	r.o *= scale;
	t_min_max *= scale;
	
	// avoid div by zero
	if (r.d.x * sign_rd.x < epsilon) r.d.x = sign_rd.x * epsilon;
	if (r.d.y * sign_rd.y < epsilon) r.d.y = sign_rd.y * epsilon;
	if (r.d.z * sign_rd.z < epsilon) r.d.z = sign_rd.z * epsilon;
	
	const vec3 clip_box_min = (sceneBBoxMin - octree_min) * scale; 
	const vec3 clip_box_max = (sceneBBoxMax - octree_min) * scale; 
	
	const vec2 t_intersection = intersectAABB(r, clip_box_min, clip_box_max);
	
	t_min_max.x = max(t_intersection.x, t_min_max.x + 1e-10);
	t_min_max.y = min(t_intersection.y, t_min_max.y);
	
	return t_intersection.x < t_intersection.y;
}


void init(inout Ray r, inout traversal_status ts) {
	ts.inv_ray_d = vec3(1.0/r.d);
	ts.delta_idx = ivec3(sign(r.d));
	
	// Level status
	ts.mirror_mask = ivec3(0,0,0);
	ts.level = 0;
	ts.cell_size = 0.5;
	
	// Step status
	dda_init(r, ts);
	ts.current_node_size = 2;
	
	ts.node_index = 0;
	fetch_data(ts);
	ts.child_linear_index =  voxel_to_linear_idx(ts.mirror_mask, ts.local_idx, ts.current_node_size);
}

// TRACE RAY
// returns vec3
//	X: intersection t
//		 >= 0 := intersection!
//		-1   := inside scene bbox, but no intersection
//		-2   := -2 out of t bounds
//		-3   := too many iterations used (> maxIters)
//		-4   := out of scene bbox
//	Y: level of the intersection (-1 => no intersection)
//	Z: num Iterations used.
//  W: extended t, inside voxel

vec4 trace_ray(in Ray r, in vec2 t_min_max, const in float projection_factor) {
	
	if (!transform_ray(r, t_min_max))
		return vec4(-4.0,0,0,0); // out of scene Bbox
	
	const float scale = 2.0 * rootHalfSide;
	traversal_status ts;
	ts.t_current = t_min_max.x;
	init(r, ts);
	
	int iteration_count = 0;
	const uint max_level = min(INNER_LEVELS, drawLevel-1);
	do {
		bool full_voxel = fetch_voxel_bit(ts);
	  
		if (!full_voxel) {
			dda_next(ts);
			if (!in_bounds(ts.local_idx, ts.current_node_size)) {
				if (stack_is_empty()) {
					return vec4(-1.,0,float(iteration_count),0); // inside scene BBox, but no intersection
				}
				up_in(r, ts);
			}	
		} else {
			const bool hit = (ts.level >= max_level || resolution_ok(ts.t_current, ts.cell_size, projection_factor)); 
			if (hit) {
#if DEPTH_MODE
				float t_hit = ts.t_current;
				dda_next(ts);
				return vec4(t_hit * scale, ts.cell_size * scale, float(iteration_count), (t_hit + ts.t_current)*0.5f * scale);  // intersection
#else
				return vec4(ts.t_current * scale, ts.cell_size * scale, float(iteration_count), 0);  // intersection
#endif
			} else {
				down_in(r, ts);
				fetch_data(ts);
			}
		}
	    
		ts.child_linear_index = voxel_to_linear_idx(ts.mirror_mask, ts.local_idx, ts.current_node_size);
		++iteration_count;
	} while ((ts.t_current < t_min_max.y) && (iteration_count < maxIters));
	
	if (iteration_count >= maxIters) return vec4(-3.,0,float(iteration_count),0); // too much itarations
	return vec4(-2.,0,float(iteration_count),0); // intersection out of t bounds
}


vec3 fromHomog(in const vec4 v) {
	return v.xyz/v.w;
}


Ray computeCameraRay(in const vec2 pixelScreenCoords) {
	const vec4 pixel_s0 = vec4(pixelScreenCoords.x, pixelScreenCoords.y, 0, 1);
	const vec4 pixel_s1 = vec4(pixelScreenCoords.x, pixelScreenCoords.y, 1, 1);
	
	const vec3 pixel_w0 = fromHomog(projMatInv * pixel_s0);
	const vec3 pixel_w1 = fromHomog(projMatInv * pixel_s1);
	
	Ray r;
	r.o = vec3(0,0,0);
	r.d = normalize(pixel_w1 - pixel_w0);
#ifdef VIEWER_MODE
	const vec3 o_prime = vec3( (doStereo && (int(gl_FragCoord.y) % 2 == 0) ? viewMat2Inv : viewMatInv) * vec4(r.o, 1));
	const vec3 e_prime = vec3( (doStereo && (int(gl_FragCoord.y) % 2 == 0) ? viewMat2Inv : viewMatInv) * vec4(r.d, 1));
#else
	const vec3 o_prime = vec3( viewMatInv * vec4(r.o, 1));
	const vec3 e_prime = vec3( viewMatInv * vec4(r.d, 1));
#endif
	r.o = o_prime;
	r.d = normalize(e_prime - o_prime);
	return r;
}

#if (VIEWER_MODE || DEPTH_MODE)

float getMinT(const in int delta) {
	
	const ivec2 p = ivec2(gl_FragCoord.xy / delta);

	float tl = texelFetch(minDepthTex, ivec2(p.x, p.y), 0).x;
	float tr = texelFetch(minDepthTex, ivec2(p.x+1, p.y), 0).x;
	float bl = texelFetch(minDepthTex, ivec2(p.x, p.y+1), 0).x;
	float br = texelFetch(minDepthTex, ivec2(p.x+1, p.y+1), 0).x;

	return min(min(tl, tr), min(bl, br));
}

#endif

///////////////////////// MAIN STUFF ////////////////////////////////////


#if VIEWER_MODE

void main() {
	const vec2 screenCoords = (gl_FragCoord.xy/screenRes) * 2.0 - 1.0;
	Ray r = computeCameraRay(screenCoords);

#if 0 // DEBUG for seeing the low-res first-depth pre-pass
	if(useMinDepthTex) {
		//color = texture(minDepthTex, (gl_FragCoord.xy/screenRes) ).xxx/10000.0f;
		color = vec3(getMinT(1) / length(sceneBBoxMax-sceneBBoxMin));
		return;
	}
#endif
	vec2 t_min_max = vec2(useMinDepthTex ? getMinT(1) : 0, 1e30);
	vec4 result = trace_ray(r, t_min_max, projectionFactor);
	if (result.x >= 0) // Intersection!!!
	{
		float t = 0;
		if(viewerRenderMode == 0) // ITERATIONS
			t =  1. - (result.z / float(maxIters));
		else if(viewerRenderMode == 1) // DEPTH
			t = result.x / length(sceneBBoxMax-sceneBBoxMin);
		else if (viewerRenderMode == 2) // VOXEL LEVELS
			t = log2(2. * rootHalfSide / result.y) / float(LEVELS);
	
		color = vec3(t, t, t);
	}
	else {
		if (result.x == -4.) // no intersection, out of BBox => background
		{ 
			color = 0.5 * (screenCoords.y + 1) * vec3(0.3,0.2,0.9);
		}
		else if (result.x == -1. || result.x == -2.) // inside BBox, but no intersection
		{ 
			float t = (viewerRenderMode == 0) ? 1. - (result.z / float(maxIters)) : 1.;
			color = vec3(t,t*0.5,0);
		}
		else if (result.x == -3.) // too many iterations
		{
			color = vec3(1.,0,0);
		}
		else // never should happen
		{
			discard; 
		}
	}
}

#elif DEPTH_MODE

void main() {
	const vec2 screenCoords = (gl_FragCoord.xy/screenRes) * 2.0 - 1.0;
	Ray r = computeCameraRay(screenCoords);	
	vec2 t_min_max = vec2(useMinDepthTex ? getMinT(1) : 0, 1e30);
	vec4 result = trace_ray(r, t_min_max, projectionFactor);
	if (result.x > 0) // Intersection!!!
		output_t = result;
	else
		discard;
}

#elif SHADOW_MODE 

void main() {
	const ivec2 coord = ivec2(gl_FragCoord.xy);
	const vec3 hitPos = texelFetch(hitPosTex, coord, 0).xyz;
	if(hitPos == vec3(0,0,0)) discard;
	const float cellSize = texelFetch(depthTex, coord, 0).y;
	const vec3 hitNorm = texelFetch(hitNormTex, coord, 0).xyz;

	const vec3 p = hitPos + hitNorm * cellSize * 1.0;

	Ray r;
	r.o = lightPos;
	const vec3 lightToP = p - lightPos;
	const float lightToPLength = length(lightToP);
	r.d = normalize(lightToP);
	vec2 t_min_max = vec2(0, lightToPLength);
	const float projFactor = lightToPLength / cellSize;
	vec4 result = trace_ray(r, t_min_max, projFactor);

	output_t = (result.x > 0) ? 0.0 : 1.0;
}

#elif AO_MODE 

void main() {
	const ivec2 coord = ivec2(gl_FragCoord.xy);
	vec3 hitPos = texelFetch(hitPosTex, coord, 0).xyz;
	if(hitPos == vec3(0,0,0)) discard;
	const vec3 hitNorm = texelFetch(hitNormTex, coord, 0).xyz;
	const float cellSize = texelFetch(depthTex, coord, 0).y;
	hitPos += hitNorm * 1e-3;

	Ray aoRay;
	aoRay.o = hitPos;

	//const vec3 rvec = gl_FragCoord.xyz; // TODO review this !!!
	const vec3 rvec = vec3(0,1,0); // TODO review this !!!
	//const vec3 rvec = normalize(vec3(gl_FragCoord.xy, gl_FragCoord.x * gl_FragCoord.y));
	//const vec3 rvec = normalize(vec3(gl_FragCoord.x, 0, gl_FragCoord.y));
	const vec3 tangent = normalize(rvec - hitNorm * dot(rvec, hitNorm));
	const vec3 bitangent = cross(hitNorm, tangent);
	//const mat3 tnb = mat3(tangent, bitangent, hitNorm);
	const mat3 tnb = mat3(tangent, hitNorm, bitangent);
	float largo = 0.5;
	const vec2 t_min_max = vec2(0,largo);
	const float projFactor = largo / cellSize;
	float k = 0;

	for(int i=0; i<numAORays; i++) {
		aoRay.d = normalize(tnb *  hsSamples[(i+int(gl_FragCoord.x*gl_FragCoord.y))%N_HS_SAMPLES]);
		//aoRay.d = normalize(tnb *  hsSamples[i]);
		vec4 result = trace_ray(aoRay, t_min_max, projFactor);
		if(result.x > 0) k += 1.0;// - (result.x/0.3);
	}
	float visibility = (numAORays>0) ? 1.0 - (k/float(numAORays)) : 1.0;
	output_t = visibility * visibility;
}

#endif
```

### src/builder/main.cpp
```
#include <fstream>
#include <common/format.hpp>
#include <common/timing.hpp>
#include <common/aabb.hpp>
#include <common/paths.hpp>
#include <string>
#include <string.h>

#include <voxelator/scene.hpp>
#include <voxelator/geom_octree.hpp>
#include <voxelator/material_octree.hpp>
#include <voxelator/encoded_svo.hpp>
#include <voxelator/encoded_dag.hpp>
#include <voxelator/encoded_dag4.hpp>
#include <voxelator/encoded_sdag1.hpp>
#include <voxelator/encoded_sdag2.hpp>
#include <voxelator/encoded_sdag3.hpp>
#include <voxelator/encoded_sdag4.hpp>
#include <voxelator/encoded_sdag5.hpp>

//#define BUILD_MAT_OCTREE
#define DO_LOG_LINE // Log line to stderr for batching results


int main(int argc, char ** argv) {

	if (argc < 4) {
		printf("Use: builder input_model(obj or tsp) nLevels nBuildStep <output.[svo, dag, sdag1, sdag2, sdag3]> ...\n");
		exit(1);
	}

	std::string inputFile(argv[1]);
	int nLevels = atoi(argv[2]);
	int levelStep = atoi(argv[3]);

	printf("\n===============================================================================\n");
	printf("   MODEL: '%s'   [ %d levels, step %i ]   (%.0fK^3)\n", inputFile.c_str(), nLevels, levelStep, pow(2, nLevels) / 1024.f);
	printf("===============================================================================\n\n");

	vox::WallClock ck;
	ck.restart();

	Scene scene;

	if (strstr(inputFile.c_str(), ".tsp") || strstr(inputFile.c_str(), ".TSP")) {
		scene.loadTriSoup(inputFile, levelStep == 0 ? false : true);
	} else if (strstr(inputFile.c_str(), ".obj") || strstr(inputFile.c_str(), ".OBJ")) {
#ifdef BUILD_MAT_OCTREE
		scene.loadObj(inputFile, false, true, false, false);
#else
		scene.loadObj(inputFile, true, false, false, false);
#endif
	}
	else {
		printf("Can't read input file '%s'\n", inputFile.c_str());
		exit(1);
	}

	GeomOctree geomOctree(&scene);

	vox::AABB3d sceneBBoxD = vox::AABB3d(scene.getAABB());

	EncodedSVO svo;
	if (levelStep == 0) {
#ifdef BUILD_MAT_OCTREE
		geomOctree.buildSVO(nLevels, sceneBBoxD, false, NULL, true);
		MaterialOctree matOctree;
		matOctree.setSceneParams(scene);
		matOctree.build(geomOctree);
		matOctree.encode();
		matOctree.save(vox::path_without_extension(inputFile) + "_" + std::to_string(nLevels) + ".mto");
		geomOctree.clearLeavesPointers();
#else
		geomOctree.buildSVO(nLevels, sceneBBoxD);
#endif
		svo.encode(geomOctree);
		geomOctree.toDAG();
	}
	else {
		geomOctree.buildDAG(nLevels, levelStep, sceneBBoxD, true);
	}

#if 0
	if (!octree.checkIntegrity()) {
		printf("Something wrong!\n");
		return 1;
	}
#endif

	EncodedDAG dag; dag.encode(geomOctree);
	EncodedDAG4 dag4; dag4.encode(geomOctree);

	geomOctree.toSDAG();
	//geomOctree.toSDAGCanonical();

	EncodedSDAG1 sdag1; sdag1.encode(geomOctree);
	EncodedSDAG2 sdag2; sdag2.encode(geomOctree);
	EncodedSDAG3 sdag3; sdag3.encode(geomOctree);
	EncodedSDAG4 sdag4; sdag4.encode(geomOctree);
	EncodedSDAG5 sdag5; sdag5.encode(geomOctree);

	for (int i = 4; i < argc; ++i) {
		std::string outputFile = argv[i];
		if     (strstr(outputFile.c_str(), ".svo") || strstr(outputFile.c_str(), ".SVO")) {
			if (levelStep == 0) svo.save(outputFile);
		}
		else if (strstr(outputFile.c_str(), ".dag4") || strstr(outputFile.c_str(), ".DAG4")) {
			dag4.save(outputFile);
		}
		else if (strstr(outputFile.c_str(), ".dag") || strstr(outputFile.c_str(), ".DAG")) {
			dag.save(outputFile);
		}
		else if (strstr(outputFile.c_str(), ".sdag1") || strstr(outputFile.c_str(), ".SDAG1")) {
			sdag1.save(outputFile);
		}
		else if (strstr(outputFile.c_str(), ".sdag2") || strstr(outputFile.c_str(), ".SDAG2")) {
			sdag2.save(outputFile);
		}
		else if (strstr(outputFile.c_str(), ".sdag3") || strstr(outputFile.c_str(), ".SDAG3")) {
			sdag3.save(outputFile);
		}
		else if (strstr(outputFile.c_str(), ".sdag4") || strstr(outputFile.c_str(), ".SDAG4")) {
			sdag4.save(outputFile);
		}
		else if (strstr(outputFile.c_str(), ".sdag5") || strstr(outputFile.c_str(), ".SDAG5")) {
			sdag5.save(outputFile);
		}
	}

	vox::time_duration totalTime = ck.elapsed();

	GeomOctree::Stats stats = geomOctree.getStats();

	// WARNING! Pointless SVO stats are THEORICAL
	// meaning that, is calculated as 1 byte / node.

	printf("\n========= RESULTS '%s' [%d levels] (%.0fK^3) =========\n", inputFile.c_str(), nLevels, pow(2, nLevels) / 1024.f);
	printf("Voxels:     %s\t(%zu)\n", vox::human_readable_quantity(stats.nTotalVoxels).c_str(), stats.nTotalVoxels);
	printf("SVO Nodes:  %s\t(%zu)\n", vox::human_readable_quantity(stats.nNodesSVO).c_str(), stats.nNodesSVO);
	printf("DAG Nodes:  %s\t(%zu)\n", vox::human_readable_quantity(stats.nNodesDAG).c_str(), stats.nNodesDAG);
	printf("SDAG Nodes: %s\t(%zu)\n", vox::human_readable_quantity(stats.nNodesSDAG).c_str(), stats.nNodesSDAG);
	printf("Pointerless SVO : %s\t(%.3f bits/vox)\n", vox::human_readable_size(stats.nNodesSVO).c_str(), (8 * stats.nNodesSVO) / float(geomOctree.getNVoxels()));
	if(levelStep == 0) printf("Encoded SVO     : %s\t(%.3f bits/vox)\n", vox::human_readable_size(svo.getDataSize()).c_str(), (8 * svo.getDataSize()) / float(geomOctree.getNVoxels()));
	printf("Encoded DAG     : %s\t(%.3f bits/vox)\n", vox::human_readable_size(dag.getDataSize()).c_str(),   (8 * dag.getDataSize())   / float(geomOctree.getNVoxels()));
	printf("Encoded DAG4    : %s\t(%.3f bits/vox)\n", vox::human_readable_size(dag4.getDataSize()).c_str(),  (8 * dag4.getDataSize())  / float(geomOctree.getNVoxels()));
	printf("Encoded SDAG1   : %s\t(%.3f bits/vox)\n", vox::human_readable_size(sdag1.getDataSize()).c_str(), (8 * sdag1.getDataSize()) / float(geomOctree.getNVoxels()));
	printf("Encoded SDAG2   : %s\t(%.3f bits/vox)\n", vox::human_readable_size(sdag2.getDataSize()).c_str(), (8 * sdag2.getDataSize()) / float(geomOctree.getNVoxels()));
	printf("Encoded SDAG3   : %s\t(%.3f bits/vox)\n", vox::human_readable_size(sdag3.getDataSize()).c_str(), (8 * sdag3.getDataSize()) / float(geomOctree.getNVoxels()));
	printf("Encoded SDAG4   : %s\t(%.3f bits/vox)\n", vox::human_readable_size(sdag4.getDataSize()).c_str(), (8 * sdag4.getDataSize()) / float(geomOctree.getNVoxels()));
	printf("SDAG4 / DAG     : %.1f %%\n", 100.f * sdag4.getDataSize() / (float)dag.getDataSize());
	printf("SDAG4 / PL_SVO  : %.1f %%\n", 100.f * sdag4.getDataSize() / (float)stats.nNodesSVO);
	printf("To DAG time     : %s\n", vox::human_readable_duration(stats.toDAGTime).c_str());
	printf("To SDAG time    : %s\n", vox::human_readable_duration(stats.toSDAGTime).c_str());
	printf("Total time      : %s\n", vox::human_readable_duration(totalTime).c_str());
	printf("===================================================================\n\n");
	
#ifdef DO_LOG_LINE
	fprintf(stderr, "%s\t%i\t%.0fK\t%.3f\t%.3f\t%.3f\t%.3f\t%.2f\t%.2f\t%.2f\t%.2f\t%.2f\t%.2f\t%.3f\t%.3f\t%.3f\t%.3f\t%.3f\t%.3f\t%.3f\t%.3f\t%.3f\n",
		argv[1], // model name
		nLevels, // levels
		pow(2, nLevels) / 1024.f, // resolution
		stats.nTotalVoxels / 1000000.f, // voxels (M)
		stats.nNodesSVO / 1000000.f, // nodes Pointeless SVO (M)
		stats.nNodesDAG / 1000000.f, // nodes DAG (M)
		stats.nNodesSDAG / 1000000.f, // nodes SDAG (M)
		stats.nNodesSVO / (1024.f*1024.f), // size Pointeless SVO (MB)
		dag.getDataSize() / (1024.f*1024.f), // size encoded DAG (MB)
		sdag1.getDataSize() / (1024.f*1024.f), // size encoded SDAG2 (MB)
		sdag2.getDataSize() / (1024.f*1024.f), // size encoded SDAG2 (MB)
		sdag3.getDataSize() / (1024.f*1024.f), // size encoded SDAG3 (MB)
		sdag4.getDataSize() / (1024.f*1024.f), // size encoded SDAG3 (MB)
		(8.f * stats.nNodesSVO) / float(geomOctree.getNVoxels()), // bitrate Pointeless SVO
		(8.f * dag.getDataSize()) / float(geomOctree.getNVoxels()),   // bitrate DAG
		(8.f * dag4.getDataSize()) / float(geomOctree.getNVoxels()),  // bitrate DAG4
		(8.f * sdag1.getDataSize()) / float(geomOctree.getNVoxels()), // bitrate SDAG1
		(8.f * sdag2.getDataSize()) / float(geomOctree.getNVoxels()), // bitrate SDAG2
		(8.f * sdag3.getDataSize()) / float(geomOctree.getNVoxels()), // bitrate SDAG3
		(8.f * sdag4.getDataSize()) / float(geomOctree.getNVoxels()), // bitrate SDAG3
		(float)stats.toDAGTime.count(),
		(float)stats.toSDAGTime.count()
	);
#endif

	return 0;
}

### src/builder_lite/main.cpp
```
#include <fstream>
#include <common/format.hpp>
#include <common/timing.hpp>
#include <common/aabb.hpp>
#include <string>
#include <string.h>

#include <voxelator/scene.hpp>
#include <voxelator/geom_octree.hpp>
#include <voxelator/encoded_svo.hpp>
#include <voxelator/encoded_dag.hpp>
#include <voxelator/encoded_sdag4.hpp>

int main(int argc, char ** argv) {

	if (argc < 3) {
		printf("Use: builder_lite input_model.obj nLevels\n");
		exit(1);
	}

	std::string inputFile(argv[1]);
	int nLevels = atoi(argv[2]);

	printf("\n===============================================================================\n");
	printf("   MODEL: '%s'   [ %d levels ]   (%.0fK^3)\n", inputFile.c_str(), nLevels, pow(2, nLevels) / 1024.f);
	printf("===============================================================================\n\n");

	vox::WallClock ck;
	ck.restart();

	Scene scene;

	if (strstr(inputFile.c_str(), ".obj") || strstr(inputFile.c_str(), ".OBJ")) {
		scene.loadObj(inputFile, true, false, false, false);
	}
	else {
		printf("Can't read input file '%s'\n", inputFile.c_str());
		exit(1);
	}

	GeomOctree octree(&scene);

	vox::AABB3d sceneBBoxD = vox::AABB3d(scene.getAABB());

	octree.buildSVO(nLevels, sceneBBoxD);
	EncodedSVO svo; svo.encode(octree);
	octree.toDAG();
	EncodedDAG dag; dag.encode(octree);
	octree.toSDAG();
	EncodedSDAG4 sdag4; sdag4.encode(octree);

	vox::time_duration totalTime = ck.elapsed();

	GeomOctree::Stats stats = octree.getStats();

	// WARNING! Pointless SVO stats are THEORICAL
	// meaning that, is calculated as 1 byte x node.

	printf("\n========= RESULTS '%s' [%d levels] (%.0fK^3) =========\n", inputFile.c_str(), nLevels, pow(2, nLevels) / 1024.f);
	printf("Voxels:     %s\t(%zu)\n", vox::human_readable_quantity(stats.nTotalVoxels).c_str(), stats.nTotalVoxels);
	printf("SVO Nodes:  %s\t(%zu)\n", vox::human_readable_quantity(stats.nNodesSVO).c_str(), stats.nNodesSVO);
	printf("DAG Nodes:  %s\t(%zu)\n", vox::human_readable_quantity(stats.nNodesDAG).c_str(), stats.nNodesDAG);
	printf("SDAG Nodes: %s\t(%zu)\n", vox::human_readable_quantity(stats.nNodesSDAG).c_str(), stats.nNodesSDAG);
	printf("Pointerless SVO : %s\t(%.3f bits/vox)\n", vox::human_readable_size(stats.nNodesSVO).c_str(), (8 * stats.nNodesSVO) / float(octree.getNVoxels()));
	printf("Encoded SVO     : %s\t(%.3f bits/vox)\n", vox::human_readable_size(svo.getDataSize()).c_str(), (8 * svo.getDataSize()) / float(octree.getNVoxels()));
	printf("Encoded SDAG   : %s\t(%.3f bits/vox)\n", vox::human_readable_size(sdag4.getDataSize()).c_str(), (8 * sdag4.getDataSize()) / float(octree.getNVoxels()));
	printf("SDAG / DAG     : %.1f %%\n", 100.f * sdag4.getDataSize() / (float)dag.getDataSize());
	printf("SDAG / PL_SVO  : %.1f %%\n", 100.f * sdag4.getDataSize() / (float)stats.nNodesSVO);
	printf("Total time      : %s\n", vox::human_readable_duration(totalTime).c_str());
	printf("===================================================================\n\n");
	
	return 0;
}
