### src/voxelator/encoded_dag4.cpp
```
#include <iostream>
#include <glm/gtc/type_ptr.hpp>
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <common/format.hpp>
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>
#include <tuple>

#include <voxelator/encoded_dag4.hpp>

const int EncodedDAG4::sizesMask[] = { 0,1,2,2 };

EncodedDAG4::EncodedDAG4() {
	compute_compact_histogram_enabled_ = false;
	voxel_matrix_order_enabled_ = true;
	check_decode_enabled_ = true;
}

bool EncodedDAG4::load(const std::string filename)
{
	printf("* Loading DAG4 '%s'... ", filename.c_str()); fflush(stdout);
	std::ifstream ifs;
	ifs.open(filename, std::ios::in | std::ios::binary);
	if (!ifs.is_open()) {
		printf("FAILED!!!\n");
		return false;
	}

	_dataInner.clear();

	ifs.read((char *)glm::value_ptr(_sceneBBox[0]), 12);
	ifs.read((char *)glm::value_ptr(_sceneBBox[1]), 12);
	ifs.read((char *)&_rootSide, 4);
	ifs.read((char *)&_levels, 4);
	ifs.read((char *)&_nNodes, 4);
	unsigned int count;
	// inner nodes
	count = (unsigned int)_dataInner.size();
	ifs.read((char *)&count, 4);
	_dataInner.resize(count);
	ifs.read((char *)_dataInner.data(), count * sizeof(std::uint16_t));
	// leaves
	count = (unsigned int)_dataLeaves.size();
	ifs.read((char *)&count, 4);
	_dataLeaves.resize(count);
	ifs.read((char *)_dataLeaves.data(), count * sizeof(std::uint8_t));
	// level offset table
	count = (unsigned int)_levelOffsets.size();
	ifs.read((char *)&count, 4);
	_levelOffsets.resize(count);
	ifs.read((char *)_levelOffsets.data(), count * sizeof(std::uint32_t));

	printf("OK!\n");
	return true;
}

bool EncodedDAG4::save(const std::string filename, const bool onlyHeader) const
{
	printf("* Saving DAG4 '%s'... ", filename.c_str()); fflush(stdout);

	std::ofstream ofs;
	ofs.open(filename, std::ios::out | std::ios::binary);
	if (!ofs.is_open()) {
		printf("FAILED!\n");
		return false;
	}

	ofs.write((char *)glm::value_ptr(_sceneBBox[0]), 12);
	ofs.write((char *)glm::value_ptr(_sceneBBox[1]), 12);
	ofs.write((char *)&_rootSide, 4);
	ofs.write((char *)&_levels, 4);
	ofs.write((char *)&_nNodes, 4);
	unsigned int count;
	// inner nodes
	count = (unsigned int)_dataInner.size();
	ofs.write((char *)&count, 4);
	ofs.write((char *)_dataInner.data(), count * sizeof(std::uint16_t));
	// leaves
	count = (unsigned int)_dataLeaves.size();
	ofs.write((char *)&count, 4);
	ofs.write((char *)_dataLeaves.data(), count * sizeof(std::uint8_t));
	// level offset table
	count = (unsigned int)_levelOffsets.size();
	ofs.write((char *)&count, 4);
	ofs.write((char *)_levelOffsets.data(), count * sizeof(std::uint32_t));

	ofs.close();
	printf("OK!\n");
	return true;
}

static std::pair<std::uint8_t, std::uint8_t> xyz_to_byte_voxel(std::uint8_t x, std::uint8_t y, std::uint8_t z) {
	// Convert xyz matrix voxel position to  byte/index of the hierarchical pos
	std::uint8_t byte_x = x / 2;
	std::uint8_t byte_y = y / 2;
	std::uint8_t byte_z = z / 2;
	std::uint8_t bit_x = x % 2;
	std::uint8_t bit_y = y % 2;
	std::uint8_t bit_z = z % 2;
#if 0
	std::uint8_t byte_id = byte_x * 4 + byte_y * 2 + byte_z;
	std::uint8_t bit_id = bit_x * 4 + bit_y * 2 + bit_z;
#else
	std::uint8_t byte_id = byte_x + byte_y * 2 + byte_z * 4;
	std::uint8_t bit_id = bit_x + bit_y * 2 + bit_z * 4;
#endif
	return std::make_pair(byte_id, bit_id);
}

bool EncodedDAG4::check_decode(const std::uint16_t* encoded_node,
	std::uint32_t child_id,
	const std::uint16_t child_offset,
	std::uint32_t verify_mask,
	std::uint32_t verify_addr,
	bool verify_mx,
	bool verify_my,
	bool verify_mz) const {
	// CHECK MASK
	int mask = getChildMask(encoded_node, child_id);
	if (mask != verify_mask) {
		std::cerr << "EncodedDAG4::check_decode(): WRONG CHILD MASK " << mask << " vs " << verify_mask << std::endl;
		return false;
	}
	if (mask == 0) {
		std::cerr << "EncodedDAG4::check_decode(): NULL CHILD MASK" << std::endl;
		return false;
	}

	// CHECK SIMMETRY
	std::uint16_t ptr_1 = encoded_node[child_offset];
	std::uint32_t result = 0;
	bool mx = ((ptr_1 >> 13) & 1) == 1;
	bool my = ((ptr_1 >> 14) & 1) == 1;
	bool mz = ((ptr_1 >> 15) & 1) == 1;

	if (mx != verify_mx) { std::cerr << "EncodedDAG4::check_decode(): WRONG MX" << std::endl; return false; }
	if (my != verify_my) { std::cerr << "EncodedDAG4::check_decode(): WRONG MY" << std::endl; return false; }
	if (mz != verify_mz) { std::cerr << "EncodedDAG4::check_decode(): WRONG MZ" << std::endl; return false; }

	// CHECK PTR
	ptr_1 &= ~(7U << 13);

	if (mask == 1) {
		if (ptr_1 != verify_addr) {
			std::cerr << "EncodedDAG4::check_decode(): WRONG 1US PTR LO " << ptr_1 << " !=  " << verify_addr << std::endl;
			return false;
		}
		else {
			result = ptr_1;
		}
	}
	else if (mask > 1) {
		std::uint16_t ptr_2 = encoded_node[child_offset + 1];
		result = (std::uint32_t(ptr_1) << 16) | ptr_2;

		std::uint32_t x_bit = mask & 1;
		result |= (x_bit << 29);
		if (result != verify_addr) {
			std::cerr << "EncodedDAG4::check_decode(): WRONG 2US PTR " << result << " !=  " << verify_addr << " x bit " << x_bit << std::endl;
			return false;
		}
	}
	//  std::cerr << "test passed, c-addr " << result << std::endl;
	return true;
}

void EncodedDAG4::encode(const GeomOctree & octree) {
	// ENCCODING
	// ---------
	//	leaves:	raw 4^3 voxels
	//          NO MORE! -> adressing ChildIdx[maxlev-1] * 8 + ChildIdx[maxlev]
	//
	//	nodes:	variable size
	//			header (2 bytes):	[0..1] childrenMask:
	//											00 : no child
	//											01 : 16b pointer
	//											1X : 32b pointer
	//											1X : 32b pointer
	//											(X is the 30th bit of 32bits pointers)
	//			body (2..32 bytes): children pointers
	//						if(16bits) -> [][][][.... 2^13 pointer ]
	//									  ^ ^ ^--- bits symmetry (x,y,z)
	//
	//											   __bit 30th -> bit X in header
	//						if(32bits) -> [][][][ []... 2^13 pointer ]
	//									  ^ ^ ^--- bits symmetry (x,y,z)
	// NO PADDING

	printf("* Encoding to DAG4... ");

	if (octree.getState() != GeomOctree::S_DAG) {
		printf("FAILED! Octree is not in DAG state\n");
		return;
	}


	_sceneBBox = octree.getSceneBBox();
	_rootSide = octree.getRootSide();
	_levels = octree.getLevels();
	_nVoxels = octree.getNVoxels();
	_nNodes = octree.getNNodes();

	levelsStats.resize(_levels);

	const GeomOctree::NodeData &octData = octree.getNodeData();


	std::vector< std::vector<std::uint16_t> > encInners(_levels - 2);

	// histogram vector with pairs of idx, number of pointers to the nodes
	std::vector< std::pair< GeomOctree::id_t, GeomOctree::id_t > > hist;

	// indirection vector to map between one level indices (position in the vector)
	// and their children offsets
	std::vector<GeomOctree::id_t> indirection;

	// init for stats
	//SDAG2LevelsStats.resize(_levels - 1);

	_clock.restart();

	for (int lev = _levels - 2; lev >= 0; --lev) {

		LevelStats ls;

		if (octData[lev].size() > (1U << 30)) {
			printf("EncodedDAG4:encode(): ERROR!!! Level %i size %zu.\nToo big for encoding in 30 bits!\n", lev, octData[lev].size());
			exit(1);
		}

		// clean and fit the refs vector
		hist.resize(octData[lev].size());
		hist.shrink_to_fit();

		// refs inizialization to ordered indices and zeros
		for (GeomOctree::id_t i = 0; i < octData[lev].size(); ++i) {
			hist[i].first = i; // idx
			hist[i].second = 0; // num of refs
		}

		if (lev > 0) { // Don't do it for the root node
			// count num of references in the superior level
			for (GeomOctree::Node n : octData[lev - 1])
				for (int c = 0; c < 8; ++c)
					if (n.existsChildPointer(c)) hist[n.children[c]].second++;

			// sort by number of references (c++11's lambdas r00lez)
			std::sort(hist.begin(), hist.end(),
				[](std::pair< GeomOctree::id_t, GeomOctree::id_t > a, std::pair< GeomOctree::id_t, GeomOctree::id_t> b) { return a.second > b.second; }
			);
		}

		std::vector<GeomOctree::id_t> newIndirection(octData[lev].size());

		if (lev == _levels - 2) { // LEAVES (two last levels, 4^3)

			_dataLeaves.resize(hist.size() * 8);
			std::uint8_t buf[8];
			std::uint8_t new_buf[8];
			for (GeomOctree::id_t i = 0; i < hist.size(); ++i) {
				newIndirection[hist[i].first] = i;
				const GeomOctree::Node &n = octData[lev][hist[i].first];
				if (!voxel_matrix_order_enabled_) {
					for (int c = 7; c >= 0; --c) {
						if (n.existsChildPointer(c)) {
							const GeomOctree::Node &cn = octData[lev + 1][n.children[c]];
							GeomOctree::Node cnm = cn.mirror(n.getChildMirrrorBit(0, c),
								n.getChildMirrrorBit(1, c),
								n.getChildMirrrorBit(2, c));
							_dataLeaves[i * 8 + c] = cnm.childrenBitmask;
						}
						else _dataLeaves[i * 8 + c] = 0;
					}
				}
				else {
					// Fetch the 8 leaf bytes 
					for (int c = 7; c >= 0; --c) {
						if (n.existsChildPointer(c)) {
							const GeomOctree::Node &cn = octData[lev + 1][n.children[c]];
							GeomOctree::Node cnm = cn.mirror(n.getChildMirrrorBit(0, c),
								n.getChildMirrrorBit(1, c),
								n.getChildMirrrorBit(2, c));
							buf[c] = cnm.childrenBitmask;
						}
						else {
							buf[c] = 0;
						}
					}

					memset(new_buf, 0, 8);

					// Shuffle bytes and bits to store bits in matrix row order
					std::uint8_t byte_pos = 0;
					std::uint8_t bit_pos = 0;
					std::uint8_t output_offset = 0;
					for (std::uint8_t z = 0; z < 4; ++z) {
						for (std::uint8_t y = 0; y < 4; ++y) {
							for (std::uint8_t x = 0; x < 4; ++x) {
								std::tie(byte_pos, bit_pos) = xyz_to_byte_voxel(x, y, z);

								std::uint8_t voxel = (buf[byte_pos] >> bit_pos) & 1;

								std::uint8_t output_byte_index = output_offset / 8;
								std::uint8_t output_bit_index = output_offset % 8;

								new_buf[output_byte_index] |= (voxel << output_bit_index);

								++output_offset;
							}
						}
					}

					// bool all_zero_new = true;
					// bool all_zero_old = true;
					// for(std::uint8_t k = 0; k < 8; ++k) {
					//   all_zero_old &= (buf[k] == 0);
					//   all_zero_new &= (new_buf[k] == 0);
					// }
					// if (all_zero_new || all_zero_old) {
					//   std::cerr << "WARNING ALL ZERO" << all_zero_old << " " << all_zero_new << std::endl;
					//   exit(1);
					// }

#if 0
					std::cerr << "Hierarchy" << std::endl;
					for (std::uint8_t i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
						for (int b = 0; b < 8; ++b) {
							std::cerr << ((buf[i] >> (7 - b)) & 1);
						}
						std::cerr << " ";
					}
					std::cerr << std::endl;
					std::cerr << "Linear" << std::endl;
					for (std::uint8_t i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
						for (int b = 0; b < 8; ++b) {
							std::cerr << ((new_buf[i] >> (7 - b)) & 1);
						}
						std::cerr << " ";
					}
					std::cerr << std::endl;
#endif
					// Write to output
					for (std::uint8_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
						_dataLeaves[i * 8 + c] = new_buf[c];
					}
				}

				ls.nNodes++;
				ls.avgSizeNode += 8;
			}
		}
		else { // INNER NODES

			for (GeomOctree::id_t i = 0; i < hist.size(); ++i) {
				const GeomOctree::Node &n = octData[lev][hist[i].first];

				std::uint16_t tmpEncNode[100];

				// tmpEncNode[0..1] <- inizializate to zero
				tmpEncNode[0] = 0;

				int tmpNodeSize = 1;

				for (int c = 7; c >= 0; --c) {
					if (n.existsChild(c)) {
						std::uint32_t child_offset = tmpNodeSize;
						std::uint32_t verify_mask = 0;
						GeomOctree::id_t childAddress = indirection[n.children[c]];
						if (childAddress < (1U << 13)) { // < 2^13
							std::uint16_t encChildPtr = std::uint16_t(childAddress);
							tmpEncNode[0] |= (1U << (2 * c)); // mask 01
							if (n.getChildMirrrorBit(0, c)) encChildPtr |= 1U << 13U; //mX
							if (n.getChildMirrrorBit(1, c)) encChildPtr |= 1U << 14U; //mY
							if (n.getChildMirrrorBit(2, c)) encChildPtr |= 1U << 15U; //mZ
							tmpEncNode[tmpNodeSize] = encChildPtr;
							tmpNodeSize += 1;
							ls.nPtr16b++;
							verify_mask = 1;
						}
						else if (childAddress < (1U << 30)) { // < 2^30
							std::uint32_t encChildPtr = std::uint32_t(childAddress);
							if (encChildPtr & (1U << 29U)) {
								tmpEncNode[0] |= (3U << (2 * c)); // mask 11
								encChildPtr &= (~(1U << 29));       // Set to 0 29 bit
								ls.nPtr29b++;
								verify_mask = 3;
							}
							else {
								tmpEncNode[0] |= (2U << (2 * c)); // mask 10
								ls.nPtr28b++;
								verify_mask = 2;
							}

							if (n.getChildMirrrorBit(0, c)) encChildPtr |= 1U << 29U;
							if (n.getChildMirrrorBit(1, c)) encChildPtr |= 1U << 30U;
							if (n.getChildMirrrorBit(2, c)) encChildPtr |= 1U << 31U;
							//							*(std::uint32_t *)(&tmpEncNode[tmpNodeSize]) = encChildPtr;
							// Encode in the first ushort the most significant bits 
							tmpEncNode[tmpNodeSize] = encChildPtr >> 16;
							++tmpNodeSize;
							// Encode in the first ushort the least significant bits 
							tmpEncNode[tmpNodeSize] = encChildPtr & 0xFFFF;
							++tmpNodeSize;
							//							ls.nPtr32b++;
						}
						else {
							std::cerr << "ERRROR Unsupported pointer sz " << childAddress << " > 2^30 " << (1U << 30) << std::endl;

						}

						if (check_decode_enabled_ &&
							!check_decode(tmpEncNode, c, child_offset, verify_mask, childAddress,
								n.getChildMirrrorBit(0, c),
								n.getChildMirrrorBit(1, c),
								n.getChildMirrrorBit(2, c))) {
							exit(1);
						}

						ls.avgNumPtrsPerNode++;
					}
				}

				// update the new indirection table
				newIndirection[hist[i].first] = std::uint32_t(encInners[lev].size());

				encInners[lev].insert(encInners[lev].end(), tmpEncNode, tmpEncNode + tmpNodeSize);

				ls.nNodes++;
				ls.avgSizeNode += tmpNodeSize;
			}
		}

		indirection = newIndirection;

		if (compute_compact_histogram_enabled_) {
			//std::cerr << "Compact histogram level " << lev << std::endl;
			build_compact_histogram_in(ls.compact_histogram, hist);
		}
		else {
			//std::cerr << "Disabled compact histogram" << std::endl;
		}

		ls.avgNumPtrsPerNode /= ls.nNodes;
		ls.avgSizeNode /= ls.nNodes;
		levelsStats[lev] = ls;
	}

	// create a index table per level
	_levelOffsets.resize(_levels - 2);
	_levelOffsets[0] = 0;
	for (int i = 1; i < _levelOffsets.size(); ++i)
		_levelOffsets[i] = std::uint32_t(encInners[i - 1].size()) + _levelOffsets[i - 1];

	// join all the encoded inner levels into a the single encoded data array
	_dataInner.clear();
	for (int i = 0; i < encInners.size(); ++i)
		_dataInner.insert(_dataInner.end(), encInners[i].begin(), encInners[i].end());

	_encodingTime = _clock.elapsed();
	printf("OK! [%s]\n", vox::human_readable_duration(_encodingTime).c_str());
#if 0
	for (int i = 0; i < levelsStats.size(); ++i) {
		printf("\t-Level %2i:  Ptr16:%12zu\tPtr28:%12zu\tPtr29:%12zu\n", i, levelsStats[i].nPtr16b, levelsStats[i].nPtr28b, levelsStats[i].nPtr29b);
	}
#endif
}

EncodedOctree::TravNode EncodedDAG4::getRootTravNode() const
{
	TravNode tn;
	tn.idx = 0;
	tn.level = 0;
	return tn;
}

bool EncodedDAG4::hasChild(const TravNode & node, const int c) const
{
	return ((_dataInner[node.idx] >> (2U * c)) & 3U) != 0;
}

EncodedOctree::TravNode EncodedDAG4::getChild(const TravNode & node, const int c, bool & mX, bool & mY, bool & mZ) const
{
	TravNode tn;
	if (node.level >= _levels - 2) {
		printf("EncodedDAG4::getChild: WARNING! Asking for a node child, but node is in its max level\n");
		return tn;
	}

	unsigned int offset = 1;
	for (int i = 7; i > c; --i) {
		offset += std::min((_dataInner[node.idx] >> (2U * i)) & 3U, 2U);
	}
	tn.idx = _dataInner[node.idx + offset];
	std::uint32_t childMask = (_dataInner[node.idx] >> (2U * c)) & 3U;
	mX = ((tn.idx >> 13U) & 1U) == 1;
	mY = ((tn.idx >> 14U) & 1U) == 1;
	mZ = ((tn.idx >> 15U) & 1U) == 1;
	tn.idx &= ~(7U << 13U);

	if (childMask > 1) {
		std::uint16_t tmp = _dataInner[node.idx + offset + 1];
		tn.idx = (tn.idx << 16) | tmp;
		tn.idx |= (childMask & 1U) << 29U;
	}
	tn.level = node.level + 1;
	if (tn.level < _levels - 2)
		tn.idx += _levelOffsets[tn.level];
	else
		tn.idx *= 8;

	return tn;
}

bool EncodedDAG4::isLeaf(const TravNode & node) const
{
	return node.level == (_levels - 2);
}

bool EncodedDAG4::hasVoxel(const TravNode & leaf, const int i, const int j, const int k) const
{
	int c = i + 4 * j + 16 * k;
	int idxByte = leaf.idx + (c / 8);
	int idxBit = c % 8;
	return (_dataLeaves[idxByte] & (1U << idxBit)) != 0;
}

int EncodedDAG4::traverse(glm::vec3 p) const
{
	if (!_sceneBBox.contains(p)) return -1;

	size_t nodeIdx = 0;
	unsigned int nodeLevel = 0;
	glm::vec3 nodeCenter = _sceneBBox.center();
	bool mX = false, mY = false, mZ = false;

	while (nodeLevel < _levels) {
		const std::uint16_t * nodePtr = &_dataInner[nodeIdx];
		if (mX) p[0] = 2.f * nodeCenter[0] - p[0];
		if (mY) p[1] = 2.f * nodeCenter[1] - p[1];
		if (mZ) p[2] = 2.f * nodeCenter[2] - p[2];
		int selectedChild = 4 * int(p[0] > nodeCenter[0]) + 2 * int(p[1] > nodeCenter[1]) + int(p[2] > nodeCenter[2]);
		if (!existsChild(nodePtr, selectedChild)) break;
		nodeLevel++;
		float hs = getHalfSide(nodeLevel);
		nodeCenter[0] += (p[0] > nodeCenter[0]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
		nodeCenter[1] += (p[1] > nodeCenter[1]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
		nodeCenter[2] += (p[2] > nodeCenter[2]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
		std::uint32_t childIdx = getChild(nodePtr, selectedChild, mX, mY, mZ);
		if (nodeLevel < _levels - 2) { // INNER
			nodeIdx = _levelOffsets[nodeLevel] + childIdx;
		}
		else if (nodeLevel == _levels - 2) { // 4^3 LEAVES
			size_t leafIdx = childIdx * 8;
			const std::uint8_t * leafPtr = &_dataLeaves[leafIdx];
			if (mX) p[0] = 2.f * nodeCenter[0] - p[0];
			if (mY) p[1] = 2.f * nodeCenter[1] - p[1];
			if (mZ) p[2] = 2.f * nodeCenter[2] - p[2];
			selectedChild = 4 * int(p[0] > nodeCenter[0]) + 2 * int(p[1] > nodeCenter[1]) + int(p[2] > nodeCenter[2]);
			if (*(leafPtr + selectedChild) == 0) break;

			nodeLevel++;
			hs = getHalfSide(nodeLevel);
			nodeCenter[0] += (p[0] > nodeCenter[0]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
			nodeCenter[1] += (p[1] > nodeCenter[1]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
			nodeCenter[2] += (p[2] > nodeCenter[2]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
			std::uint8_t selectedVoxel = 4 * int(p[0] > nodeCenter[0]) + 2 * int(p[1] > nodeCenter[1]) + int(p[2] > nodeCenter[2]);
			if (_dataLeaves[leafIdx + selectedChild] & (1U << selectedVoxel)) nodeLevel++;

			break;
		}
	}

	return nodeLevel;
}


bool EncodedDAG4::existsChild(const std::uint16_t * nodePtr, int c) const {
	return getChildMask(nodePtr, c) != 0;
}

bool EncodedDAG4::getChildMirrorBit(int axis, const std::uint16_t * nodePtr, int c) const {
	bool m[3];
	getChild(nodePtr, c, m[0], m[1], m[2]);
	return m[axis];
}

int EncodedDAG4::getChildPtrSize(const std::uint16_t * nodePtr, int childId) const {
	return sizesMask[getChildMask(nodePtr, childId)];
}

int EncodedDAG4::getChildMask(const std::uint16_t * nodePtr, int childId) const {
	return (nodePtr[0] >> (2 * childId)) & 3U;
}

int EncodedDAG4::getNodeSize(const std::uint16_t * nodePtr, int &nNodes) const {
	/*
		unsigned int nodeSize = 5; // header
		nNodes = 0;
		for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
			int cPtrSize = getChildPtrSize(nodePtr, i);
			if (cPtrSize > 0) nNodes++;
			nodeSize += cPtrSize;
		}
		return nodeSize;
		*/
	return 0;
}

std::uint32_t EncodedDAG4::getChild(const std::uint16_t * nodePtr, int childId, bool &mX, bool &mY, bool &mZ) const {
	int offset = 1; // init to header size
	for (int i = 7; i > childId; --i) {
		offset += getChildPtrSize(nodePtr, i);
	}
	int mask = getChildMask(nodePtr, childId);
	const std::uint16_t * childPtr = (nodePtr + offset);

	std::uint32_t childIdx = 0;
	if (mask == 1) {
		childIdx = std::uint32_t(*childPtr);
		mX = (childIdx & (1U << 13U)) != 0;
		mY = (childIdx & (1U << 14U)) != 0;
		mZ = (childIdx & (1U << 15U)) != 0;
		childIdx &= ~(7U << 13U);
	}
	else if (mask == 2 || mask == 3) {
		childIdx = std::uint32_t(*((std::uint32_t *)childPtr));
		mX = (childIdx & (1U << 29U)) != 0;
		mY = (childIdx & (1U << 30U)) != 0;
		mZ = (childIdx & (1U << 31U)) != 0;
		childIdx &= ~(7U << 29U);
		childIdx |= ((mask & 1U) << 29U);
	}
	else {
		// shouldnt arrive here
		printf("EncodedDAG4::getChild(): WARNING. Asking for a inexistent child\n");
	}

	return childIdx;
}

void EncodedDAG4::set_compute_compact_histogram(bool x) {
	compute_compact_histogram_enabled_ = x;
}

void EncodedDAG4::set_voxel_matrix_order(bool x) {
	voxel_matrix_order_enabled_ = x;
}

void EncodedDAG4::build_compact_histogram_in(std::vector<std::uint32_t>& compact_histogram,
	const std::vector< std::pair< GeomOctree::id_t, GeomOctree::id_t > >& hist) const {
	// Make an histogram, with % values on x and y.
	// Use 10 bins
	std::size_t N = hist.size();
	std::size_t bin_count = 10;
	std::size_t compact_histogram_samples_per_bin = std::max(N / bin_count, std::size_t(1));
	compact_histogram.resize(bin_count);
	std::size_t total_count = 0;
	std::cerr << "build_compact_histogram from " << N << " samples, to " << compact_histogram_samples_per_bin << " samples per bin" << std::endl;
	for (std::size_t ci = 0; ci < bin_count; ++ci) {
		compact_histogram[ci] = 0;
		std::size_t offset = ci*compact_histogram_samples_per_bin;
		for (std::size_t s = 0; s < compact_histogram_samples_per_bin && offset < N; ++s) {
			compact_histogram[ci] += hist[offset].second;
			++offset;
		}
		total_count += compact_histogram[ci];
	}
	if (total_count) {
		for (std::size_t ci = 0; ci < bin_count; ++ci) {
			compact_histogram[ci] = (unsigned int)(std::size_t(compact_histogram[ci]) * 100 / total_count);
		}
	}
}

#pragma once

#include <voxelator/encoded_octree.hpp>
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

class EncodedDAG4 : public EncodedOctree {
public:
	EncodedDAG4();

public:
	virtual bool load(const std::string filename);
	virtual bool save(const std::string filename, const bool onlyHeader = false) const;
	virtual void encode(const GeomOctree & octree);
	virtual size_t getDataSize() const {
		return _dataInner.size() * sizeof(std::uint16_t) + _dataLeaves.size() * sizeof(std::uint8_t) + _levelOffsets.size() * sizeof(std::uint32_t);
	}
	virtual std::string getDescription() const { return "DAG4. A DAG with SDAG4 encoding."; }

	virtual TravNode getRootTravNode() const;
	virtual bool hasChild(const TravNode &node, const int c) const;
	virtual TravNode getChild(const TravNode &node, const int c, bool &mX, bool &mY, bool &mZ) const;
	virtual bool isLeaf(const TravNode &node) const;
	virtual int getLeafSize() const { return 4; }
	virtual bool hasVoxel(const TravNode &leaf, const int i, const int j, const int k) const;


	virtual int traverse(glm::vec3 p) const;

	inline void * getInnerNodesDataPtr() const { return (void *)&_dataInner[0]; }
	inline unsigned int getInnerNodesSize() const { return (unsigned int)_dataInner.size() * sizeof(std::uint16_t); }
	inline unsigned int getNInnerNodes() const { return (unsigned int)_dataInner.size(); }
	inline void * getLeafNodesDataPtr() const { return (void *)&_dataLeaves[0]; }
	inline unsigned int getLeafNodesSize() const { return (unsigned int)_dataLeaves.size() * sizeof(std::uint8_t); }
	inline void * getLevelOffsetsPtr() const { return (void *)&_levelOffsets[0]; }
	inline unsigned int getNLevelOffsets() const { return (unsigned int)_levelOffsets.size(); }
	inline void expandInnerBuffer(unsigned int upTo) { _dataInner.resize(upTo); }
	inline void compressInnerBuffer(unsigned int upTo) { _dataInner.resize(upTo); }
protected:
	std::vector<std::uint8_t> _dataLeaves;
	std::vector<std::uint16_t> _dataInner;
	std::vector<std::uint32_t> _levelOffsets;
	bool compute_compact_histogram_enabled_;
	bool voxel_matrix_order_enabled_;
	bool check_decode_enabled_;

public: // stats
	struct LevelStats {
		LevelStats() : nNodes(0), avgNumPtrsPerNode(0), avgSizeNode(0), nPtr16b(0), nPtr28b(0), nPtr29b(0) {}
		size_t nNodes;
		float avgNumPtrsPerNode;
		float avgSizeNode;
		size_t nPtr16b, nPtr28b, nPtr29b;
		std::vector<std::uint32_t> compact_histogram;
	};
	std::vector<LevelStats> levelsStats;

	void set_compute_compact_histogram(bool x);
	void set_voxel_matrix_order(bool x);

protected:
	static const int sizesMask[];
	bool existsChild(const std::uint16_t * nodePtr, int c) const;
	bool getChildMirrorBit(int axis, const std::uint16_t * nodePtr, int c) const;
	int getChildPtrSize(const std::uint16_t * nodePtr, int childId) const;
	int getNodeSize(const std::uint16_t * nodePtr, int &nNodes) const;
	std::uint32_t getChild(const std::uint16_t * nodePtr, int childId, bool &mX, bool &mY, bool &mZ) const;
	int getChildMask(const std::uint16_t * nodePtr, int childId) const;


	void build_compact_histogram_in(std::vector<std::uint32_t>& compact_histogram,
		const std::vector< std::pair< GeomOctree::id_t, GeomOctree::id_t > >& hist) const;

	bool check_decode(const std::uint16_t* encoded_node,
		std::uint32_t child_id,
		const std::uint16_t child_offset,
		std::uint32_t verify_mask,
		std::uint32_t verify_addr,
		bool verify_mx,
		bool verify_my,
		bool verify_mz) const;

};

### src/voxelator/encoded_mto.cpp
```
#include <iterator>
#include <glm/gtc/type_ptr.hpp>
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <common/format.hpp>
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>
#include <iostream>
#include <fstream>
#include <bitset>
#include <stack>

#include <voxelator/encoded_mto.hpp>
#include <common/util.hpp>

EncodedMTO::EncodedMTO() : EncodedOctree() {
	_data.clear();
}

bool EncodedMTO::load(const std::string filename)
{
	printf("* Loading DAG '%s'... ", filename.c_str()); fflush(stdout);

	std::ifstream ifs;
	ifs.open(filename, std::ios::in | std::ios::binary);
	if (!ifs.is_open()) {
		printf("FAILED!!!\n");
		return false;
	}

	_data.clear();

	ifs.read((char *)glm::value_ptr(_sceneBBox[0]), 12);
	ifs.read((char *)glm::value_ptr(_sceneBBox[1]), 12);
	ifs.read((char *)&_rootSide, 4);
	ifs.read((char *)&_levels, 4);
	ifs.read((char *)&_nNodes, 4);
	ifs.read((char *)&_firstLeafPtr, 4);
	unsigned int count = (unsigned int)_data.size();
	ifs.read((char *)&count, 4);
	_data.resize(count);
	ifs.read((char *)_data.data(), count * sizeof(std::uint32_t));

	printf("OK!\n");

	return true;
}

bool EncodedMTO::save(const std::string filename, const bool onlyHeader) const
{

	printf("* Saving DAG '%s'... ", filename.c_str()); fflush(stdout);

	std::ofstream ofs;
	ofs.open(filename, std::ios::out | std::ios::binary);
	if (!ofs.is_open()) {
		printf("FAILED!!!\n");
		return false;
	}

	ofs.write((char *)glm::value_ptr(_sceneBBox[0]), 12);
	ofs.write((char *)glm::value_ptr(_sceneBBox[1]), 12);
	ofs.write((char *)&_rootSide, 4);
	ofs.write((char *)&_levels, 4);
	ofs.write((char *)&_nNodes, 4);
	ofs.write((char *)&_firstLeafPtr, 4);
	unsigned int count = (unsigned int)_data.size();
	ofs.write((char *)&count, 4);
	if (!onlyHeader) ofs.write((char *)_data.data(), count * sizeof(std::uint32_t));

	ofs.close();

	printf("OK!\n");

	return true;
}

void EncodedMTO::encode(const GeomOctree & octree) {

	printf("* Encoding to DAG... ");

	if (octree.getState() != GeomOctree::S_DAG) {
		printf("FAILED! Octree is not in DAG state\n");
		return;
	}

	_data.reserve(octree.getNNodes());
	_sceneBBox = octree.getSceneBBox();
	_rootSide = octree.getRootSide();
	_levels = octree.getLevels();
	_nVoxels = octree.getNVoxels();
	_nNodes = octree.getNNodes();

	const GeomOctree::NodeData &octData = octree.getNodeData();

	std::vector<std::uint32_t> truePtrs;
	std::uint32_t counter = 0;

	_clock.restart();

	for (unsigned int lev = 0; lev < _levels; ++lev) {
		for (unsigned int i = 0; i < octData[lev].size(); ++i) {
			truePtrs.push_back(counter);
			//printf("%i\t%i\n", truePtrs.size() - 1, counter);
			counter += (lev < _levels - 1) ? octData[lev][i].getNChildren() + 1 : 1;
		}
	}

	_firstLeafPtr = counter;

	unsigned int levSizeAcum = 0;

	for (unsigned int lev = 0; lev < _levels; ++lev) {
		unsigned int levSize = (unsigned int)octData[lev].size();
		levSizeAcum += levSize;
		for (unsigned int i = 0; i < levSize; ++i) {
			const GeomOctree::Node &n = octData[lev][i];
			_data.push_back(std::uint32_t(n.childrenBitmask));
			//printf("[%2d](%3d)[%i]", lev, dagEncoded.size() - 1, std::bitset<8>(bn.childrenBitmask).count());
			if (_data.size() < _firstLeafPtr) {
				counter = 0;
				for (int k = 7; k >= 0; --k) {
					if (n.children[k] != GeomOctree::nullNode) {
						_data.push_back(truePtrs[n.children[k] + levSizeAcum]);
						//printf("\t%i", dagEncoded[dagEncoded.size() - 1]);
						counter++;
					}
					//else printf("\t");
				}
#if 0
				if (check) {
					if (counter != n.getNChildren() && (lev < _levels - 1)) {
						printf("\nWRONG! bitcount != not null sons!!!\n");
						printf("Lev %u  NoID: %u  bitcount: %d  Bitmaks: \n", lev, i, n.getNChildren());
						for (int k = 0; k < 8; k++) {
							printf("  * [%i] ptr: %u\n", int((n.childrenBitmask&(1 << k))), n.children[k]);

						}
						return false;
					}
				}
#endif
			}
			else {
				//printf("\t");
				//for (int k = 7; k >= 0; --k)
				//bn.childrenBitmask & (1<<k) ? printf("1") : printf("0");
			}
			//	printf("\n");
		}
	}

	_encodingTime = _clock.elapsed();

	printf("OK! [%s]\n", vox::human_readable_duration(_encodingTime).c_str());
}

EncodedMTO::TravNode EncodedMTO::getRootTravNode() const
{
	TravNode tn;
	tn.idx = 0;
	tn.level = 0;
	return tn;
}

bool EncodedMTO::hasChild(const TravNode & node, const int c) const
{
	return (_data[node.idx] & (1U << c)) != 0;
}

EncodedMTO::TravNode EncodedMTO::getChild(const TravNode & node, const int c, bool & mX, bool & mY, bool & mZ) const
{
	TravNode tn;
	if (node.level >= _levels-1) {
		printf("EncodedMTO::getChild: WARNING! Asking for a node child, but node is in its max level\n");
		return tn;
	}
	std::uint8_t mask = std::uint8_t(_data[node.idx] & 0xFF);
	tn.idx = _data[node.idx + bitCount(mask >> c)];
	tn.level = node.level + 1;
	mX = mY = mZ = false;
	return tn;
}

bool EncodedMTO::isLeaf(const TravNode & node) const
{
	return node.level == (_levels - 1);
}

bool EncodedMTO::hasVoxel(const TravNode & leaf, const int i, const int j, const int k) const
{
	int c = 4 * i + 2 * j + k;
	return hasChild(leaf, c);
}

### src/voxelator/encoded_octree.hpp
```
#pragma once

#include <voxelator/geom_octree.hpp>
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <common/timing.hpp>
#include <common/aabb.hpp>
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>

class EncodedOctree {


public:
	EncodedOctree() : _rootSide(0), _levels(0), _nVoxels(0), _nNodes(0), _nLeaves(0) { _sceneBBox.to_empty(); }

	// Encoding and storaging interface---------------------------
	virtual void encode(const GeomOctree & octree) = 0;
	virtual std::string getDescription() const = 0;
	// optional
	virtual bool load(const std::string filename) {
		printf("WARNING:EncodedOctree:load() Not implemented\n");
		return false;
	};
	virtual bool save(const std::string filename, const bool onlyHeader = false) const {
		printf("WARNING:EncodedOctree:save() Not implemented\n");
		return false;
	}
	virtual int traverse(glm::vec3 p) const {
		printf("WARNING:EncodedOctree:traverse() Not implemented\n");
		return 0;
	}

	// Traversal interface ---------------------------------------

public:
	struct TravNode {
		TravNode() : idx(0), level(0), mX(0), mY(0), mZ(0) { center = glm::vec3(0,0,0); };
		std::uint32_t idx;
		unsigned int level;
		glm::vec3 center;
		bool mX, mY, mZ;
	};
	inline float getHalfSide(const unsigned int level = 0) const {
		return _rootSide / float(1U << (level + 1));
	}
	virtual TravNode getRootTravNode() const = 0;
	virtual bool hasChild(const TravNode &node, const int c) const = 0;
	virtual TravNode getChild(const TravNode &node, const int c, bool &mX, bool &mY, bool &mZ) const = 0;
	virtual bool isLeaf(const TravNode &node) const = 0;
	virtual int getLeafSize() const = 0;
	virtual bool hasVoxel(const TravNode &leaf, const int i, const int j, const int k) const = 0;
	
	// Getters ---------------------

public:
	inline vox::AABB3f getSceneBBox() const { return _sceneBBox; }
	inline float getRootSide() const { return _rootSide; }
	inline glm::vec3 getCenter() const { return _sceneBBox.center(); }
	inline unsigned int getNLevels() const { return _levels; }
	
	inline size_t getNVoxels() const { return _nVoxels; }
	inline size_t getNLeaves() const { return _nLeaves; }
	inline size_t getNNodes() const { return _nNodes; }


protected:
	vox::AABB3f _sceneBBox;
	float _rootSide;
	unsigned int _levels;
	size_t _nVoxels, _nNodes, _nLeaves;
	vox::WallClock _clock;
	vox::time_duration _encodingTime;
};
```

### src/voxelator/encoded_sdag1.cpp

#include <iostream>

bool EncodedSDAG1::load(const std::string filename)
{
	printf("* Loading SDAG1 '%s'... ", filename.c_str()); fflush(stdout);
	std::ifstream ifs;
	ifs.open(filename, std::ios::in | std::ios::binary);
	if (!ifs.is_open()) {
		printf("FAILED!!!\n");
		return false;
	}

	_data.clear();

	ifs.read((char *)glm::value_ptr(_sceneBBox[0]), 12);
	ifs.read((char *)glm::value_ptr(_sceneBBox[1]), 12);
	ifs.read((char *)&_rootSide, 4);
	ifs.read((char *)&_levels, 4);
	ifs.read((char *)&_nNodes, 4);
	ifs.read((char *)&_firstLeafPtr, 4);
	unsigned int count = (unsigned int)_data.size();
	ifs.read((char *)&count, 4);
	_data.resize(count);
	ifs.read((char *)_data.data(), count * sizeof(std::uint32_t));

	printf("OK!\n");
	return true;
}

bool EncodedSDAG1::save(const std::string filename, const bool onlyHeader) const
{
	printf("* Saving SDAG1 '%s'... ", filename.c_str()); fflush(stdout);

	std::ofstream ofs;
	ofs.open(filename, std::ios::out | std::ios::binary);
	if (!ofs.is_open()) {
		printf("FAILED!\n");
		return false;
	}

	ofs.write((char *)glm::value_ptr(_sceneBBox[0]), 12);
	ofs.write((char *)glm::value_ptr(_sceneBBox[1]), 12);
	ofs.write((char *)&_rootSide, 4);
	ofs.write((char *)&_levels, 4);
	ofs.write((char *)&_nNodes, 4);
	ofs.write((char *)&_firstLeafPtr, 4);
	unsigned int count = (unsigned int)_data.size();
	ofs.write((char *)&count, 4);
	if (!onlyHeader) ofs.write((char *)_data.data(), count * sizeof(std::uint32_t));

	ofs.close();
	printf("OK!\n");
	return true;
}

void EncodedSDAG1::encode(const GeomOctree & octree)
{
	printf("* Encoding to SDAG1... ");

	if (octree.getState() != GeomOctree::S_SDAG) {
		printf("FAILED! Octree is not in SDAG state\n");
		return;
	}

	_data.reserve(octree.getNNodes());
	_sceneBBox = octree.getSceneBBox();
	_rootSide = octree.getRootSide();
	_levels = octree.getLevels();
	_nVoxels = octree.getNVoxels();
	_nNodes = octree.getNNodes();

	const GeomOctree::NodeData &octData = octree.getNodeData();

	std::vector<std::uint32_t> truePtrs;
	std::uint32_t counter = 0;

	_clock.restart();

	for (unsigned int lev = 0; lev < _levels; ++lev) {
		for (unsigned int i = 0; i < octData[lev].size(); ++i) {
			truePtrs.push_back(counter);
			//printf("%i\t%i\n", truePtrs.size() - 1, counter);
			counter += (lev < _levels - 1) ? octData[lev][i].getNChildren() + 1 : 1;
		}
	}

	_firstLeafPtr = counter;

	unsigned int levSizeAcum = 0;

	for (unsigned int lev = 0; lev < _levels; ++lev) {
		unsigned int levSize = (unsigned int)octData[lev].size();
		levSizeAcum += levSize;
		for (unsigned int i = 0; i < levSize; ++i) {
			const GeomOctree::Node &n = octData[lev][i];
			std::uint32_t nodeHeader =
				n.childrenMirroredBitmask[2] << 24U |
				n.childrenMirroredBitmask[1] << 16U |
				n.childrenMirroredBitmask[0] << 8U  |
				n.childrenBitmask;
				_data.push_back(nodeHeader);
			//printf("[%2d](%3d)[%i]", lev, dagEncoded.size() - 1, std::bitset<8>(bn.childrenBitmask).count());
			if (_data.size() < _firstLeafPtr) {
				counter = 0;
				for (int k = 7; k >= 0; --k) {
					if (n.children[k] != GeomOctree::nullNode) {
						_data.push_back(truePtrs[n.children[k] + levSizeAcum]);
						//printf("\t%i", dagEncoded[dagEncoded.size() - 1]);
						counter++;
					}
					//else printf("\t");
				}
#if 0
				if (check) {
					if (counter != n.getNChildren() && (lev < _levels - 1)) {
						printf("\nWRONG! bitcount != not null sons!!!\n");
						printf("Lev %u  NoID: %u  bitcount: %d  Bitmaks: \n", lev, i, n.getNChildren());
						for (int k = 0; k < 8; k++) {
							printf("  * [%i] ptr: %u\n", int((n.childrenBitmask&(1 << k))), n.children[k]);

						}
						return false;
					}
				}
#endif
			}
			else {
				//printf("\t");
				//for (int k = 7; k >= 0; --k)
				//bn.childrenBitmask & (1<<k) ? printf("1") : printf("0");
			}
			//	printf("\n");
		}
	}


	_encodingTime = _clock.elapsed();

	printf("OK! [%s]\n", vox::human_readable_duration(_encodingTime).c_str());
}

EncodedOctree::TravNode EncodedSDAG1::getRootTravNode() const
{
	TravNode tn;
	tn.idx = 0;
	tn.level = 0;
	return tn;
}

bool EncodedSDAG1::hasChild(const TravNode & node, const int c) const
{
	return (_data[node.idx] & (1U << c)) != 0;
}

EncodedOctree::TravNode EncodedSDAG1::getChild(const TravNode & node, const int c, bool & mX, bool & mY, bool & mZ) const
{
	TravNode tn;
	if (node.level >= _levels-1) {
		printf("EncodedSDAG1::getChild: WARNING! Asking for a node child, but node is in its max level\n");
		return tn;
	}
	std::uint8_t mask = std::uint8_t(_data[node.idx] & 0xFF);
	tn.idx = _data[node.idx + bitCount(mask >> c)];
	tn.level = node.level + 1;
	mX = (_data[node.idx] & (1U << (c + 8))) != 0;
	mY = (_data[node.idx] & (1U << (c + 16))) != 0;
	mZ = (_data[node.idx] & (1U << (c + 24))) != 0;
	return tn;
}

bool EncodedSDAG1::isLeaf(const TravNode & node) const
{
	return node.level == (_levels - 1);
}

bool EncodedSDAG1::hasVoxel(const TravNode & leaf, const int i, const int j, const int k) const
{
	int c = 4 * i + 2 * j + k;
	return hasChild(leaf, c);
}

};

#include <iostream>
#include <glm/gtc/type_ptr.hpp>
#include <glm/glm.hpp>

bool EncodedSDAG2::load(const std::string filename)
{
	printf("* Loading SDAG2 '%s'... ", filename.c_str()); fflush(stdout);
	std::ifstream ifs;
	ifs.open(filename, std::ios::in | std::ios::binary);
	if (!ifs.is_open()) {
		printf("FAILED!!!\n");
		return false;
	}

	_data.clear();

	ifs.read((char *)glm::value_ptr(_sceneBBox[0]), 12);
	ifs.read((char *)glm::value_ptr(_sceneBBox[1]), 12);
	ifs.read((char *)&_rootSide, 4);
	ifs.read((char *)&_levels, 4);
	ifs.read((char *)&_nNodes, 4);
	ifs.read((char *)&_firstLeafPtr, 4);
	unsigned int count = (unsigned int)_data.size();
	ifs.read((char *)&count, 4);

bool EncodedSDAG2::save(const std::string filename, const bool onlyHeader) const
{
	printf("* Saving SDAG2 '%s'... ", filename.c_str()); fflush(stdout);

	std::ofstream ofs;
	ofs.open(filename, std::ios::out | std::ios::binary);
	if (!ofs.is_open()) {
		printf("FAILED!\n");
		return false;
	}

	ofs.write((char *)glm::value_ptr(_sceneBBox[0]), 12);
	ofs.write((char *)glm::value_ptr(_sceneBBox[1]), 12);
	ofs.write((char *)&_rootSide, 4);
	ofs.write((char *)&_levels, 4);
	ofs.write((char *)&_nNodes, 4);
	ofs.write((char *)&_firstLeafPtr, 4);
	unsigned int count = (unsigned int)_data.size();
	ofs.write((char *)&count, 4);
	if (!onlyHeader) ofs.write((char *)_data.data(), count * sizeof(std::uint32_t));

	ofs.close();
	printf("OK!\n");
	return true;

void EncodedSDAG2::encode(const GeomOctree & octree)
{
	printf("* Encoding to SDAG2... ");

	if (octree.getState() != GeomOctree::S_SDAG) {
		printf("FAILED! Octree is not in SDAG state\n");
		return;
	}

	_data.reserve(octree.getNNodes());
	_sceneBBox = octree.getSceneBBox();
	_rootSide = octree.getRootSide();
	_levels = octree.getLevels();
	_nVoxels = octree.getNVoxels();
	_nNodes = octree.getNNodes();

	const GeomOctree::NodeData &octData = octree.getNodeData();

	std::vector<std::uint32_t> truePtrs;
	std::uint32_t counter = 0;

	_clock.restart();

	for (unsigned int lev = 0; lev < _levels; ++lev) {
		if (lev == _levels - 1) _firstLeafPtr = counter;
		for (unsigned int i = 0; i < octData[lev].size(); ++i) {
			truePtrs.push_back(counter);
			const GeomOctree::Node &n = octData[lev][i];
			counter += (lev < _levels - 1) ? n.getNChildren() + 1 : 1;
		}
	}

	_data.reserve(_nNodes * 2);

	unsigned int levSizeAcum = 0;

	unsigned int memInner = 0;
	unsigned int memLeaves = 0;

	for (unsigned int lev = 0; lev < _levels - 1; ++lev) {
		unsigned int levSize = (unsigned int)octData[lev].size();

		levSizeAcum += levSize;

		for (unsigned int i = 0; i < levSize; ++i) {

			const GeomOctree::Node &n = octData[lev][i];

			if (lev == _levels - 2) {
				for (int k = 0; k < 2; k++) {
					std::uint32_t tmp = 0;
					for (int j = 0; j < 4; j++) {
						if (n.existsChildPointer(j + 4 * k)) {
							const GeomOctree::Node & nt = octData[_levels - 1][n.children[j + 4 * k]];
							n.mirror(n.getChildMirrrorBit(0, j + 4 * k), n.getChildMirrrorBit(1, j + 4 * k), n.getChildMirrrorBit(2, j + 4 * k));
							tmp |= nt.childrenBitmask << (8 * j);
						}
					}
					_data.push_back(tmp);
					memLeaves += 4;
				}
				continue;
			}

			std::uint32_t tmp = n.childrenMirroredBitmask[0] << 24 | n.childrenMirroredBitmask[1] << 16 | n.childrenMirroredBitmask[2] << 8 | n.childrenBitmask;
			_data.push_back(tmp);
			memInner += 4;
			//printf("[%2d](%3d)[%i]", lev, dagEncoded.size() - 1, std::bitset<8>(bn.childrenBitmask).count());
			if (_data.size() < _firstLeafPtr) {
				counter = 0;
				for (int k = 7; k >= 0; --k) {
					if (n.existsChildPointer(k)) {
						_data.push_back(truePtrs[n.children[k] + levSizeAcum]);
						memInner += 4;
						//printf("\t%i", dagEncoded[dagEncoded.size() - 1]);
						//if (bn.mirroredX & (1U << k)) printf("X");
						//if (bn.mirroredY & (1U << k)) printf("Y");
						//if (bn.mirroredZ & (1U << k)) printf("Z");
						counter++;
					}
					//else printf("\t");
				}
			}
			else {
				//	printf("\t");
				//for (int k = 7; k >= 0; --k)
				//bn.childrenBitmask & (1 << k) ? printf("1") : printf("0");
			}
			//printf("\n");
		
		}
	}

	_encodingTime = _clock.elapsed();

	printf("OK! [%s]\n", vox::human_readable_duration(_encodingTime).c_str());

}
```

class EncodedSDAG2 : public EncodedOctree {

	// NOT REAL IMPLEMENTATION!
	virtual TravNode getRootTravNode() const { return TravNode(); }
	virtual bool hasChild(const TravNode &node, const int c) const { return false; }

protected:
	std::vector<std::uint32_t> _data;
	std::uint32_t _firstLeafPtr;
};

### src/voxelator/encoded_sdag3.cpp
```
#include <iostream>
#include <algorithm>
#include <glm/gtc/type_ptr.hpp>
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <common/format.hpp>
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

#include <voxelator/encoded_sdag3.hpp>
#include <common/util.hpp>

const int EncodedSDAG3::sizesMask[] = { 0,1,2,4 };

bool EncodedSDAG3::load(const std::string filename)
{
	printf("* Loading SDAG3 '%s'... ", filename.c_str()); fflush(stdout);
	std::ifstream ifs;
	ifs.open(filename, std::ios::in | std::ios::binary);
	if (!ifs.is_open()) {
		printf("FAILED!!!\n");
		return false;
	}

	_dataInner.clear();

	ifs.read((char *)glm::value_ptr(_sceneBBox[0]), 12);
	ifs.read((char *)glm::value_ptr(_sceneBBox[1]), 12);
	ifs.read((char *)&_rootSide, 4);
	ifs.read((char *)&_levels, 4);
	ifs.read((char *)&_nNodes, 4);
	unsigned int count;
	// inner nodes
	count = (unsigned int)_dataInner.size();
	ifs.read((char *)&count, 4);
	_dataInner.resize(count);
	ifs.read((char *)_dataInner.data(), count * sizeof(std::uint8_t));
	// leaves
	count = (unsigned int)_dataLeaves.size();
	ifs.read((char *)&count, 4);
	_dataLeaves.resize(count);
	ifs.read((char *)_dataLeaves.data(), count * sizeof(std::uint8_t));
	// level offset table
	count = (unsigned int)_levelsIdxTable.size();
	ifs.read((char *)&count, 4);
	_levelsIdxTable.resize(count);
	ifs.read((char *)_levelsIdxTable.data(), count * sizeof(std::uint32_t));

	printf("OK!\n");
	return true;
}

bool EncodedSDAG3::save(const std::string filename, const bool onlyHeader) const
{
	printf("* Saving SDAG3 '%s'... ", filename.c_str()); fflush(stdout);

	std::ofstream ofs;
	ofs.open(filename, std::ios::out | std::ios::binary);
	if (!ofs.is_open()) {
		printf("FAILED!\n");
		return false;
	}

	ofs.write((char *)glm::value_ptr(_sceneBBox[0]), 12);
	ofs.write((char *)glm::value_ptr(_sceneBBox[1]), 12);
	ofs.write((char *)&_rootSide, 4);
	ofs.write((char *)&_levels, 4);
	ofs.write((char *)&_nNodes, 4);
	unsigned int count;
	// inner nodes
	count = (unsigned int)_dataInner.size();
	ofs.write((char *)&count, 4);
	ofs.write((char *)_dataInner.data(), count * sizeof(std::uint8_t));
	// leaves
	count = (unsigned int)_dataLeaves.size();
	ofs.write((char *)&count, 4);
	ofs.write((char *)_dataLeaves.data(), count * sizeof(std::uint8_t));
	// level offset table
	count = (unsigned int)_levelsIdxTable.size();
	ofs.write((char *)&count, 4);
	ofs.write((char *)_levelsIdxTable.data(), count * sizeof(std::uint32_t));

	ofs.close();
	printf("OK!\n");
	return true;
}


void EncodedSDAG3::encode(const GeomOctree & octree) {

	// ENCCODING
	// ---------
	// BODY:
	//	leaves:	raw 4^3 voxels
	//          adressing ChildIdx[maxlev-1] * 8 + ChildIdx[maxlev]
	//
	//	nodes:	variable size
	//			header (5 bytes):	[0..1] childrenMask:
	//											00 : no child
	//											01 : 8b pointer
	//											10 : 16b pointer
	//											11 : 32b pointer
	//								[2] mirroring X
	//								[3] mirroring Y
	//								[4] mirroring Z
	//			body (1..32 bytes): children pointers

	printf("* Encoding to SDAG3... ");

	if (octree.getState() != GeomOctree::S_SDAG) {
		printf("FAILED! Octree is not in SDAG state\n");
		return;
	}

	_sceneBBox = octree.getSceneBBox();
	_rootSide = octree.getRootSide();
	_levels = octree.getLevels();
	_nVoxels = octree.getNVoxels();
	_nNodes = octree.getNNodes();

	levelsStats.resize(_levels);

	const GeomOctree::NodeData &octData = octree.getNodeData();

	std::vector< std::vector<std::uint8_t> > encData(_levels - 1);

	// histogram vector with pairs of idx, number of pointers to the nodes
	std::vector< std::pair< GeomOctree::id_t, GeomOctree::id_t > > hist;

	// indirection vector to map between one level indices (position in the vector)
	// and their children offsets
	std::vector<GeomOctree::id_t> indirection;

	// init for stats
	//SDAG2LevelsStats.resize(_levels - 1);

	_clock.restart();

	for (int lev = _levels - 2; lev >= 0; --lev) {

		LevelStats ls;

		// clean and fit the refs vector
		hist.resize(octData[lev].size());
		hist.shrink_to_fit();

		// refs inizialization to ordered indices and zeros
		for (GeomOctree::id_t i = 0; i < octData[lev].size(); ++i) {
			hist[i].first = i; // idx
			hist[i].second = 0; // num of refs
		}

		if (lev > 0) { // Don't do it for the root node
			// count num of references in the superior level
			for (GeomOctree::Node n : octData[lev - 1])
				for (int c = 0; c < 8; ++c)
					if (n.existsChildPointer(c)) hist[n.children[c]].second++;

			// sort by number of references (c++11's lambdas r00lez)
			std::sort(hist.begin(), hist.end(),
				[](std::pair< GeomOctree::id_t, GeomOctree::id_t > a, std::pair< GeomOctree::id_t, GeomOctree::id_t> b) { return a.second > b.second; }
			);
		}

		std::vector<GeomOctree::id_t> newIndirection(octData[lev].size());

		if (lev == _levels - 2) { // LEAVES (two last levels, 4^3)

			encData[lev].resize(hist.size() * 8);

			for (GeomOctree::id_t i = 0; i < hist.size(); ++i) {
				newIndirection[hist[i].first] = i;
				const GeomOctree::Node &n = octData[lev][hist[i].first];
				for (int c = 7; c >= 0; --c) {
					if (n.existsChildPointer(c)) {
						const GeomOctree::Node &cn = octData[lev + 1][n.children[c]];
						GeomOctree::Node cnm = cn.mirror(n.getChildMirrrorBit(0, c), n.getChildMirrrorBit(1, c), n.getChildMirrrorBit(2, c));
						encData[lev][i * 8 + c] = cnm.childrenBitmask;
					} else encData[lev][i * 8 + c] = 0;
				}
				ls.nNodes++;
				ls.avgSizeNode += 8;
			}
		}
		else { // INNER NODES

			for (GeomOctree::id_t i = 0; i < hist.size(); ++i) {
				const GeomOctree::Node &n = octData[lev][hist[i].first];

				std::uint8_t tmpEncNode[40];

				// tmpEncNode[0..1] <- inizializate to zero
				tmpEncNode[0] = 0;
				tmpEncNode[1] = 0;

				// tmpEncNode[2..4] <- mirrors
				tmpEncNode[2] = n.childrenMirroredBitmask[0];
				tmpEncNode[3] = n.childrenMirroredBitmask[1];
				tmpEncNode[4] = n.childrenMirroredBitmask[2];

				int tmpNodeSize = 5;

				for (int c = 7; c >= 0; --c) {
					if (n.existsChild(c)) {
						GeomOctree::id_t childAddress = indirection[n.children[c]];
						if (childAddress < 256) {
							tmpEncNode[tmpNodeSize] = std::uint8_t(childAddress);
							tmpNodeSize += 1;
							tmpEncNode[1] |= 1U << c;
							ls.nPtr8b++;
						}
						else if (childAddress < 65536) {
							*(std::uint16_t *)(&tmpEncNode[tmpNodeSize]) = std::uint16_t(childAddress);
							tmpNodeSize += 2;
							tmpEncNode[0] |= 1U << c;
							ls.nPtr16b++;
						}
						else {
							*(std::uint32_t *)(&tmpEncNode[tmpNodeSize]) = std::uint32_t(childAddress);
							tmpNodeSize += 4;
							tmpEncNode[0] |= 1U << c;
							tmpEncNode[1] |= 1U << c;
							ls.nPtr32b++;
						}
						ls.avgNumPtrsPerNode++;
					}
				}

				// adjust pos for aligment (4) (filling with zeros)
				if (tmpNodeSize % 4 != 0) {
					int nZeros = 4 - tmpNodeSize % 4;
					for (int k = 0; k < nZeros; ++k) tmpEncNode[tmpNodeSize + k] = 0;
					tmpNodeSize += nZeros;
				}

				// update the new indirection table
				newIndirection[hist[i].first] = std::uint32_t(encData[lev].size() / 4);

				// add "pos" first bytes from tmpEncNode to encData[lev]
				encData[lev].insert(encData[lev].end(), tmpEncNode, tmpEncNode + tmpNodeSize);

				ls.nNodes++;
				ls.avgSizeNode += tmpNodeSize;
			}
		}

		indirection = newIndirection;

		ls.avgNumPtrsPerNode /= ls.nNodes;
		ls.avgSizeNode /= ls.nNodes;
		levelsStats[lev] = ls;
	}

	// create a index table per level
	_levelsIdxTable.resize(_levels - 2);
	_levelsIdxTable[0] = 0;
	for (int i = 1; i < _levelsIdxTable.size(); ++i)
		_levelsIdxTable[i] = std::uint32_t(encData[i-1].size()) + _levelsIdxTable[i-1];

	// join all the encoded levels into a the single encoded data array
	_dataInner.clear();
	for (int i = 0; i < encData.size()-1; ++i)
		_dataInner.insert(_dataInner.end(), encData[i].begin(), encData[i].end());
	
	_dataLeaves.clear();
	_dataLeaves.insert(_dataLeaves.end(), encData[encData.size()-1].begin(), encData[encData.size()-1].end());

	_encodingTime = _clock.elapsed();
	printf("OK! [%s]\n", vox::human_readable_duration(_encodingTime).c_str());
}


int EncodedSDAG3::traverse(glm::vec3 p) const
{
	if (!_sceneBBox.contains(p)) return -1;

	size_t nodeIdx = 0;
	unsigned int nodeLevel = 0;
	glm::vec3 nodeCenter = _sceneBBox.center();
	bool mX = false, mY = false, mZ = false;

	while (nodeLevel < _levels) {
		const std::uint8_t * nodePtr = &_dataInner[nodeIdx];
		if (mX) p[0] = 2.f * nodeCenter[0] - p[0];
		if (mY) p[1] = 2.f * nodeCenter[1] - p[1];
		if (mZ) p[2] = 2.f * nodeCenter[2] - p[2];
		int selectedChild = 4 * int(p[0] > nodeCenter[0]) + 2 * int(p[1] > nodeCenter[1]) + int(p[2] > nodeCenter[2]);
		if (!existsChild(nodePtr, selectedChild)) break;
		nodeLevel++;
		float hs = getHalfSide(nodeLevel);
		nodeCenter[0] += (p[0] > nodeCenter[0]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
		nodeCenter[1] += (p[1] > nodeCenter[1]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
		nodeCenter[2] += (p[2] > nodeCenter[2]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
		mX = getChildMirrorBit(0, nodePtr, selectedChild);
		mY = getChildMirrorBit(1, nodePtr, selectedChild);
		mZ = getChildMirrorBit(2, nodePtr, selectedChild);
		if (nodeLevel < _levels-2) { // INNER
			nodeIdx = _levelsIdxTable[nodeLevel] + getChildIdx(nodePtr, selectedChild) * 4;
		}
		else if (nodeLevel == _levels-2) { // 4^3 LEAVES
			size_t leafIdx = getChildIdx(nodePtr, selectedChild) * 8;
			const std::uint8_t * leafPtr = &_dataLeaves[leafIdx];
			if (mX) p[0] = 2.f * nodeCenter[0] - p[0];
			if (mY) p[1] = 2.f * nodeCenter[1] - p[1];
			if (mZ) p[2] = 2.f * nodeCenter[2] - p[2];
			selectedChild = 4 * int(p[0] > nodeCenter[0]) + 2 * int(p[1] > nodeCenter[1]) + int(p[2] > nodeCenter[2]);
			if (*(leafPtr+selectedChild) == 0) break;
			
			nodeLevel++;
			hs = getHalfSide(nodeLevel);
			nodeCenter[0] += (p[0] > nodeCenter[0]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
			nodeCenter[1] += (p[1] > nodeCenter[1]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
			nodeCenter[2] += (p[2] > nodeCenter[2]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
			std::uint8_t selectedVoxel = 4 * int(p[0] > nodeCenter[0]) + 2 * int(p[1] > nodeCenter[1]) + int(p[2] > nodeCenter[2]);
			if (_dataLeaves[leafIdx + selectedChild] & (1U << selectedVoxel)) nodeLevel++;
			
			break;
		}
	}

	return nodeLevel;
}


void EncodedSDAG3::printNode(const std::uint8_t * nodePtr, bool leaf) const
{
	if (!leaf) {
		int nNodes, nodeSize;
		nodeSize = getNodeSize(nodePtr, nNodes);
		printf(" -- Encoded Inner Node SDAG3 --\n");
		printf("Mask:     %s  (Node Size: %i bytes)\n", byteToString(nodePtr[0]).c_str(), nodeSize);
		printf("          %s  (Num Nodes: %i      )\n", byteToString(nodePtr[1]).c_str(), nNodes);
		printf("Mirror X: %s\n", byteToString(nodePtr[2]).c_str());
		printf("Mirror Y: %s\n", byteToString(nodePtr[3]).c_str());
		printf("Mirror Z: %s\n", byteToString(nodePtr[4]).c_str());
		for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
			if (existsChild(nodePtr, i))
				printf("Child %i [%i-bits] :\t%i\n", i, getChildPtrSize(nodePtr, i) * 8, getChildIdx(nodePtr, i));
		}
	}
	else {
		printf(" -- Encoded Leaf 4^3 Node SDAG3 --\n");
		for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
			printf("Child %i bm: %s\n", i, byteToString(nodePtr[i]).c_str());
		}
	}
	printf("-----------\n");
}

bool EncodedSDAG3::existsChild(const std::uint8_t * nodePtr, int c) const {
	return (((nodePtr[0] & (1U << c)) | (nodePtr[1] & (1U << c))) != 0);
}

bool EncodedSDAG3::getChildMirrorBit(int axis, const std::uint8_t * nodePtr, int c) const {
	return (nodePtr[2 + axis] & (1U << c)) != 0;
}

int EncodedSDAG3::getChildPtrSize(const std::uint8_t * nodePtr, int childId) const {
	int maskId = ((nodePtr[0] & (1U << childId)) >> childId) << 1 | ((nodePtr[1] & (1U << childId)) >> childId);
	return sizesMask[maskId];
}

int EncodedSDAG3::getNodeSize(const std::uint8_t * nodePtr, int &nNodes) const {
	unsigned int nodeSize = 5; // header
	nNodes = 0;
	for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
		int cPtrSize = getChildPtrSize(nodePtr, i);
		if (cPtrSize > 0) nNodes++;
		nodeSize += cPtrSize;
	}
	return nodeSize;
}

std::uint32_t EncodedSDAG3::getChildIdx(const std::uint8_t * nodePtr, int childId) const {
	int offset = 5; // init to header size
	for (int i = 7; i > childId; --i) {
		offset += getChildPtrSize(nodePtr, i);
	}
	int ptrSize = getChildPtrSize(nodePtr, childId);
	const std::uint8_t * childPtr = (nodePtr + offset);
	if (ptrSize == 1) return size_t(*childPtr);
	if (ptrSize == 2) return size_t(*((std::uint16_t *)childPtr));
	if (ptrSize == 4) return size_t(*((std::uint32_t *)childPtr));
	return 0; // shouldnt arrive here
}
```

### src/voxelator/encoded_sdag3.hpp
```
#pragma once

#include <voxelator/encoded_octree.hpp>
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <vector>


class EncodedSDAG3 : public EncodedOctree {

public:
	virtual bool load(const std::string filename);
	virtual bool save(const std::string filename, const bool onlyHeader = false) const;
	virtual void encode(const GeomOctree & octree);
	virtual void * getDataPtr() const { return (void *)_dataInner.data(); }
	virtual size_t getDataSize() const { 
		return _dataInner.size() * sizeof(std::uint8_t) + _dataLeaves.size() * sizeof(std::uint8_t) + _levelsIdxTable.size() * sizeof(std::uint32_t);
	}
	virtual std::string getDescription() const { return "SDAG3. Like SDAG2 (4^3 leaves) but compressed pointers."; }

	// NOT REAL IMPLEMENTATION!
	virtual TravNode getRootTravNode() const { return TravNode(); }
	virtual bool hasChild(const TravNode &node, const int c) const { return false; }
	virtual TravNode getChild(const TravNode &node, const int c, bool &mX, bool &mY, bool &mZ) const { return TravNode(); }
	virtual bool isLeaf(const TravNode &node) const { return false; }
	virtual int getLeafSize() const { return 4; }
	virtual bool hasVoxel(const TravNode &leaf, const int i, const int j, const int k) const { return false; }

	virtual int traverse(glm::vec3 p) const;

protected:
	std::vector<std::uint8_t> _dataInner, _dataLeaves;
	std::vector<std::uint32_t> _levelsIdxTable;

public: // stats
	struct LevelStats {
		LevelStats() : nNodes(0), avgNumPtrsPerNode(0), avgSizeNode(0), nPtr8b(0), nPtr16b(0), nPtr32b(0) {}
		size_t nNodes;
		float avgNumPtrsPerNode;
		float avgSizeNode;
		size_t nPtr8b, nPtr16b, nPtr32b;
	};
	std::vector<LevelStats> levelsStats;

protected:
	static const int sizesMask[];
	void printNode(const std::uint8_t * nodePtr, bool leaf = false) const;
	bool existsChild(const std::uint8_t * nodePtr, int c) const;
	bool getChildMirrorBit(int axis, const std::uint8_t * nodePtr, int c) const;
	int getChildPtrSize(const std::uint8_t * nodePtr, int childId) const;
	int getNodeSize(const std::uint8_t * nodePtr, int &nNodes) const;
	std::uint32_t getChildIdx(const std::uint8_t * nodePtr, int childId) const;
};
```

### src/voxelator/encoded_sdag4.cpp
```
#include <iostream>
#include <glm/gtc/type_ptr.hpp>
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <common/format.hpp>
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>
#include <tuple>

#include <voxelator/encoded_sdag4.hpp>

const int EncodedSDAG4::sizesMask[] = { 0,1,2,2 };

EncodedSDAG4::EncodedSDAG4() {
	compute_compact_histogram_enabled_ = false;
	voxel_matrix_order_enabled_ = true;
	check_decode_enabled_ = true;
}

bool EncodedSDAG4::load(const std::string filename)
{
	printf("* Loading SDAG4 '%s'... ", filename.c_str()); fflush(stdout);
	std::ifstream ifs;
	ifs.open(filename, std::ios::in | std::ios::binary);
	if (!ifs.is_open()) {
		printf("FAILED!!!\n");
		return false;
	}

	_dataInner.clear();

	ifs.read((char *)glm::value_ptr(_sceneBBox[0]), 12);
	ifs.read((char *)glm::value_ptr(_sceneBBox[1]), 12);
	ifs.read((char *)&_rootSide, 4);
	ifs.read((char *)&_levels, 4);
	ifs.read((char *)&_nNodes, 4);
	unsigned int count;
	// inner nodes
	count = (unsigned int)_dataInner.size();
	ifs.read((char *)&count, 4);
	_dataInner.resize(count);
	ifs.read((char *)_dataInner.data(), count * sizeof(std::uint16_t));
	// leaves
	count = (unsigned int)_dataLeaves.size();
	ifs.read((char *)&count, 4);
	_dataLeaves.resize(count);
	ifs.read((char *)_dataLeaves.data(), count * sizeof(std::uint8_t));
	// level offset table
	count = (unsigned int)_levelOffsets.size();
	ifs.read((char *)&count, 4);
	_levelOffsets.resize(count);
	ifs.read((char *)_levelOffsets.data(), count * sizeof(std::uint32_t));

	printf("OK!\n");
	return true;
}

bool EncodedSDAG4::save(const std::string filename, const bool onlyHeader) const
{
	printf("* Saving SDAG4 '%s'... ", filename.c_str()); fflush(stdout);

	std::ofstream ofs;
	ofs.open(filename, std::ios::out | std::ios::binary);
	if (!ofs.is_open()) {
		printf("FAILED!\n");
		return false;
	}

	ofs.write((char *)glm::value_ptr(_sceneBBox[0]), 12);
	ofs.write((char *)glm::value_ptr(_sceneBBox[1]), 12);
	ofs.write((char *)&_rootSide, 4);
	ofs.write((char *)&_levels, 4);
	ofs.write((char *)&_nNodes, 4);
	unsigned int count;
	// inner nodes
	count = (unsigned int)_dataInner.size();
	ofs.write((char *)&count, 4);
	ofs.write((char *)_dataInner.data(), count * sizeof(std::uint16_t));
	// leaves
	count = (unsigned int)_dataLeaves.size();
	ofs.write((char *)&count, 4);
	ofs.write((char *)_dataLeaves.data(), count * sizeof(std::uint8_t));
	// level offset table
	count = (unsigned int)_levelOffsets.size();
	ofs.write((char *)&count, 4);
	ofs.write((char *)_levelOffsets.data(), count * sizeof(std::uint32_t));

	ofs.close();
	printf("OK!\n");
	return true;
}

static std::pair<std::uint8_t, std::uint8_t> xyz_to_byte_voxel(std::uint8_t x, std::uint8_t y, std::uint8_t z) {
	// Convert xyz matrix voxel position to  byte/index of the hierarchical pos
	std::uint8_t byte_x = x / 2;
	std::uint8_t byte_y = y / 2;
	std::uint8_t byte_z = z / 2;
	std::uint8_t bit_x = x % 2;
	std::uint8_t bit_y = y % 2;
	std::uint8_t bit_z = z % 2;
#if 0
	std::uint8_t byte_id = byte_x * 4 + byte_y * 2 + byte_z;
	std::uint8_t bit_id = bit_x * 4 + bit_y * 2 + bit_z;
#else
	std::uint8_t byte_id = byte_x + byte_y * 2 + byte_z * 4;
	std::uint8_t bit_id = bit_x + bit_y * 2 + bit_z * 4;
#endif
	return std::make_pair(byte_id, bit_id);
}

bool EncodedSDAG4::check_decode(const std::uint16_t* encoded_node,
	std::uint32_t child_id,
	const std::uint16_t child_offset,
	std::uint32_t verify_mask,
	std::uint32_t verify_addr,
	bool verify_mx,
	bool verify_my,
	bool verify_mz) const {
	// CHECK MASK
	int mask = getChildMask(encoded_node, child_id);
	if (mask != verify_mask) {
		std::cerr << "EncodedSDAG4::check_decode(): WRONG CHILD MASK " << mask << " vs " << verify_mask << std::endl;
		return false;
	}
	if (mask == 0) {
		std::cerr << "EncodedSDAG4::check_decode(): NULL CHILD MASK" << std::endl;
		return false;
	}

	// CHECK SIMMETRY
	std::uint16_t ptr_1 = encoded_node[child_offset];
	std::uint32_t result = 0;
	bool mx = ((ptr_1 >> 13) & 1) == 1;
	bool my = ((ptr_1 >> 14) & 1) == 1;
	bool mz = ((ptr_1 >> 15) & 1) == 1;

	if (mx != verify_mx) { std::cerr << "EncodedSDAG4::check_decode(): WRONG MX" << std::endl; return false; }
	if (my != verify_my) { std::cerr << "EncodedSDAG4::check_decode(): WRONG MY" << std::endl; return false; }
	if (mz != verify_mz) { std::cerr << "EncodedSDAG4::check_decode(): WRONG MZ" << std::endl; return false; }

	// CHECK PTR
	ptr_1 &= ~(7U << 13);

	if (mask == 1) {
		if (ptr_1 != verify_addr) {
			std::cerr << "EncodedSDAG4::check_decode(): WRONG 1US PTR LO " << ptr_1 << " !=  " << verify_addr << std::endl;
			return false;
		}
		else {
			result = ptr_1;
		}
	}
	else if (mask > 1) {
		std::uint16_t ptr_2 = encoded_node[child_offset + 1];
		result = (std::uint32_t(ptr_1) << 16) | ptr_2;

		std::uint32_t x_bit = mask & 1;
		result |= (x_bit << 29);
		if (result != verify_addr) {
			std::cerr << "EncodedSDAG4::check_decode(): WRONG 2US PTR " << result << " !=  " << verify_addr << " x bit " << x_bit << std::endl;
			return false;
		}
	}
	//  std::cerr << "test passed, c-addr " << result << std::endl;
	return true;
}

void EncodedSDAG4::encode(const GeomOctree & octree) {
	// ENCCODING
	// ---------
	//	leaves:	raw 4^3 voxels
	//          NO MORE! -> adressing ChildIdx[maxlev-1] * 8 + ChildIdx[maxlev]
	//
	//	nodes:	variable size
	//			header (2 bytes):	[0..1] childrenMask:
	//											00 : no child
	//											01 : 16b pointer
	//											1X : 32b pointer
	//											1X : 32b pointer
	//											(X is the 30th bit of 32bits pointers)
	//			body (2..32 bytes): children pointers
	//						if(16bits) -> [][][][.... 2^13 pointer ]
	//									  ^ ^ ^--- bits symmetry (x,y,z)
	//
	//											   __bit 30th -> bit X in header
	//						if(32bits) -> [][][][ []... 2^13 pointer ]
	//									  ^ ^ ^--- bits symmetry (x,y,z)
	// NO PADDING

	printf("* Encoding to SDAG4... ");

	if (octree.getState() != GeomOctree::S_SDAG) {
		printf("FAILED! Octree is not in SDAG state\n");
		return;
	}

	_sceneBBox = octree.getSceneBBox();
	_rootSide = octree.getRootSide();
	_levels = octree.getLevels();
	_nVoxels = octree.getNVoxels();
	_nNodes = octree.getNNodes();

	levelsStats.resize(_levels);

	const GeomOctree::NodeData &octData = octree.getNodeData();

	std::vector< std::vector<std::uint16_t> > encInners(_levels - 2);

	// histogram vector with pairs of idx, number of pointers to the nodes
	std::vector< std::pair< GeomOctree::id_t, GeomOctree::id_t > > hist;

	// indirection vector to map between one level indices (position in the vector)
	// and their children offsets
	std::vector<GeomOctree::id_t> indirection;

	// init for stats
	//SDAG2LevelsStats.resize(_levels - 1);

	_clock.restart();

	for (int lev = _levels - 2; lev >= 0; --lev) {

		LevelStats ls;

		if (octData[lev].size() > (1U << 30)) {
			printf("EncodedSDAG4:encode(): ERROR!!! Level %i size %zu.\nToo big for encoding in 30 bits!\n", lev, octData[lev].size());
			exit(1);
		}

		// clean and fit the refs vector
		hist.resize(octData[lev].size());
		hist.shrink_to_fit();

		// refs inizialization to ordered indices and zeros
		for (GeomOctree::id_t i = 0; i < octData[lev].size(); ++i) {
			hist[i].first = i; // idx
			hist[i].second = 0; // num of refs
		}

		if (lev > 0) { // Don't do it for the root node
			// count num of references in the superior level
			for (GeomOctree::Node n : octData[lev - 1])
				for (int c = 0; c < 8; ++c)
					if (n.existsChildPointer(c)) hist[n.children[c]].second++;

			// sort by number of references (c++11's lambdas r00lez)
			std::sort(hist.begin(), hist.end(),
				[](std::pair< GeomOctree::id_t, GeomOctree::id_t > a, std::pair< GeomOctree::id_t, GeomOctree::id_t> b) { return a.second > b.second; }
			);
		}

		std::vector<GeomOctree::id_t> newIndirection(octData[lev].size());

		if (lev == _levels - 2) { // LEAVES (two last levels, 4^3)

			_dataLeaves.resize(hist.size() * 8);
			std::uint8_t buf[8];
			std::uint8_t new_buf[8];
			for (GeomOctree::id_t i = 0; i < hist.size(); ++i) {
				newIndirection[hist[i].first] = i;
				const GeomOctree::Node &n = octData[lev][hist[i].first];
				if (!voxel_matrix_order_enabled_) {
					for (int c = 7; c >= 0; --c) {
						if (n.existsChildPointer(c)) {
							const GeomOctree::Node &cn = octData[lev + 1][n.children[c]];
							GeomOctree::Node cnm = cn.mirror(n.getChildMirrrorBit(0, c),
								n.getChildMirrrorBit(1, c),
								n.getChildMirrrorBit(2, c));
							_dataLeaves[i * 8 + c] = cnm.childrenBitmask;
						}
						else _dataLeaves[i * 8 + c] = 0;
					}
				}
				else {
					// Fetch the 8 leaf bytes 
					for (int c = 7; c >= 0; --c) {
						if (n.existsChildPointer(c)) {
							const GeomOctree::Node &cn = octData[lev + 1][n.children[c]];
							GeomOctree::Node cnm = cn.mirror(n.getChildMirrrorBit(0, c),
								n.getChildMirrrorBit(1, c),
								n.getChildMirrrorBit(2, c));
							buf[c] = cnm.childrenBitmask;
						}
						else {
							buf[c] = 0;
						}
					}

					memset(new_buf, 0, 8);

					// Shuffle bytes and bits to store bits in matrix row order
					std::uint8_t byte_pos = 0;
					std::uint8_t bit_pos = 0;
					std::uint8_t output_offset = 0;
					for (std::uint8_t z = 0; z < 4; ++z) {
						for (std::uint8_t y = 0; y < 4; ++y) {
							for (std::uint8_t x = 0; x < 4; ++x) {
								std::tie(byte_pos, bit_pos) = xyz_to_byte_voxel(x, y, z);

								std::uint8_t voxel = (buf[byte_pos] >> bit_pos) & 1;

								std::uint8_t output_byte_index = output_offset / 8;
								std::uint8_t output_bit_index = output_offset % 8;

								new_buf[output_byte_index] |= (voxel << output_bit_index);

								++output_offset;
							}
						}
					}

					// bool all_zero_new = true;
					// bool all_zero_old = true;
					// for(std::uint8_t k = 0; k < 8; ++k) {
					//   all_zero_old &= (buf[k] == 0);
					//   all_zero_new &= (new_buf[k] == 0);
					// }
					// if (all_zero_new || all_zero_old) {
					//   std::cerr << "WARNING ALL ZERO" << all_zero_old << " " << all_zero_new << std::endl;
					//   exit(1);
					// }

#if 0
					std::cerr << "Hierarchy" << std::endl;
					for (std::uint8_t i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
						for (int b = 0; b < 8; ++b) {
							std::cerr << ((buf[i] >> (7 - b)) & 1);
						}
						std::cerr << " ";
					}
					std::cerr << std::endl;
					std::cerr << "Linear" << std::endl;
					for (std::uint8_t i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
						for (int b = 0; b < 8; ++b) {
							std::cerr << ((new_buf[i] >> (7 - b)) & 1);
						}
						std::cerr << " ";
					}
					std::cerr << std::endl;
#endif
					// Write to output
					for (std::uint8_t c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
						_dataLeaves[i * 8 + c] = new_buf[c];
					}
				}

				ls.nNodes++;
				ls.avgSizeNode += 8;
			}
		}
		else { // INNER NODES

			for (GeomOctree::id_t i = 0; i < hist.size(); ++i) {
				const GeomOctree::Node &n = octData[lev][hist[i].first];

				std::uint16_t tmpEncNode[100];

				// tmpEncNode[0..1] <- inizializate to zero
				tmpEncNode[0] = 0;

				int tmpNodeSize = 1;

				for (int c = 7; c >= 0; --c) {
					if (n.existsChild(c)) {
						std::uint32_t child_offset = tmpNodeSize;
						std::uint32_t verify_mask = 0;
						GeomOctree::id_t childAddress = indirection[n.children[c]];
						if (childAddress < (1U << 13)) { // < 2^13
							std::uint16_t encChildPtr = std::uint16_t(childAddress);
							tmpEncNode[0] |= (1U << (2 * c)); // mask 01
							if (n.getChildMirrrorBit(0, c)) encChildPtr |= 1U << 13U; //mX
							if (n.getChildMirrrorBit(1, c)) encChildPtr |= 1U << 14U; //mY
							if (n.getChildMirrrorBit(2, c)) encChildPtr |= 1U << 15U; //mZ
							tmpEncNode[tmpNodeSize] = encChildPtr;
							tmpNodeSize += 1;
							ls.nPtr16b++;
							verify_mask = 1;
						}
						else if (childAddress < (1U << 30)) { // < 2^30
							std::uint32_t encChildPtr = std::uint32_t(childAddress);
							if (encChildPtr & (1U << 29U)) {
								tmpEncNode[0] |= (3U << (2 * c)); // mask 11
								encChildPtr &= (~(1U << 29));       // Set to 0 29 bit
								ls.nPtr29b++;
								verify_mask = 3;
							}
							else {
								tmpEncNode[0] |= (2U << (2 * c)); // mask 10
								ls.nPtr28b++;
								verify_mask = 2;
							}

							if (n.getChildMirrrorBit(0, c)) encChildPtr |= 1U << 29U;
							if (n.getChildMirrrorBit(1, c)) encChildPtr |= 1U << 30U;
							if (n.getChildMirrrorBit(2, c)) encChildPtr |= 1U << 31U;
							//							*(std::uint32_t *)(&tmpEncNode[tmpNodeSize]) = encChildPtr;
							// Encode in the first ushort the most significant bits 
							tmpEncNode[tmpNodeSize] = encChildPtr >> 16;
							++tmpNodeSize;
							// Encode in the first ushort the least significant bits 
							tmpEncNode[tmpNodeSize] = encChildPtr & 0xFFFF;
							++tmpNodeSize;
							//							ls.nPtr32b++;
						}
						else {
							std::cerr << "ERRROR Unsupported pointer sz " << childAddress << " > 2^30 " << (1U << 30) << std::endl;

						}

						if (check_decode_enabled_ &&
							!check_decode(tmpEncNode, c, child_offset, verify_mask, childAddress,
								n.getChildMirrrorBit(0, c),
								n.getChildMirrrorBit(1, c),
								n.getChildMirrrorBit(2, c))) {
							exit(1);
						}

						ls.avgNumPtrsPerNode++;
					}
				}

				// update the new indirection table
				newIndirection[hist[i].first] = std::uint32_t(encInners[lev].size());

				encInners[lev].insert(encInners[lev].end(), tmpEncNode, tmpEncNode + tmpNodeSize);

				ls.nNodes++;
				ls.avgSizeNode += tmpNodeSize;
			}
		}

		indirection = newIndirection;

		if (compute_compact_histogram_enabled_) {
			//std::cerr << "Compact histogram level " << lev << std::endl;
			build_compact_histogram_in(ls.compact_histogram, hist);
		}
		else {
			//std::cerr << "Disabled compact histogram" << std::endl;
		}

		ls.avgNumPtrsPerNode /= ls.nNodes;
		ls.avgSizeNode /= ls.nNodes;
		levelsStats[lev] = ls;
	}

	// create a index table per level
	_levelOffsets.resize(_levels - 2);
	_levelOffsets[0] = 0;
	for (int i = 1; i < _levelOffsets.size(); ++i)
		_levelOffsets[i] = std::uint32_t(encInners[i - 1].size()) + _levelOffsets[i - 1];

	// join all the encoded inner levels into a the single encoded data array
	_dataInner.clear();
	for (int i = 0; i < encInners.size(); ++i)
		_dataInner.insert(_dataInner.end(), encInners[i].begin(), encInners[i].end());

	_encodingTime = _clock.elapsed();
	printf("OK! [%s]\n", vox::human_readable_duration(_encodingTime).c_str());
#if 0
	for (int i = 0; i < levelsStats.size(); ++i) {
		printf("\t-Level %2i:  Ptr16:%12zu\tPtr28:%12zu\tPtr29:%12zu\n", i, levelsStats[i].nPtr16b, levelsStats[i].nPtr28b, levelsStats[i].nPtr29b);
	}
#endif
}

EncodedOctree::TravNode EncodedSDAG4::getRootTravNode() const
{
	TravNode tn;
	tn.idx = 0;
	tn.level = 0;
	return tn;
}

bool EncodedSDAG4::hasChild(const TravNode & node, const int c) const
{
	return ((_dataInner[node.idx] >> (2U * c)) & 3U) != 0;
}

EncodedOctree::TravNode EncodedSDAG4::getChild(const TravNode & node, const int c, bool & mX, bool & mY, bool & mZ) const
{
	TravNode tn;
	if (node.level >= _levels - 2) {
		printf("EncodedSDAG4::getChild: WARNING! Asking for a node child, but node is in its max level\n");
		return tn;
	}

	unsigned int offset = 1;
	for (int i = 7; i > c; --i) {
		offset += std::min((_dataInner[node.idx] >> (2U * i)) & 3U, 2U);
	}
	tn.idx = _dataInner[node.idx + offset];
	std::uint32_t childMask = (_dataInner[node.idx] >> (2U * c)) & 3U;
	mX = ((tn.idx >> 13U) & 1U) == 1;
	mY = ((tn.idx >> 14U) & 1U) == 1;
	mZ = ((tn.idx >> 15U) & 1U) == 1;
	tn.idx &= ~(7U << 13U);

	if (childMask > 1) {
		std::uint16_t tmp = _dataInner[node.idx + offset + 1];
		tn.idx = (tn.idx << 16) | tmp;
		tn.idx |= (childMask & 1U) << 29U;
	}
	tn.level = node.level + 1;
	if (tn.level < _levels - 2)
		tn.idx += _levelOffsets[tn.level];
	else
		tn.idx *= 8;

	return tn;
}

bool EncodedSDAG4::isLeaf(const TravNode & node) const
{
	return node.level == (_levels - 2);
}

bool EncodedSDAG4::hasVoxel(const TravNode & leaf, const int i, const int j, const int k) const
{
	int c = i + 4 * j + 16 * k;
	int idxByte = leaf.idx + (c / 8);
	int idxBit = c % 8;
	return (_dataLeaves[idxByte] & (1U << idxBit)) != 0;
}

int EncodedSDAG4::traverse(glm::vec3 p) const
{
	if (!_sceneBBox.contains(p)) return -1;

	size_t nodeIdx = 0;
	unsigned int nodeLevel = 0;
	glm::vec3 nodeCenter = _sceneBBox.center();
	bool mX = false, mY = false, mZ = false;

	while (nodeLevel < _levels) {
		const std::uint16_t * nodePtr = &_dataInner[nodeIdx];
		if (mX) p[0] = 2.f * nodeCenter[0] - p[0];
		if (mY) p[1] = 2.f * nodeCenter[1] - p[1];
		if (mZ) p[2] = 2.f * nodeCenter[2] - p[2];
		int selectedChild = 4 * int(p[0] > nodeCenter[0]) + 2 * int(p[1] > nodeCenter[1]) + int(p[2] > nodeCenter[2]);
		if (!existsChild(nodePtr, selectedChild)) break;
		nodeLevel++;
		float hs = getHalfSide(nodeLevel);
		nodeCenter[0] += (p[0] > nodeCenter[0]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
		nodeCenter[1] += (p[1] > nodeCenter[1]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
		nodeCenter[2] += (p[2] > nodeCenter[2]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
		std::uint32_t childIdx = getChild(nodePtr, selectedChild, mX, mY, mZ);
		if (nodeLevel < _levels - 2) { // INNER
			nodeIdx = _levelOffsets[nodeLevel] + childIdx;
		}
		else if (nodeLevel == _levels - 2) { // 4^3 LEAVES
			size_t leafIdx = childIdx * 8;
			const std::uint8_t * leafPtr = &_dataLeaves[leafIdx];
			if (mX) p[0] = 2.f * nodeCenter[0] - p[0];
			if (mY) p[1] = 2.f * nodeCenter[1] - p[1];
			if (mZ) p[2] = 2.f * nodeCenter[2] - p[2];
			selectedChild = 4 * int(p[0] > nodeCenter[0]) + 2 * int(p[1] > nodeCenter[1]) + int(p[2] > nodeCenter[2]);
			if (*(leafPtr + selectedChild) == 0) break;

			nodeLevel++;
			hs = getHalfSide(nodeLevel);
			nodeCenter[0] += (p[0] > nodeCenter[0]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
			nodeCenter[1] += (p[1] > nodeCenter[1]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
			nodeCenter[2] += (p[2] > nodeCenter[2]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
			std::uint8_t selectedVoxel = 4 * int(p[0] > nodeCenter[0]) + 2 * int(p[1] > nodeCenter[1]) + int(p[2] > nodeCenter[2]);
			if (_dataLeaves[leafIdx + selectedChild] & (1U << selectedVoxel)) nodeLevel++;

			break;
		}
	}

	return nodeLevel;
}


bool EncodedSDAG4::existsChild(const std::uint16_t * nodePtr, int c) const {
	return getChildMask(nodePtr, c) != 0;
}

bool EncodedSDAG4::getChildMirrorBit(int axis, const std::uint16_t * nodePtr, int c) const {
	bool m[3];
	getChild(nodePtr, c, m[0], m[1], m[2]);
	return m[axis];
}

int EncodedSDAG4::getChildPtrSize(const std::uint16_t * nodePtr, int childId) const {
	return sizesMask[getChildMask(nodePtr, childId)];
}

int EncodedSDAG4::getChildMask(const std::uint16_t * nodePtr, int childId) const {
	return (nodePtr[0] >> (2 * childId)) & 3U;
}

int EncodedSDAG4::getNodeSize(const std::uint16_t * nodePtr, int &nNodes) const {
	/*
		unsigned int nodeSize = 5; // header
		nNodes = 0;
		for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
			int cPtrSize = getChildPtrSize(nodePtr, i);
			if (cPtrSize > 0) nNodes++;
			nodeSize += cPtrSize;
		}
		return nodeSize;
		*/
	return 0;
}

std::uint32_t EncodedSDAG4::getChild(const std::uint16_t * nodePtr, int childId, bool &mX, bool &mY, bool &mZ) const {
	int offset = 1; // init to header size
	for (int i = 7; i > childId; --i) {
		offset += getChildPtrSize(nodePtr, i);
	}
	int mask = getChildMask(nodePtr, childId);
	const std::uint16_t * childPtr = (nodePtr + offset);

	std::uint32_t childIdx = 0;
	if (mask == 1) {
		childIdx = std::uint32_t(*childPtr);
		mX = (childIdx & (1U << 13U)) != 0;
		mY = (childIdx & (1U << 14U)) != 0;
		mZ = (childIdx & (1U << 15U)) != 0;
		childIdx &= ~(7U << 13U);
	}
	else if (mask == 2 || mask == 3) {
		childIdx = std::uint32_t(*((std::uint32_t *)childPtr));
		mX = (childIdx & (1U << 29U)) != 0;
		mY = (childIdx & (1U << 30U)) != 0;
		mZ = (childIdx & (1U << 31U)) != 0;
		childIdx &= ~(7U << 29U);
		childIdx |= ((mask & 1U) << 29U);
	}
	else {
		// shouldnt arrive here
		printf("EncodedSDAG4::getChild(): WARNING. Asking for a inexistent child\n");
	}

	return childIdx;
}

void EncodedSDAG4::set_compute_compact_histogram(bool x) {
	compute_compact_histogram_enabled_ = x;
}

void EncodedSDAG4::set_voxel_matrix_order(bool x) {
	voxel_matrix_order_enabled_ = x;
}

void EncodedSDAG4::build_compact_histogram_in(std::vector<std::uint32_t>& compact_histogram,
	const std::vector< std::pair< GeomOctree::id_t, GeomOctree::id_t > >& hist) const {
	// Make an histogram, with % values on x and y.
	// Use 10 bins
	std::size_t N = hist.size();
	std::size_t bin_count = 10;
	std::size_t compact_histogram_samples_per_bin = std::max(N / bin_count, std::size_t(1));
	compact_histogram.resize(bin_count);
	std::size_t total_count = 0;
	std::cerr << "build_compact_histogram from " << N << " samples, to " << compact_histogram_samples_per_bin << " samples per bin" << std::endl;
	for (std::size_t ci = 0; ci < bin_count; ++ci) {
		compact_histogram[ci] = 0;
		std::size_t offset = ci*compact_histogram_samples_per_bin;
		for (std::size_t s = 0; s < compact_histogram_samples_per_bin && offset < N; ++s) {
			compact_histogram[ci] += hist[offset].second;
			++offset;
		}
		total_count += compact_histogram[ci];
	}
	if (total_count) {
		for (std::size_t ci = 0; ci < bin_count; ++ci) {
			compact_histogram[ci] = (unsigned int)(std::size_t(compact_histogram[ci]) * 100 / total_count);
		}
	}
}
