### src/voxelator/geom_octree.cpp
```
#include <queue>
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <common/format.hpp>
#include <common/timing.hpp>
#include <common/aabb.hpp>
#include <cstdint>
#include <map>
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>
#include <stack>
#include <set>
#include <omp.h>


#include <voxelator/geom_octree.hpp>
#include <voxelator/test_triangle_box.hpp>

GeomOctree::GeomOctree(Scene * scene) : Octree(scene) {
	_state = S_EMPTY;
}


GeomOctree::GeomOctree(const GeomOctree &other) : Octree (other) {
	_state = other._state;
	_stats = other._stats;
	_data.reserve(other._data.size());
	_data = other._data;
}

void GeomOctree::buildSVO(unsigned int levels, vox::AABB3d bbox, bool internalCall, std::vector< glm::dvec3 > * leavesCenters, bool putMaterialIdInLeaves) {
	
	if (!internalCall) printf("* Building SVO... "); fflush(stdout);

	_bbox = vox::AABB3f(bbox);
	_levels = levels;
	glm::vec3 sides =_bbox.half_side_lengths() * 2.0f;
	_rootSide = std::max(std::max(sides[0], sides[1]), sides[2]);
	
	_data.resize(_levels);

	Node root;
	_data[0].push_back(root);

	struct QueueItem {
		QueueItem(id_t id, std::uint8_t l, glm::dvec3 c) : nodeID(id), level(l), center(c) {}
		id_t nodeID;
		std::uint8_t level;
		glm::dvec3 center;
	};
	glm::dvec3 childrenCenters[8];
	std::stack<QueueItem> queue;

	if(!internalCall) _clock.restart();

	int stepLogger = (int)round(_scene->getNTriangles() / 10.f);
	for (std::size_t iTri = 0; iTri < _scene->getNTriangles(); iTri++) {
		if (!internalCall && (iTri % stepLogger == 0)) {
			printf("%.0f%%..", round(100.f * (iTri / (float)_scene->getNTriangles())));
			fflush(stdout);
		}
		unsigned int triMatId;
		if (putMaterialIdInLeaves) triMatId = (unsigned int)_scene->getTriangleMaterialId(iTri);
		queue.push(QueueItem(0, 0, bbox.center()));
		while (!queue.empty()) {
			const QueueItem qi = queue.top(); queue.pop();

			double k = getHalfSideD(qi.level + 1);
			childrenCenters[PXPYPZ] = qi.center + glm::dvec3(+k, +k, +k);
			childrenCenters[PXPYNZ] = qi.center + glm::dvec3(+k, +k, -k);
			childrenCenters[PXNYPZ] = qi.center + glm::dvec3(+k, -k, +k);
			childrenCenters[PXNYNZ] = qi.center + glm::dvec3(+k, -k, -k);
			childrenCenters[NXPYPZ] = qi.center + glm::dvec3(-k, +k, +k);
			childrenCenters[NXPYNZ] = qi.center + glm::dvec3(-k, +k, -k);
			childrenCenters[NXNYPZ] = qi.center + glm::dvec3(-k, -k, +k);
			childrenCenters[NXNYNZ] = qi.center + glm::dvec3(-k, -k, -k);

			Node & node = _data[qi.level][qi.nodeID];
			for (int i = 7; i >= 0; --i) {
				if (testTriBox(childrenCenters[i], k, _scene->getTriangleVertice(iTri,0), _scene->getTriangleVertice(iTri, 1), _scene->getTriangleVertice(iTri, 2))) {
					node.setChildBit(i);
					if ((qi.level < (_levels - 1)) && !node.existsChildPointer(i)) {
						node.children[i] = (id_t)_data[qi.level + 1].size();
						_data[qi.level + 1].emplace_back();
						_nNodes++;
						if (leavesCenters!=NULL && (qi.level == (_levels - 2))) leavesCenters->push_back(childrenCenters[i]);
					}
					if((qi.level+1U) < _levels)
						queue.push(QueueItem(node.children[i], qi.level + 1, childrenCenters[i]));
					else {
						if (putMaterialIdInLeaves) node.children[i] = (id_t)triMatId;
#if 0 // outputs a debug obj of voxels as points with their colours
						glm::vec3 c;
						_scene->getTriangleColor(iTri, c);
						printf("v %f %f %f %f %f %f\n", childrenCenters[i][0], childrenCenters[i][1], childrenCenters[i][2], c[0], c[1], c[2]);
#endif
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	cleanEmptyNodes();
	if (!internalCall) _stats.buildSVOTime = _clock.elapsed();

	// compute NVoxels
	for (id_t i = 0; i < _data[_levels - 1].size(); ++i) {
		_nVoxels += _data[_levels - 1][i].getNChildren();
	}
	
	_state = S_SVO;
	_stats.nNodesSVO = _nNodes;
	_stats.nNodesLastLevSVO = _data[_levels - 1].size();
	_stats.simulatedEncodedSVOSize = (_stats.nNodesSVO - _stats.nNodesLastLevSVO) * 4;
	_stats.nTotalVoxels = _nVoxels;

	if(!internalCall) printf("OK! [%s]\n",vox::human_readable_duration(_stats.buildSVOTime).c_str());
}


void GeomOctree::buildDAG(unsigned int levels, unsigned int stepLevel, vox::AABB3d bbox, bool verbose) {
	
	printf("* Building DAG [stepLevel: %i]\n", stepLevel); fflush(stdout);
	
	std::vector< glm::dvec3 > leavesCenters;
	
	unsigned int stepLevels = stepLevel + 1;

	_clock.restart();
	vox::time_duration timeStamp = _clock.elapsed();

	printf("\t- Building root SVO subtree... ");
	
	buildSVO(stepLevels, bbox, true, &leavesCenters);
	
	printf("OK! [%s]\n", vox::human_readable_duration(_clock.elapsed() - timeStamp).c_str()); fflush(stdout);
	

	double lhs = getHalfSideD(stepLevels - 1);
	glm::dvec3 corners[8];
	corners[PXPYPZ] = glm::dvec3(+lhs, +lhs, +lhs); // PXPYPZ
	corners[PXPYNZ] = glm::dvec3(+lhs, +lhs, -lhs); // PXPYNZ
	corners[PXNYPZ] = glm::dvec3(+lhs, -lhs, +lhs); // PXNYPZ
	corners[PXNYNZ] = glm::dvec3(+lhs, -lhs, -lhs); // PXNYNZ
	corners[NXPYPZ] = glm::dvec3(-lhs, +lhs, +lhs); // NXPYPZ
	corners[NXPYNZ] = glm::dvec3(-lhs, +lhs, -lhs); // NXPYNZ
	corners[NXNYPZ] = glm::dvec3(-lhs, -lhs, +lhs); // NXNYPZ
	corners[NXNYNZ] = glm::dvec3(-lhs, -lhs, -lhs); // NXNYNZ

	_stats.nNodesSVO = _nNodes;
	_stats.nNodesLastLevSVO = 0;
	std::map<unsigned int, GeomOctree *> leavesOctrees;

	float nodesProcessed = 0;
	const size_t nodesToProcess = _nVoxels;
	size_t memConsumed = 0;
	size_t acumSubtreesDAGNNodes = 0;

	printf("\t- Building %zu subtress (SVO->DAG) [%i threads]... ", _nVoxels, (int)omp_get_max_threads());
	if (verbose) printf("\n");
	timeStamp = _clock.elapsed();
	float acumDAGTimeFactor = 0;
#if 1 // More memory consumtion, but ensures CPUs occupancy
#pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic,2)
#else
#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
#endif
	for (int i = 0; i < _data[_levels - 1].size(); ++i) {
		for (int j = 7; j >= 0; --j) {
			if (_data[_levels - 1][i].existsChild(j)) {
				vox::time_duration threatInitTime = _clock.elapsed();
				GeomOctree * leafOctree = new GeomOctree(_scene);
				vox::AABB3d lbbox;
				lbbox.to_empty();
				lbbox.merge(leavesCenters[i]);
				lbbox.merge(leavesCenters[i] + corners[j]);
				leafOctree->buildSVO(levels - stepLevels, lbbox, true);
				size_t nNodesLeafSVO = leafOctree->getNNodes();
				size_t nNodesLeafLastLevSVO = leafOctree->_data[leafOctree->_data.size() - 1].size();
#pragma omp atomic
				_stats.nNodesSVO += nNodesLeafSVO;
#pragma omp atomic
				_stats.nNodesLastLevSVO += nNodesLeafLastLevSVO;
				size_t incVoxels = leafOctree->getNVoxels() - 1;
#pragma omp atomic
				_nVoxels += incVoxels; // root doesn't count

				leafOctree->toDAG(true);

#pragma omp atomic		
				nodesProcessed += 1.f;
				size_t incNNodes = leafOctree->getNNodes();
#pragma omp atomic		
				acumSubtreesDAGNNodes += incNNodes;
				
#pragma omp atomic
				acumDAGTimeFactor += leafOctree->getStats().toDAGTime.count() / (float)(_clock.elapsed() - threatInitTime).count();
#pragma omp critical
				{
					leavesOctrees[i * 8 + j] = leafOctree;
					memConsumed += leafOctree->getMemFootprint();
					_stats.memFootprint = std::max(_stats.memFootprint, leafOctree->getMemFootprint());
					if (verbose) {
						printf("\t\t- [%3d%%] [%3u-%u / %zu (%d)]\t[Mem: %s]\t%zu -> %zu\n", int(100.f * (nodesProcessed / (float)nodesToProcess)), i, j, _data[_levels - 1].size(), omp_get_thread_num(), vox::human_readable_size(memConsumed).c_str(), nNodesLeafSVO, leafOctree->getNNodes());
						fflush(stdout);
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	acumDAGTimeFactor /= nodesProcessed;
	vox::time_duration acumDAGTime = _clock.elapsed() * acumDAGTimeFactor;

	if(verbose)
		printf("\t\t- [100%%] All subtrees done! [%s]\n", vox::human_readable_duration(_clock.elapsed() - timeStamp).c_str());
	else
		printf("OK! [%s]\n", vox::human_readable_duration(_clock.elapsed() - timeStamp).c_str());

	_stats.simulatedEncodedSVOSize = (_stats.nNodesSVO - _stats.nNodesLastLevSVO) * 4;
	_stats.nTotalVoxels = _nVoxels;

	_data.resize(levels);
	_levels = levels;

	timeStamp = _clock.elapsed();

	printf("\t- Joining subtrees... ");

	for (int i = 0; i < _data[stepLevels - 1].size(); ++i) {
		for (int j = 7; j >= 0; --j) {
			if (_data[stepLevels - 1][i].existsChild(j)) {
				GeomOctree * oct = leavesOctrees[i * 8 + j];
				_data[stepLevels - 1][i].children[j] = (id_t)_data[stepLevels].size();
				for (unsigned int k = stepLevels; k < levels; ++k) {
					if (k < (levels - 1)) { // update pointers
						id_t offset = (id_t)_data[k + 1].size();
						for (unsigned int m = 0; m < oct->_data[k - stepLevels].size(); ++m) {
							for (unsigned int n = 0; n < 8; ++n) {
								if (oct->_data[k - stepLevels][m].existsChildPointer(n))
									oct->_data[k - stepLevels][m].children[n] += offset;
							}
						}
					}
					_data[k].insert(_data[k].end(), oct->_data[k - stepLevels].begin(), oct->_data[k - stepLevels].end());
				}
				delete oct;
			}
		}
	}

	printf("OK! [%s]\n", vox::human_readable_duration(_clock.elapsed() - timeStamp).c_str());
	
	printf("\t- Last DAG pass to whole subtree... ");
	acumDAGTime += (_clock.elapsed() - timeStamp);
	timeStamp = _clock.elapsed();
	
	toDAG(true);

	acumDAGTime += (_clock.elapsed() - timeStamp);
	_stats.toDAGTime = acumDAGTime;
	printf("OK! [%s]\t(%zu -> %zu)\n", vox::human_readable_duration(_clock.elapsed() - timeStamp).c_str(), acumSubtreesDAGNNodes, _nNodes);

	_stats.buildDAGTime = _clock.elapsed();

	printf("\t- Finished! Total time [%s]\n", vox::human_readable_duration(_stats.buildDAGTime).c_str());

}

unsigned int GeomOctree::cleanEmptyNodes() {
	std::set<id_t> emptyNodes;
	unsigned int nDelPtrs = 0;
	for (int lev = _levels - 1; lev > 0; --lev) {
		emptyNodes.clear();
		for (id_t i = 0; i < _data[lev].size(); ++i) {
			if (!_data[lev][i].hasChildren()) emptyNodes.insert(i);
		}
		for (id_t i = 0; i < _data[lev-1].size(); ++i) {
			for (int j = 0; j < 8; ++j) {
				if (emptyNodes.find(_data[lev - 1][i].children[j]) != emptyNodes.end()) {
					_data[lev-1][i].unsetChildBit(j);
					_data[lev-1][i].children[j] = nullNode;
					nDelPtrs++;
				}
			}
		}
	}
	return nDelPtrs;
}

void GeomOctree::toDAG(bool iternalCall) {

	if (_state == S_SVO) {
		if (!iternalCall) printf("* Transforming SVO -> DAG ... "); fflush(stdout);
	} else {
		printf("ERROR! This is not a SVO!\n");
		return;
	}

	_nNodes = 1;
	std::vector<id_t> correspondences;
	std::map<Node, id_t> uniqueNodesChecker;
	std::vector<Node> uniqueNodes;

	 vox::time_duration ts = _clock.elapsed();

	for (unsigned int lev = _levels - 1; lev > 0; --lev) {
		size_t oldLevSize = _data[lev].size();
		uniqueNodes.clear();
		uniqueNodes.shrink_to_fit();
		uniqueNodesChecker.clear();
		correspondences.clear();
		correspondences.resize(oldLevSize);
		for (id_t i = 0; i < _data[lev].size(); i++) {
			Node n = _data[lev][i];
			if (!n.hasChildren()) continue; // skip empty nodes
			auto k = uniqueNodesChecker.find(n);

			if (k != uniqueNodesChecker.end()) { // found
				correspondences[i] = (*k).second;
			}
			else { // !found
				uniqueNodesChecker[n] = (id_t)uniqueNodes.size();
				correspondences[i] = (id_t)uniqueNodes.size();
				uniqueNodes.push_back(n);
			}
		}

		_data[lev].clear();
		_data[lev].shrink_to_fit();
		uniqueNodes.shrink_to_fit();
#if 0		
		std::copy(uniqueNodes.begin(), uniqueNodes.end(), _data[lev].begin());
#else
		_data[lev] = uniqueNodes;
#endif
		_data[lev].shrink_to_fit();
		_nNodes += _data[lev].size();
		
		for (id_t i = 0; i < _data[lev-1].size(); i++) {
			Node * bn = &_data[lev-1][i];
			for (int j = 0; j < 8; j++) {
				if (bn->existsChild(j)) bn->children[j] = correspondences[bn->children[j]];
			}
		}

	}

	_stats.toDAGTime = _clock.elapsed() - ts;
	
	_state = S_DAG;
	_stats.nNodesDAG = _nNodes;

	if (!iternalCall) printf("OK! [%s]\n", vox::human_readable_duration(_stats.toDAGTime).c_str());
}


void GeomOctree::toDAG2(bool iternalCall) {

	if (_state == S_SVO) {
		if (!iternalCall) printf("* Transforming SVO -> DAG (2th method, for some reason much slower) ... "); fflush(stdout);
	}
	else {
		printf("ERROR! This is not a SVO!\n");
		return;
	}

	_nNodes = 1;
	vox::time_duration ts = _clock.elapsed();
	std::vector<id_t> sortedIdx;
	std::vector<Node> newLev;
	std::vector<id_t> mapping;

	for (unsigned int lev = _levels - 1; lev > 0; --lev) {

		sortedIdx.clear();
		newLev.clear();
		mapping.resize(_data[lev].size());

		sortIndexes(sortedIdx, _data[lev]);
		std::uint32_t last_written = 0, pivot = 0;
		for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < _data[lev].size(); i = pivot) {
			const Node & uniqueNode = _data[lev][sortedIdx[i]];
			while ((pivot<_data[lev].size()) && (_data[lev][sortedIdx[pivot] ] == uniqueNode)) pivot++;
			for (unsigned int k = i; k < pivot; ++k) mapping[sortedIdx[k]] = (Octree::id_t)newLev.size();
			newLev.push_back(uniqueNode);
		}

		_data[lev].clear();
		_data[lev] = newLev;
		_data[lev].shrink_to_fit();
		_nNodes += _data[lev].size();

		for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < _data[lev-1].size(); ++i) {
			for (int c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
				if (_data[lev - 1][i].existsChild(c)) {
					_data[lev - 1][i].children[c] = mapping[_data[lev - 1][i].children[c]];
				}
			}
		}


	}

	_stats.toDAGTime = _clock.elapsed() - ts;

	_state = S_DAG;
	_stats.nNodesDAG = _nNodes;

	if (!iternalCall) printf("OK! [%s]\n", vox::human_readable_duration(_stats.toDAGTime).c_str());
}


void GeomOctree::toSDAG(bool internalCall) {

	if (!internalCall) {
		if (_state == S_SVO) {
			printf("* Transforming SVO -> SDAG (not best way)... "); fflush(stdout);
		}
		else if (_state == S_DAG) {
			printf("* Transforming DAG -> SDAG (normal)... "); fflush(stdout);
		}
		else {
			printf("ERROR! This is not a DAG or SDAG!\n");
			return;
		}
	}

	struct MirroredNode{
		MirroredNode() : mirrorX(false), mirrorY(false), mirrorZ(false), id(0) {}
		MirroredNode(bool mX, bool mY, bool mZ, id_t id) :
			mirrorX(mX), mirrorY(mY), mirrorZ(mZ), id(id) {}
		bool mirrorX, mirrorY, mirrorZ;
		id_t id;
	};

	std::vector<MirroredNode> correspondences;
	std::map<Node, id_t> uniqueNodesChecker;
	std::vector<Node> uniqueNodes;

	_nNodes = 0;

	if (!internalCall) _clock.restart();

	for (unsigned int lev = _levels - 1; lev > 0; lev--) {
		unsigned int oldLevSize = (unsigned int)_data[lev].size();
		uniqueNodes.clear();
		uniqueNodesChecker.clear();
		correspondences.resize(oldLevSize);

		for (id_t i = 0; i < _data[lev].size(); i++) {

			Node n = _data[lev][i];
			if (!n.hasChildren()) continue; // skip empty nodes

			// Check invariances
			if (n == n.mirror(true, false, false)) n.setInvariantBit(0);
			if (n == n.mirror(false, true, false)) n.setInvariantBit(1);
			if (n == n.mirror(false, false, true)) n.setInvariantBit(2);

			Node niX = n.mirror(true, false, false);
			Node niY = n.mirror(false, true, false);
			Node niZ = n.mirror(false, false, true);
			Node niXY = niX.mirror(false, true, false);
			Node niXZ = niX.mirror(false, false, true);
			Node niYZ = niY.mirror(false, false, true);
			Node niXYZ = niXY.mirror(false, false, true);

			// Check children invariances
			if (lev < (_levels - 1)) {
				invertInvs(niX, lev, true, false, false);
				invertInvs(niY, lev, false, true, false);
				invertInvs(niZ, lev, false, false, true);
				invertInvs(niXY, lev, true, true, false);
				invertInvs(niXZ, lev, true, false, true);
				invertInvs(niYZ, lev, false, true, true);
				invertInvs(niXYZ, lev, true, true, true);
			}

			std::map<GeomOctree::Node, std::uint32_t>::iterator it;
			if ((it = uniqueNodesChecker.find(n)) != uniqueNodesChecker.end()) {
				correspondences[i] = MirroredNode(false, false, false, (*it).second);
			}
			else if ((it = uniqueNodesChecker.find(niX)) != uniqueNodesChecker.end()) {
				correspondences[i] = MirroredNode(true, false, false, (*it).second);
			}
			else if ((it = uniqueNodesChecker.find(niY)) != uniqueNodesChecker.end()) {
				correspondences[i] = MirroredNode(false, true, false, (*it).second);
			}
			else if ((it = uniqueNodesChecker.find(niZ)) != uniqueNodesChecker.end()) {
				correspondences[i] = MirroredNode(false, false, true, (*it).second);
			}
			else if ((it = uniqueNodesChecker.find(niXY)) != uniqueNodesChecker.end()) {
				correspondences[i] = MirroredNode(true, true, false, (*it).second);
			}
			else if ((it = uniqueNodesChecker.find(niXZ)) != uniqueNodesChecker.end()) {
				correspondences[i] = MirroredNode(true, false, true, (*it).second);
			}
			else if ((it = uniqueNodesChecker.find(niYZ)) != uniqueNodesChecker.end()) {
				correspondences[i] = MirroredNode(false, true, true, (*it).second);
			}
			else if ((it = uniqueNodesChecker.find(niXYZ)) != uniqueNodesChecker.end()) {
				correspondences[i] = MirroredNode(true, true, true, (*it).second);
			}
			else { // !found
				uniqueNodesChecker[n] = (id_t)uniqueNodes.size();
				correspondences[i] = MirroredNode(false, false, false, id_t(uniqueNodes.size()));
				uniqueNodes.push_back(n);
			}
		}
		_data[lev].clear();
		_data[lev] = uniqueNodes;
		_data[lev].shrink_to_fit();
		_nNodes += _data[lev].size();

		for (id_t i = 0; i < _data[lev - 1].size(); i++) {
			Node &n = _data[lev - 1][i];
			for (id_t j = 0; j < 8; j++) {
				if (n.existsChildPointer(j)) {
					MirroredNode mn = correspondences[n.children[j]];
					n.children[j] = mn.id;
					if (mn.mirrorX) n.setChildMirrrorBit(0, j);
					if (mn.mirrorY) n.setChildMirrrorBit(1, j);
					if (mn.mirrorZ) n.setChildMirrrorBit(2, j);
				}
			}
		}
	}

	if (!internalCall) _stats.toSDAGTime = _clock.elapsed();
	_stats.nNodesSDAG = _nNodes;
	_state = S_SDAG;
	
#if 0 // DEGUG HOW MUCH MIRRORED NODES THERE ARE
	for (int lev = 0; lev < _maxLevel; ++lev) {
		int sCount = 0;
		for (int i = 0; i < _svoLevelsHDD[lev]->size(); ++i) {
			BuildNode bn = _svoLevelsHDD[lev]->item(i);
			if (bn.mirroredX != 0 || bn.mirroredY != 0 || bn.mirroredZ != 0) sCount++;
		}
		printf("LEVEL %d :\t %d nodes with some symm flag\n", lev, sCount);
	}
#endif
	if(!internalCall) printf("OK! [%s]\n", vox::human_readable_duration(_stats.toSDAGTime).c_str());
}

void GeomOctree::toSSDAG(bool internalCall)
{
	if (!internalCall) {
		if (_state != S_SDAG) {
			printf("ERROR! This is not a SDAG!\n");
			return;
		}
		else {
			printf("* Transforming SDAG -> SSDAG ... "); fflush(stdout);
		}
	}

	for (unsigned int lev = _levels - 1; lev > 0; lev--) {
		
	}

}


void GeomOctree::toSDAGCanonical() {

	printf("* Transforming DAG -> SDAG (canonical)... "); fflush(stdout);

	struct MirroredNode {
		MirroredNode() : mirrorX(false), mirrorY(false), mirrorZ(false), id(0) {}
		MirroredNode(bool mX, bool mY, bool mZ, id_t id) :
			mirrorX(mX), mirrorY(mY), mirrorZ(mZ), id(id) {}
		bool mirrorX, mirrorY, mirrorZ;
		id_t id;
	};

	std::vector<MirroredNode> correspondences;
	std::map<Node, id_t> uniqueNodesChecker;
	std::vector<Node> uniqueNodes;

	_nNodes = 0;

	_clock.restart();

	for (unsigned int lev = _levels - 1; lev > 0; lev--) {
		unsigned int oldLevSize = (unsigned int)_data[lev].size();
		uniqueNodes.clear();
		uniqueNodesChecker.clear();
		correspondences.resize(oldLevSize);

		for (id_t i = 0; i < _data[lev].size(); i++) {

			Node n = _data[lev][i];
			if (!n.hasChildren()) continue; // skip empty nodes

			// Set invariances
			if (n == n.mirror(true, false, false)) n.setInvariantBit(0);
			if (n == n.mirror(false, true, false)) n.setInvariantBit(1);
			if (n == n.mirror(false, false, true)) n.setInvariantBit(2);

			bool cx, cy, cz;
			Node nC = n.getCanonical(cx, cy, cz);

			// Check children invariances
			if (lev < (_levels - 1)) invertInvs(nC, lev, cx, cy, cz);

			if (uniqueNodesChecker.find(nC) != uniqueNodesChecker.end()) {
				correspondences[i] = MirroredNode(cx, cy, cz, uniqueNodesChecker[nC]);
			} else { // !found
				uniqueNodesChecker[nC] = (id_t)uniqueNodes.size();
				correspondences[i] = MirroredNode(cx, cy, cz, id_t(uniqueNodes.size()));
				uniqueNodes.push_back(nC);
			}
		}
		_data[lev].clear();
		_data[lev] = uniqueNodes;
		_data[lev].shrink_to_fit();
		_nNodes += _data[lev].size();

		for (id_t i = 0; i < _data[lev - 1].size(); i++) {
			Node &n = _data[lev - 1][i];
			for (id_t j = 0; j < 8; j++) {
				if (n.existsChildPointer(j)) {
					MirroredNode mn = correspondences[n.children[j]];
					n.children[j] = mn.id;
					if (mn.mirrorX) n.setChildMirrrorBit(0, j);
					if (mn.mirrorY) n.setChildMirrrorBit(1, j);
					if (mn.mirrorZ) n.setChildMirrrorBit(2, j);
				}
			}
		}
	}

	_stats.toSDAGTime = _clock.elapsed();
	_stats.nNodesSDAG = _nNodes;
	_state = S_SDAG;

	printf("OK! [%s]\n", vox::human_readable_duration(_stats.toSDAGTime).c_str());
}


bool GeomOctree::checkIntegrity() {
	struct QueueItem {
		QueueItem(id_t id, std::uint8_t l) : nodeID(id), level(l) {}
		id_t nodeID;
		std::uint8_t level;
	};
	std::queue<QueueItem> queue;

	queue.push(QueueItem(0, 0));

	unsigned int nVisitedNodes = 0;

	while (!queue.empty()) {
		QueueItem qi = queue.front(); queue.pop();
		nVisitedNodes++;
		if (qi.level >= _levels) return false;
		if (qi.nodeID >= _data[qi.level].size()) return false;
		Node &n = _data[qi.level][qi.nodeID];
		if (!n.hasChildren()) return false;
		for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
			if (qi.level < _levels - 1) {
				if (n.existsChild(i) && !n.existsChildPointer(i)) return false;
				if (!n.existsChild(i) && n.existsChildPointer(i)) return false;
				if (n.existsChild(i)) queue.push(QueueItem(n.children[i], qi.level+1));
			}
		}
	}

	for (unsigned int i = 0; i < _levels; i++) {
		printf("%u\t%zu\n", i, _data[i].size());
	}

	printf("Visited %i nodes\n", nVisitedNodes);

	return true;
}

void GeomOctree::invertInvs(Node &n, int lev, bool inX, bool inY, bool inZ) {
	for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
		if (n.existsChildPointer(i)) {
			Node & nc = _data[lev + 1][n.children[i]];
			if (inX && nc.getInvariantBit(0)) n.unsetChildMirrrorBit(0, i);
			if (inY && nc.getInvariantBit(1)) n.unsetChildMirrrorBit(1, i);
			if (inZ && nc.getInvariantBit(2)) n.unsetChildMirrrorBit(2, i);
		}
	}
}

unsigned int GeomOctree::computeDifferentBitCount(unsigned char lhs, unsigned char rhs) {
	unsigned char xor_result = lhs ^ rhs;
	unsigned int different_bit_count = 0;

	for (unsigned int i = 0; i < 8 && different_bit_count < 2; ++i) {
		different_bit_count += (xor_result >> i) & 1;
	}
	return different_bit_count;
}

std::vector<float> GeomOctree::getLeavesHistogram() {
	std::vector<float> lh(256);
	for (unsigned int i = 0; i < _data[_levels - 2].size(); ++i) {
		for (int c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
			if (_data[_levels - 2][i].existsChildPointer(c)) {
				lh[_data[_levels - 1][_data[_levels - 2][i].children[c]].childrenBitmask] += 1.0f;
			}
		}
	}
	return lh;
}

void GeomOctree::replaceLeaves(const std::vector<std::uint8_t> & mapping)
{
	for (unsigned int i = 0; i < _data[_levels - 1].size(); ++i) {
		std::uint8_t & nodeId = _data[_levels - 1][i].childrenBitmask;
		nodeId = mapping[nodeId];
	}
}

size_t GeomOctree::getMemFootprint() {
	size_t mem = sizeof(Octree);
	for (int i = 0; i < _data.size(); ++i) {
		mem += _data[i].capacity()*sizeof(Node);
		mem += sizeof(std::vector<Node>);
	}
	return mem;
}

int GeomOctree::traverse(glm::vec3 p) const
{
	if (_state == S_EMPTY) return -1;

	if (!_bbox.contains(p)) return -1;

	if (_state == S_SVO || _state == S_DAG) {

		const Node *n = &_data[0][0];
		glm::vec3 nodeCenter = _bbox.center();
		unsigned int nodeLevel = 0;

		while (nodeLevel < _levels) {
			int selectedChild = 4 * int(p[0] > nodeCenter[0]) + 2 * int(p[1] > nodeCenter[1]) + int(p[2] > nodeCenter[2]);
			if (!n->existsChild(selectedChild)) break;
			nodeLevel++;
			float hs = getHalfSide(nodeLevel);
			nodeCenter[0] += (p[0] > nodeCenter[0]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
			nodeCenter[1] += (p[1] > nodeCenter[1]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
			nodeCenter[2] += (p[2] > nodeCenter[2]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
			if (nodeLevel < _levels) n = &_data[nodeLevel][ n->children[selectedChild] ];
		}
		return nodeLevel;
	}

	if (_state == S_SDAG) {

		const Node *n = &_data[0][0];
		glm::vec3 nodeCenter = _bbox.center();
		unsigned int nodeLevel = 0;
		bool mX = false, mY = false, mZ = false;
		
		while (nodeLevel < _levels) {
			if (mX) p[0] = 2.f * nodeCenter[0] - p[0];
			if (mY) p[1] = 2.f * nodeCenter[1] - p[1];
			if (mZ) p[2] = 2.f * nodeCenter[2] - p[2];
			int selectedChild = 4 * int(p[0] > nodeCenter[0]) + 2 * int(p[1] > nodeCenter[1]) + int(p[2] > nodeCenter[2]);
			if (!n->existsChild(selectedChild)) break;
			nodeLevel++;
			float hs = getHalfSide(nodeLevel);
			nodeCenter[0] += (p[0] > nodeCenter[0]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
			nodeCenter[1] += (p[1] > nodeCenter[1]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
			nodeCenter[2] += (p[2] > nodeCenter[2]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
			mX = n->getChildMirrrorBit(0, selectedChild);
			mY = n->getChildMirrrorBit(1, selectedChild);
			mZ = n->getChildMirrrorBit(2, selectedChild);

			if (nodeLevel < _levels) n = &_data[nodeLevel][n->children[selectedChild]];
		}
		return nodeLevel;
	}

	return -1;
}
```

### src/voxelator/geom_octree.hpp
```
#pragma once

#include <numeric>
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <map>
#include <vector>

#include <cstdint>
#include <common/aabb.hpp>
#include <common/timing.hpp>

#include <voxelator/scene.hpp>
#include <voxelator/octree.hpp>

class GeomOctree : public Octree {
	friend class MaterialOctree;

public:
	enum State {
		S_EMPTY,
		S_SVO,
		S_DAG,
		S_SDAG,
		S_SSDAG
	};

	struct Stats {
		Stats() :
			nTotalVoxels(0),
			nNodesSVO(0), nNodesDAG(0), nNodesSDAG(0),
			nNodesLastLevSVO(0), nNodesLastLevDAG(0), nNodesLastLevSDAG(0),
			simulatedEncodedSVOSize(0), memFootprint(0) {}
		size_t nTotalVoxels;
		size_t nNodesSVO, nNodesDAG, nNodesSDAG;
		size_t nNodesLastLevSVO, nNodesLastLevDAG, nNodesLastLevSDAG;
		size_t simulatedEncodedSVOSize;
		size_t memFootprint;
		vox::time_duration buildSVOTime, buildDAGTime, toDAGTime, toSDAGTime;

	};

public:  //////// Octree Node
	struct Node : public Octree::Node {

		std::uint8_t childrenMirroredBitmask[3];
		std::uint8_t invariantBitmask;

		// Constructor
		Node();

		// Operators
		virtual bool operator==(const Node& other) const;
		virtual bool operator<(const Node& other) const;

		inline bool getInvariantBit(int axis) const { return (invariantBitmask & (1U << axis)) != 0; }
		inline void setInvariantBit(int axis) { invariantBitmask |= (1U << axis); }
		inline void unsetInvariantBit(int axis) { invariantBitmask &= ~(1U << axis); }
		inline void invInvariantBit(int axis) { invariantBitmask ^= (1U << axis); }

		inline bool getChildMirrrorBit(int axis, int childId) const { return (childrenMirroredBitmask[axis] & (1U << childId)) != 0; }
		inline void setChildMirrrorBit(int axis, int childId) { childrenMirroredBitmask[axis] |= (1U << childId); }
		inline void unsetChildMirrrorBit(int axis, int childId) { childrenMirroredBitmask[axis] &= ~(1U << childId); }
		inline void invChildMirrrorBit(int axis, int childId) { childrenMirroredBitmask[axis] ^= (1U << childId); }

		Node mirror(bool x, bool y, bool z, bool applyToChildren = true) const;
		Node getCanonical(bool &x, bool &y, bool &z) const;
		virtual void print() const;
	};

	typedef std::vector< std::vector< Node > > NodeData;

private: ///////////// Private attributes
	NodeData _data;
	State _state;
	Stats _stats;
	vox::WallClock _clock;

public:
	GeomOctree(Scene * scene);
	GeomOctree(const GeomOctree &other);
	
	// Gettters & Setters
	inline void setScene(Scene * scene) { _scene = scene; }
	inline const NodeData& getNodeData() const { return _data; }
	inline Stats getStats() const { return _stats; }
	inline State getState()  const { return _state; }
	inline virtual void * getData() { return (void *)&_data[0][0]; }

	// Main methods
	void buildDAG(unsigned int levels, unsigned int stepLevel, vox::AABB3d bbox, bool verbose = false);
	void buildSVO(unsigned int levels, vox::AABB3d bbox, bool internalCall = false, std::vector< glm::dvec3 > * leavesCenters = NULL, bool putMaterialIdInLeaves = false);
	void toDAG(bool internalCall = false);
	void toDAG2(bool internalCall = false);
	void toSDAG(bool internalCall = false);
	void toSSDAG(bool internalCall = false);
	void toSDAGCanonical(); // not working? or slow?

	inline void clearLeavesPointers() {
		for (auto & n : _data[_levels - 1])
			for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i)
				n.children[i] = nullNode;
	}

	// External tools
	bool checkIntegrity();
	//std::map<size_t, size_t> getParentLinksHistogram(int level);
	size_t getMemFootprint();
	int traverse(glm::vec3 p) const;

	std::vector<float> getLeavesHistogram();
	void replaceLeaves(const std::vector<std::uint8_t> & mapping);

private: // Internal tools
	unsigned int cleanEmptyNodes();
	void invertInvs(Node &n, int lev, bool inX, bool inY, bool inZ);
	unsigned int computeDifferentBitCount(unsigned char lhs, unsigned char rhs);
	template <typename T>
	void sortIndexes(std::vector<id_t> &idx, const std::vector<T> &v) {
		idx.resize(v.size());
		std::iota(idx.begin(), idx.end(), 0);
		sort(idx.begin(), idx.end(), [&v](id_t i1, id_t i2) {return v[i1] < v[i2]; });
	}
};
```

### src/voxelator/leaf_clusterer.cpp
```
#include <voxelator/leaf_clusterer.hpp>
#include <cstdint>
#include <vector>

LeafClusterer::LeafClusterList LeafClusterer::clusterize(const std::vector<float> leavesHistogram, int targetNumClusters)
{
	const int N = (int)leavesHistogram.size();
	
	// Inizialize solution with histogram
	LeafClusterList lcl;
	for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
		LeafCluster lc;
		lc.add(LeafFreq(i, leavesHistogram[i]));
		lcl.push_back(lc);
	}

	while (lcl.size() > targetNumClusters) {
		int best_i=256, best_j=256;
		unsigned int best_cs = 1024;
		unsigned int best_hd = 9;
		float best_error = 1e30;
		bool found = false;
		for (int j = 0; j < lcl.size(); ++j) {
			const LeafCluster & lc_j = lcl[j];
			for (int i = 0; i < j; ++i) {
				const LeafCluster & lc_i = lcl[i];
				if (lc_i.size() == lc_j.size()) { // Equal clusters dimension
					if (lc_i.size() <= best_cs) {
						unsigned int hd = lc_i.hammingDistance(lc_j);
						if (hd <= best_hd || lc_i.size() < best_cs) { // Min Hamming Distance
							float error = (lc_i + lc_j).error(); // Error of clusters merged
							if (lc_i.size() < best_cs || hd < best_hd || error < best_error) {
								best_error = error;
								best_hd = hd;
								best_cs = lc_i.size();
								best_i = i;
								best_j = j;
								found = true;
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if (!found) {
			printf("ERROR\n");
		} else {
			lcl[best_i] = lcl[best_i] + lcl[best_j];
			lcl.erase(lcl.begin() + best_j);
		}
	}

	return lcl;
}

std::vector<std::uint8_t> LeafClusterer::getMappingToRepresentatives(const LeafClusterList & lcl)
{
	std::vector<std::uint8_t> leavesMapping(256);
	for (unsigned int i = 0; i < lcl.size(); ++i) {
		int bestIdx = -1;
		float bestFreq = -1;
		for (unsigned int j = 0; j < lcl[i].size(); ++j) {
			if (lcl[i].leafFreqVector[j].freq > bestFreq) {
				bestIdx = j;
				bestFreq = lcl[i].leafFreqVector[j].freq;
			}
		}
		for (unsigned int j = 0; j < lcl[i].size(); ++j) {
			leavesMapping[lcl[i].leafFreqVector[j].leafId] = lcl[i].leafFreqVector[bestIdx].leafId;
		}
	}
	return leavesMapping;
}
```

### src/voxelator/leaf_clusterer.hpp
```
#pragma once

#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <cstdint>
#include <vector>
#include <common/util.hpp>

class LeafClusterer {

public:
	struct LeafFreq {
		LeafFreq(std::uint8_t lid, float f) : leafId(lid), freq(f) {}
		std::uint8_t leafId;
		float freq;
	};
	
	struct LeafCluster {
		std::vector < LeafFreq > leafFreqVector;
		
		inline void add(const LeafFreq & lf) { leafFreqVector.push_back(lf); }

		inline unsigned int size() const { return (unsigned int)leafFreqVector.size(); }

		inline unsigned int hammingDistance(const LeafCluster & other) const {
			unsigned int maxBitDistance = 0;
			for (auto i : leafFreqVector) {
				for (auto j : other.leafFreqVector) {
					unsigned int d = bitDistance(i.leafId, j.leafId);
					if (d > maxBitDistance) maxBitDistance = d;
				}
			}
			return maxBitDistance;
		}

		inline LeafCluster operator +(const LeafCluster & other) const {
			LeafCluster new_lc;
			new_lc.leafFreqVector = leafFreqVector;
			new_lc.leafFreqVector.insert(new_lc.leafFreqVector.end(), other.leafFreqVector.begin(), other.leafFreqVector.end());
			return new_lc;
		}

		inline float error() {
			float error = 0.0f;
			float sumFreqs = 0;
			for (auto i : leafFreqVector) sumFreqs += i.freq;

			for (int j = 0; j < leafFreqVector.size(); ++j) {
				float innerErrorSum = 0;
				for (int i = 0; i < leafFreqVector.size(); ++i) {
					innerErrorSum += leafFreqVector[i].freq * bitDistance(leafFreqVector[i].leafId, leafFreqVector[j].leafId);
				}
				innerErrorSum *= leafFreqVector[j].freq;
				error += innerErrorSum;
			}
			error /= sumFreqs;
			return error;
		}
	};

	typedef std::vector<LeafCluster> LeafClusterList;
	
	static LeafClusterList clusterize(const std::vector<float> leavesHistogram, int targetNumClusters);
	static std::vector<std::uint8_t> getMappingToRepresentatives(const LeafClusterList & lcl);
};
```

### src/voxelator/material_octree.cpp
```
#include <voxelator/material_octree.hpp>
#include <glm/gtc/type_ptr.hpp>
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <common/format.hpp>
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>
#include <common/util.hpp>
#include <stack>


MaterialOctree::matId_t MaterialOctree::nullMat = std::numeric_limits<MaterialOctree::matId_t>::max();

void MaterialOctree::build(const GeomOctree & go) {
	_levels = go._levels;
	_data.resize(_levels);

	std::vector<Node> tmpLev1;
	std::vector<Node> tmpLev2;

	for (size_t i = 0; i < go._data[_levels-1].size(); ++i) {
		Node node;
		matId_t mat = nullMat;
		bool moreThanOneMat = false;
		for (int c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
			if (go._data[_levels - 1][i].existsChild(c)) {
				matId_t tmpMat = go._data[_levels - 1][i].children[c] != nullNode ? go._data[_levels - 1][i].children[c] : nullMat;
				if (mat == MaterialOctree::nullMat) mat = tmpMat;
				else if (mat != tmpMat) {
					moreThanOneMat = true;
					break;
				}
			}
		}
		if (!moreThanOneMat) {
			node.material = mat;
		} else {
			node.material = nullMat;
			for (int c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
				if (go._data[_levels - 1][i].existsChild(c)) {
					node.setChildBit(c);
					node.children[c] = go._data[_levels - 1][i].children[c] != nullNode ? go._data[_levels - 1][i].children[c] : nullMat;
				}
			}
		}
		tmpLev2.push_back(node);
	}


	for(int lev = _levels-2; lev>=0; --lev) {
		tmpLev1.swap(tmpLev2);
		for (size_t i = 0; i < go._data[lev].size(); ++i) {
			matId_t mat = nullMat;
			bool matSet = false;
			bool moreThanOneMat = false;
			for (int c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
				if (go._data[lev][i].existsChild(c)) {
					matId_t tmpMat = tmpLev1[go._data[lev][i].children[c]].material;
					if (!matSet) {
						mat = tmpMat;
						matSet = true;
					} else if (mat != tmpMat) {
						moreThanOneMat = true;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			Node node(mat);
			if (moreThanOneMat || mat == nullMat) {
				node.material = nullMat;
				for (int c = 0; c < 8; ++c) {
					if (go._data[lev][i].existsChild(c)) {
						node.setChildBit(c);
						node.children[c] = (id_t)_data[lev + 1].size();
						_data[lev + 1].push_back(tmpLev1[go._data[lev][i].children[c]]);
						_nNodes++;
					}
				}
			}
			tmpLev2.push_back(node);
		}
		tmpLev1.clear();
	}

	_data[0].push_back(tmpLev2[0]);
}

void MaterialOctree::setSceneParams(Scene & scene)
{
	_bbox = scene.getAABB();
	glm::vec3 sides = _bbox.half_side_lengths() * 2.0f;
	_rootSide = std::max(std::max(sides[0], sides[1]), sides[2]);
	for (int i = 0; i < scene.getMaterials()->size(); ++i) {
		_materials.push_back(scene.getMaterials()->at(i).diffuseColor);
	}
}

void MaterialOctree::setLeafChild(id_t leafId, int child, matId_t matId) {
	if (leafId >= _data[_levels -1].size()) _data[_levels - 1].resize(leafId + 1);
	_data[_levels - 1][leafId].children[child] = matId;
}

void MaterialOctree::print() {
	for (int l = 0; l < _data.size(); ++l) {
		for (int n = 0; n < _data[l].size(); ++n) {
			_data[l][n].print(l, n);
		}
	}
}


void MaterialOctree::prune() {

	_nNodes = 0;
	std::vector<id_t> idxMap;
	for (int lev = _levels - 1; lev > 0; --lev) {
		idxMap.clear();
		idxMap.reserve(_data[lev].size());
		id_t count = 0;
		for (int nid = 0; nid < _data[lev].size(); ++nid) {
			if (!_data[lev][nid].isEmpty()) {
				_data[lev][count] = _data[lev][nid];
				idxMap[nid] = count;
				count++;
			}
		}
		_data[lev].resize(count);
		_data[lev].shrink_to_fit();
		_nNodes += count;
		for (int nid = 0; nid < _data[lev-1].size(); ++nid) {
			for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
				if (_data[lev - 1][nid].children[i] != nullNode)
					_data[lev - 1][nid].children[i] = idxMap[_data[lev - 1][nid].children[i]];
			}
		}
	}
}

bool MaterialOctree::load(std::string filename)
{
	printf("* Loading Material Octree '%s'... ", filename.c_str()); fflush(stdout);

	std::ifstream ifs;
	ifs.open(filename, std::ios::in | std::ios::binary);
	if (!ifs.is_open()) {
		printf("FAILED!!!\n");
		return false;
	}

	_data.clear();

	ifs.read((char *)glm::value_ptr(_bbox[0]), 12);
	ifs.read((char *)glm::value_ptr(_bbox[1]), 12);
	ifs.read((char *)&_levels, 4);
	ifs.read((char *)&_nNodes, 8);
	_data.resize(_levels);
	for (unsigned int lev = 0; lev < _levels; ++lev) {
		id_t nElems;
		ifs.read((char *)&nElems, sizeof(id_t));
		for (id_t i = 0; i < nElems; ++i) {
			Node node;
			ifs.read((char *)(&node), sizeof(Node));
			_data[lev].push_back(node);
		}
	}
	unsigned int nMaterials;
	ifs.read((char *)&nMaterials, sizeof(4));
	for (unsigned int i = 0; i < nMaterials; ++i) {
		glm::vec3 color;
		ifs.read((char *)glm::value_ptr(color), 12);
		_materials.push_back(color);
	}

	glm::vec3 sides = _bbox.half_side_lengths() * 2.0f;
	_rootSide = std::max(std::max(sides[0], sides[1]), sides[2]);
		
	printf("OK!\n");

	return true;
}


bool MaterialOctree::save(std::string filename)
{
	printf("* Saving Material Octree '%s'... ", filename.c_str()); fflush(stdout);

	std::ofstream ofs;
	ofs.open(filename, std::ios::out | std::ios::binary);
	if (!ofs.is_open()) {
		printf("FAILED!!!\n");
		return false;
	}

	ofs.write((char *)glm::value_ptr(_bbox[0]), 12);
	ofs.write((char *)glm::value_ptr(_bbox[1]), 12);
	ofs.write((char *)&_levels, 4);
	ofs.write((char *)&_nNodes, 8);
	for (unsigned int lev = 0; lev < _levels; ++lev) {
		id_t nElems = (id_t) _data[lev].size();
		ofs.write((char *)&nElems, sizeof(id_t));
		for (const auto & n : _data[lev])
			ofs.write((char *)&n, sizeof(Node));
	}
	unsigned int nMaterials = (unsigned int)_materials.size();
	ofs.write((char *)&nMaterials, sizeof(4));
	for (auto mat : _materials)
		ofs.write((char *)glm::value_ptr(mat), 12);

	ofs.close();

	printf("OK!\n");

	return true;
}

MaterialOctree::matId_t MaterialOctree::traverse(glm::vec3 p)
{ 
	const Node *n = &_data[0][0];
	glm::vec3 nodeCenter = _bbox.center();
	unsigned int nodeLevel = 0;
	id_t lastNodeID = 0;
	//printf("TRAVERSE1: ");
	while (1) {
		//printf("[%u,%u] ", nodeLevel, lastNodeID);
		if (n->material != nullMat) {
			//printf("[INNER Mat:%i]\n", n->material);
			return n->material;
		}
		int selectedChild = 4 * int(p[0] > nodeCenter[0]) + 2 * int(p[1] > nodeCenter[1]) + int(p[2] > nodeCenter[2]);
		//printf("[%i] ", selectedChild);
		nodeLevel++;
		float hs = getHalfSide(nodeLevel);
		nodeCenter[0] += (p[0] > nodeCenter[0]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
		nodeCenter[1] += (p[1] > nodeCenter[1]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
		nodeCenter[2] += (p[2] > nodeCenter[2]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
		if (nodeLevel < _levels) {
			if (!n->existsChild(selectedChild)) {
				printf("WARNING MaterialOctree::traverse(): This shouldn't happen\n");
				return nullMat;
			}
			lastNodeID = n->children[selectedChild];
			n = &_data[nodeLevel][n->children[selectedChild]];
		}
		else {
			//printf("[LEAF Mat:%i]\n", n->children[selectedChild]);
			return n->children[selectedChild];
		}
	}

	return nullMat;
}

void MaterialOctree::Node::print(unsigned int level, id_t nodeID) const {
	if (isEmpty()) printf("EMPTY!");
	else {
		printf("L%2i\tID %4i\tMat %2i", level, nodeID, material);
		if (!isLeaf()) {
			printf("INNER\t");
			for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++)
				printf("\t[%s]", children[i] != nullNode ? std::to_string(children[i]).c_str() : "-");
		}
		else {
			printf("LEAF\t Mat %2i", material);
			for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++)
				printf("\t[%s]", children[i] != nullNode ? std::to_string(children[i]).c_str() : "-");
		}
	}
	printf("\n");

}

void MaterialOctree::encode() {
	printf("* Encoding Material Octree...      "); fflush(stdout);

	struct EncodeTravNode {
		EncodeTravNode(std::uint8_t level, id_t buildNodeId, std::uint32_t encodedNodeId) :
			level(level), buildNodeId(buildNodeId), encodedNodeId(encodedNodeId) {}
		std::uint8_t level;
		GeomOctree::id_t buildNodeId;
		std::uint32_t encodedNodeId;
	};

	typedef std::stack<EncodeTravNode> EncodeNodeStack;

	_encodedData.reserve(_nNodes);
	_encodedData.resize(1);

	EncodeNodeStack stack;

	stack.push(EncodeTravNode(0, 0, 0));

	_clock.restart();

	while (!stack.empty()) {

		EncodeTravNode travNode = stack.top(); stack.pop();
		const Node &node = _data[travNode.level][travNode.buildNodeId];
		if (travNode.level < (_levels - 1)) {
			_encodedData[travNode.encodedNodeId].x = (std::uint32_t(node.material) << 16) | std::uint32_t(node.childrenBitmask);
			_encodedData[travNode.encodedNodeId].y = node.material == nullMat ? std::uint32_t(_encodedData.size()) : nullNode;
			if (node.material == nullMat && !node.hasChildren()) printf(" *** INCOGRUENCIA\n");
			for (int c = 7; c >= 0; --c) {
				if (node.existsChild(c)) {
					stack.push(EncodeTravNode(travNode.level + 1, node.children[c], (std::uint32_t)_encodedData.size()));
					_encodedData.resize(_encodedData.size() + 1);
				}
			}
		}
		else {
			_encodedData[travNode.encodedNodeId].x = (std::uint32_t(node.material) << 16) | std::uint32_t(node.childrenBitmask);
			if (node.material != nullMat) {
			} else {
				std::uint32_t leafPtr = (std::uint32_t)_encodedData.size();
				_encodedData.resize(_encodedData.size() + 1);
				_encodedData[travNode.encodedNodeId].y = leafPtr;
				_encodedData[leafPtr].x =
					(((node.children[3] == nullNode) ? nullMat : node.children[3]) & 0xFF) << 24 |
					(((node.children[2] == nullNode) ? nullMat : node.children[2]) & 0xFF) << 16 |
					(((node.children[1] == nullNode) ? nullMat : node.children[1]) & 0xFF) <<  8 |
					(((node.children[0] == nullNode) ? nullMat : node.children[0]) & 0xFF);

				_encodedData[leafPtr].y =
					(((node.children[7] == nullNode) ? nullMat : node.children[7]) & 0xFF) << 24 |
					(((node.children[6] == nullNode) ? nullMat : node.children[6]) & 0xFF) << 16 |
					(((node.children[5] == nullNode) ? nullMat : node.children[5]) & 0xFF) << 8 |
					(((node.children[4] == nullNode) ? nullMat : node.children[4]) & 0xFF);
			}
		}
	}

	printf("\b\b\b\b\b OK! [%s]    \n", vox::human_readable_duration(_clock.elapsed()).c_str());
	printf("\t- Encoded Material Octree Size: %s\n", vox::human_readable_size(_encodedData.size() * 4).c_str());
}

MaterialOctree::matId_t MaterialOctree::traverseEncoded(glm::vec3 p) {

	std::uint32_t nodeIdx = 0;
	glm::vec3 nodeCenter = _bbox.center();
	unsigned int nodeLevel = 0;
	//printf("TRAVERSE2: ");
	while (1) {
		//printf("[%u,%u] ", nodeLevel, ptr);
		nodeLevel++;
		const EncodedNode & node = _encodedData[nodeIdx];
		matId_t nodeMat = (node.x >> 16) & 0xFF;
		if (nodeLevel <= _levels && nodeMat != nullMat) {
			//printf("[INNER Mat:%i]\n", mat);
			return nodeMat;
		}
		int selectedChild = 4 * int(p[0] > nodeCenter[0]) + 2 * int(p[1] > nodeCenter[1]) + int(p[2] > nodeCenter[2]);
		//printf("[%i] ", selectedChild);
		if (nodeLevel < _levels) {
			float hs = getHalfSide(nodeLevel);
			nodeCenter[0] += (p[0] > nodeCenter[0]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
			nodeCenter[1] += (p[1] > nodeCenter[1]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
			nodeCenter[2] += (p[2] > nodeCenter[2]) ? hs : -1.f * hs;
			std::uint8_t nodeMask = std::uint8_t(node.x & 0xFF);
			if ((nodeMask & std::uint8_t(1<<selectedChild)) == 0) {
				printf("WARNING MaterialOctree::traverse(): This shouldn't happen\n");
				return nullMat;
			}
			nodeIdx = node.y + (bitCount(nodeMask >> selectedChild) - 1);
		}
		else {
			EncodedNode leaf = _encodedData[node.y];
			//printf(" %s ", byteToString(_encodedData[ptr + int(selectedChild / 4)]).c_str());
			id_t mat = (((selectedChild/4 == 0) ? leaf.x : leaf.y) >> (8 * (selectedChild % 4))) & 0xFF;
			//printf("[LEAF Mat:%i]\n", mat);
			return mat;
		}
	}

	return nullMat;
}

void MaterialOctree::printEncodedData() {
	printf("-----\n");
	for (size_t i = 0; i < _encodedData.size(); i++) {
		std::uint32_t k = _encodedData[i].x;
		printf("%zu\t%s\t[%3u]\t[%3u]\t[%3u]\t[%3u]\t(%10u)\n", 
			i,
			byteToString(k).c_str(), 
			(k >> 24) & 0xFF,
			(k >> 16) & 0xFF,
			(k >> 8 ) & 0xFF,
			(k >> 0 ) & 0xFF,
			k);
		if (i % 2 != 0) printf("\n");
	}
}
```

### src/voxelator/material_octree.hpp
```
#pragma once

#include <vector>
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <common/aabb.hpp>
#include <common/timing.hpp>
#include <voxelator/scene.hpp>
#include <voxelator/octree.hpp>
#include <voxelator/geom_octree.hpp>

class MaterialOctree : public Octree {
	
public:  //////// Definitions
	typedef std::uint8_t matId_t;
	static matId_t nullMat;

public:  //////// MaterialOctree Node
	struct Node : public Octree::Node {

		// Node's members
		matId_t material;

		// Constructor
		Node(matId_t matId = nullMat) : Octree::Node() {
			material = matId;
		}
		inline bool isLeaf() const {
			if (children[0] == nullNode && children[1] == nullNode &&
				children[2] == nullNode && children[3] == nullNode &&
				children[4] == nullNode && children[5] == nullNode &&
				children[6] == nullNode && children[7] == nullNode) return true;
			return false;
		}
		inline bool isEmpty() const {
			return (material == nullMat && isLeaf());
		}
		void print(unsigned int level, id_t nodeID) const;
		virtual void print() const {}
	};

	typedef std::vector< std::vector< Node > > NodeData;


private: ///////////// Private attributes
	NodeData _data;
	std::vector<glm::vec3> _materials;
	vox::WallClock _clock;
	struct EncodedNode {
		std::uint32_t x, y;
	};
	std::vector<EncodedNode> _encodedData;

public:

	inline const NodeData& getNodeData() const { return _data; }
	void build(const GeomOctree & go);

	// Gettters & Setters
	void setSceneParams(Scene &scene);
	void setLeafChild(id_t leafId, int child, matId_t mat);
	inline size_t getNLeaves() { return _data[_levels-1].size(); }
	inline std::vector<glm::vec3> * getMaterials() { return &_materials; }

	void print();
	void prune();
	bool save(std::string filename);
	bool load(std::string filename);
	matId_t traverse(glm::vec3 p);
	inline glm::vec3 getMaterial(matId_t mid) { 
		return mid<_materials.size() ? _materials[mid] : glm::vec3(0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f);
	}
	void encode();
	void printEncodedData();
	matId_t traverseEncoded(glm::vec3 p);
	inline size_t getEncodedDataSize() { return _encodedData.size() * sizeof(EncodedNode); }
	inline void * getEncodedData() { return (void *)_encodedData.data(); }
};
```

```
#include <voxelator/octree.hpp>

Octree::id_t Octree::nullNode = std::numeric_limits<id_t>::max() - 1;

Octree::Octree(Scene * scene) : _scene(scene) {
	_nNodes = _nVoxels = _nLeaves = 0;
	_levels = 0;
	_rootSide = 0;
	_bbox.to_empty();
}

Octree::Octree(const Octree &other) {
	_bbox = other._bbox;
	_rootSide = other._rootSide;
	_levels = other._levels;
	_scene = other._scene;
	_nVoxels = other._nVoxels;
	_nNodes = other._nNodes;
	_nLeaves = other._nLeaves;
}
```

### src/voxelator/octree.hpp
```
#pragma once

#include <voxelator/scene.hpp>
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <common/aabb.hpp>
#include <cstdint>

class Octree {

public:  //////// Definitions
	typedef std::uint32_t id_t;
	typedef glm::uvec3 VoxelCoords;

	// null node definition
	static id_t nullNode;

	enum ChildrenIdx {
		PXPYPZ = 7,
		PXPYNZ = 6,
		PXNYPZ = 5,
		PXNYNZ = 4,
		NXPYPZ = 3,
		NXPYNZ = 2,
		NXNYPZ = 1,
		NXNYNZ = 0
	};

public:  //////// Octree Node
	struct Node {

		// Node's attributes
		id_t children[8];
		std::uint8_t childrenBitmask;

		// Constructor
		Node() {
			childrenBitmask = 0;
			children[0] = children[1] = children[2] = children[3] =
				children[4] = children[5] = children[6] = children[7] = nullNode;
		};

		// Methods
		inline bool existsChild(int childId) const { return (childrenBitmask & (1U << childId)) != 0; }
		inline bool existsChildPointer(int childId) const { return (children[childId] != nullNode); }
		inline bool hasChildren() const { return (childrenBitmask != 0); }
		inline unsigned int getNChildren() const { return (childrenBitmask * 01001001001ULL & 042104210421ULL) % 017; }

		inline void setChildBit(int childId) { childrenBitmask |= (1U << childId); }
		inline void unsetChildBit(int childId) { childrenBitmask &= ~(1U << childId); }

		virtual void print() const = 0;
	};

protected: ///////////// Private attributes
	vox::AABB3f _bbox;
	double _rootSide;
	unsigned int _levels;
	Scene * _scene;
	size_t _nVoxels, _nNodes, _nLeaves;
public:
	// Constructrs
	Octree(Scene * scene = NULL);
	Octree(const Octree &other);
	virtual ~Octree() {};

	// Gettters & Setters
	inline void setScene(Scene * scene) { _scene = scene; }
	inline size_t getNNodes() const { return _nNodes; }
	inline size_t getNVoxels() const { return _nVoxels; }
	inline vox::AABB3f getSceneBBox() const { return _bbox; }
	inline double getRootSideD() const { return _rootSide; }
	inline float getRootSide() const { return float(_rootSide); }
	inline unsigned int getLevels() const { return _levels; }

	inline float getHalfSide(const unsigned int level) const { return float(_rootSide / double(1U << (level + 1))); }
	inline double getHalfSideD(const unsigned int level) const { return double(_rootSide) / double(1U << (level + 1)); }

	//inline VoxelCoord getVoxelCoord(glm::vec3 point) {
	//	glm::vec3 k = float(1<<_levels) * ((point - _bbox[0]) / float(_rootSide));
	//	return VoxelCoord(std::uint32_t(k[0]), std::uint32_t(k[1]), std::uint32_t(k[2]));
	//}

};
```

### src/voxelator/octree_node.cpp
```
#include <voxelator/geom_octree.hpp>

GeomOctree::Node::Node()
{
	children[0] = nullNode;
	children[1] = nullNode;
	children[2] = nullNode;
	children[3] = nullNode;
	children[4] = nullNode;
	children[5] = nullNode;
	children[6] = nullNode;
	children[7] = nullNode;
	childrenBitmask = 0;
	childrenMirroredBitmask[0] = 0;
	childrenMirroredBitmask[1] = 0;
	childrenMirroredBitmask[2] = 0;
	invariantBitmask = 0;
}

bool GeomOctree::Node::operator==(const GeomOctree::Node& other) const {
	if (childrenBitmask != other.childrenBitmask) return false;
	if (children[0] != other.children[0]) return false;
	if (children[1] != other.children[1]) return false;
	if (children[2] != other.children[2]) return false;
	if (children[3] != other.children[3]) return false;
	if (children[4] != other.children[4]) return false;
	if (children[5] != other.children[5]) return false;
	if (children[6] != other.children[6]) return false;
	if (children[7] != other.children[7]) return false;
	if (childrenMirroredBitmask[0] != other.childrenMirroredBitmask[0]) return false;
	if (childrenMirroredBitmask[1] != other.childrenMirroredBitmask[1]) return false;
	if (childrenMirroredBitmask[2] != other.childrenMirroredBitmask[2]) return false;
	//if (invariantBitmask != other.invariantBitmask) return false;
	return true;
}

bool GeomOctree::Node::operator<(const GeomOctree::Node& other) const {
	
	if (childrenBitmask < other.childrenBitmask) return true;
	else if (childrenBitmask > other.childrenBitmask) return false;
	
	if (children[0] < other.children[0]) return true;
	else if (children[0] > other.children[0]) return false;
	if (children[1] < other.children[1]) return true;
	else if (children[1] > other.children[1]) return false;
	if (children[2] < other.children[2]) return true;
	else if (children[2] > other.children[2]) return false;
	if (children[3] < other.children[3]) return true;
	else if (children[3] > other.children[3]) return false;
	if (children[4] < other.children[4]) return true;
	else if (children[4] > other.children[4]) return false;
	if (children[5] < other.children[5]) return true;
	else if (children[5] > other.children[5]) return false;
	if (children[6] < other.children[6]) return true;
	else if (children[6] > other.children[6]) return false;
	if (children[7] < other.children[7]) return true;
	else if (children[7] > other.children[7]) return false;

	if (childrenMirroredBitmask[0] < other.childrenMirroredBitmask[0]) return true;
	else if (childrenMirroredBitmask[0] > other.childrenMirroredBitmask[0]) return false;
	if (childrenMirroredBitmask[1] < other.childrenMirroredBitmask[1]) return true;
	else if (childrenMirroredBitmask[1] > other.childrenMirroredBitmask[1]) return false;
	if (childrenMirroredBitmask[2] < other.childrenMirroredBitmask[2]) return true;
	else if (childrenMirroredBitmask[2] > other.childrenMirroredBitmask[2]) return false;

	return false;
}

GeomOctree::Node GeomOctree::Node::mirror(bool x, bool y, bool z, bool applyToChildren) const {
	
	Node n = *this;
	if (x) {
		Node n2;
		if (n.childrenBitmask & (1 << PXPYPZ)) n2.childrenBitmask |= 1U << NXPYPZ;
		if (n.childrenBitmask & (1 << PXPYNZ)) n2.childrenBitmask |= 1U << NXPYNZ;
		if (n.childrenBitmask & (1 << PXNYPZ)) n2.childrenBitmask |= 1U << NXNYPZ;
		if (n.childrenBitmask & (1 << PXNYNZ)) n2.childrenBitmask |= 1U << NXNYNZ;
		if (n.childrenBitmask & (1 << NXPYPZ)) n2.childrenBitmask |= 1U << PXPYPZ;
		if (n.childrenBitmask & (1 << NXPYNZ)) n2.childrenBitmask |= 1U << PXPYNZ;
		if (n.childrenBitmask & (1 << NXNYPZ)) n2.childrenBitmask |= 1U << PXNYPZ;
		if (n.childrenBitmask & (1 << NXNYNZ)) n2.childrenBitmask |= 1U << PXNYNZ;
		for (unsigned int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
			if (n.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] & (1U << PXPYPZ)) n2.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] |= 1U << NXPYPZ;
			if (n.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] & (1U << PXPYNZ)) n2.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] |= 1U << NXPYNZ;
			if (n.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] & (1U << PXNYPZ)) n2.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] |= 1U << NXNYPZ;
			if (n.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] & (1U << PXNYNZ)) n2.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] |= 1U << NXNYNZ;
			if (n.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] & (1U << NXPYPZ)) n2.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] |= 1U << PXPYPZ;
			if (n.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] & (1U << NXPYNZ)) n2.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] |= 1U << PXPYNZ;
			if (n.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] & (1U << NXNYPZ)) n2.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] |= 1U << PXNYPZ;
			if (n.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] & (1U << NXNYNZ)) n2.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] |= 1U << PXNYNZ;
		}
		n2.children[PXPYPZ] = n.children[NXPYPZ];
		n2.children[PXPYNZ] = n.children[NXPYNZ];
		n2.children[PXNYPZ] = n.children[NXNYPZ];
		n2.children[PXNYNZ] = n.children[NXNYNZ];
		n2.children[NXPYPZ] = n.children[PXPYPZ];
		n2.children[NXPYNZ] = n.children[PXPYNZ];
		n2.children[NXNYPZ] = n.children[PXNYPZ];
		n2.children[NXNYNZ] = n.children[PXNYNZ];
		n = n2;
	}

	if (y) {
		Node n2;
		if (n.childrenBitmask & (1 << PXPYPZ)) n2.childrenBitmask |= 1U << PXNYPZ;
		if (n.childrenBitmask & (1 << PXPYNZ)) n2.childrenBitmask |= 1U << PXNYNZ;
		if (n.childrenBitmask & (1 << PXNYPZ)) n2.childrenBitmask |= 1U << PXPYPZ;
		if (n.childrenBitmask & (1 << PXNYNZ)) n2.childrenBitmask |= 1U << PXPYNZ;
		if (n.childrenBitmask & (1 << NXPYPZ)) n2.childrenBitmask |= 1U << NXNYPZ;
		if (n.childrenBitmask & (1 << NXPYNZ)) n2.childrenBitmask |= 1U << NXNYNZ;
		if (n.childrenBitmask & (1 << NXNYPZ)) n2.childrenBitmask |= 1U << NXPYPZ;
		if (n.childrenBitmask & (1 << NXNYNZ)) n2.childrenBitmask |= 1U << NXPYNZ;
		for (unsigned int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
			if (n.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] & (1U << PXPYPZ)) n2.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] |= 1U << PXNYPZ;
			if (n.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] & (1U << PXPYNZ)) n2.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] |= 1U << PXNYNZ;
			if (n.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] & (1U << PXNYPZ)) n2.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] |= 1U << PXPYPZ;
			if (n.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] & (1U << PXNYNZ)) n2.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] |= 1U << PXPYNZ;
			if (n.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] & (1U << NXPYPZ)) n2.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] |= 1U << NXNYPZ;
			if (n.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] & (1U << NXPYNZ)) n2.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] |= 1U << NXNYNZ;
			if (n.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] & (1U << NXNYPZ)) n2.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] |= 1U << NXPYPZ;
			if (n.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] & (1U << NXNYNZ)) n2.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] |= 1U << NXPYNZ;
		}
		n2.children[PXPYPZ] = n.children[PXNYPZ];
		n2.children[PXPYNZ] = n.children[PXNYNZ];
		n2.children[PXNYPZ] = n.children[PXPYPZ];
		n2.children[PXNYNZ] = n.children[PXPYNZ];
		n2.children[NXPYPZ] = n.children[NXNYPZ];
		n2.children[NXPYNZ] = n.children[NXNYNZ];
		n2.children[NXNYPZ] = n.children[NXPYPZ];
		n2.children[NXNYNZ] = n.children[NXPYNZ];
		n = n2;
	}

	if (z) {
		Node n2;
		if (n.childrenBitmask & (1 << PXPYPZ)) n2.childrenBitmask |= 1U << PXPYNZ;
		if (n.childrenBitmask & (1 << PXPYNZ)) n2.childrenBitmask |= 1U << PXPYPZ;
		if (n.childrenBitmask & (1 << PXNYPZ)) n2.childrenBitmask |= 1U << PXNYNZ;
		if (n.childrenBitmask & (1 << PXNYNZ)) n2.childrenBitmask |= 1U << PXNYPZ;
		if (n.childrenBitmask & (1 << NXPYPZ)) n2.childrenBitmask |= 1U << NXPYNZ;
		if (n.childrenBitmask & (1 << NXPYNZ)) n2.childrenBitmask |= 1U << NXPYPZ;
		if (n.childrenBitmask & (1 << NXNYPZ)) n2.childrenBitmask |= 1U << NXNYNZ;
		if (n.childrenBitmask & (1 << NXNYNZ)) n2.childrenBitmask |= 1U << NXNYPZ;
		for (unsigned int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
			if (n.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] & (1U << PXPYPZ)) n2.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] |= 1U << PXPYNZ;
			if (n.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] & (1U << PXPYNZ)) n2.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] |= 1U << PXPYPZ;
			if (n.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] & (1U << PXNYPZ)) n2.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] |= 1U << PXNYNZ;
			if (n.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] & (1U << PXNYNZ)) n2.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] |= 1U << PXNYPZ;
			if (n.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] & (1U << NXPYPZ)) n2.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] |= 1U << NXPYNZ;
			if (n.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] & (1U << NXPYNZ)) n2.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] |= 1U << NXPYPZ;
			if (n.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] & (1U << NXNYPZ)) n2.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] |= 1U << NXNYNZ;
			if (n.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] & (1U << NXNYNZ)) n2.childrenMirroredBitmask[i] |= 1U << NXNYPZ;
		}
		n2.children[PXPYPZ] = n.children[PXPYNZ];
		n2.children[PXPYNZ] = n.children[PXPYPZ];
		n2.children[PXNYPZ] = n.children[PXNYNZ];
		n2.children[PXNYNZ] = n.children[PXNYPZ];
		n2.children[NXPYPZ] = n.children[NXPYNZ];
		n2.children[NXPYNZ] = n.children[NXPYPZ];
		n2.children[NXNYPZ] = n.children[NXNYNZ];
		n2.children[NXNYNZ] = n.children[NXNYPZ];
		n = n2;
	}

	if (applyToChildren) {
		for (unsigned int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
			if (n.existsChildPointer(i)) {
				if (x) n.invChildMirrrorBit(0,i);
				if (y) n.invChildMirrrorBit(1,i);
				if (z) n.invChildMirrrorBit(2,i);
			}
		}
	}

	return n;
}

GeomOctree::Node GeomOctree::Node::getCanonical(bool &x, bool &y, bool &z) const {

	Node nc = *this, ntmp;
	x = false; y = false; z = false;

	// X
	ntmp = mirror(true, false, false);
	if (ntmp.childrenBitmask < nc.childrenBitmask) {
		nc = ntmp;
		x = true; y = false; z = false;
	}

	// Y
	ntmp = mirror(false, true, false);
	if (ntmp.childrenBitmask < nc.childrenBitmask) {
		nc = ntmp;
		x = false; y = true; z = false;
	}

	// Z
	ntmp = mirror(false, false, true);
	if (ntmp.childrenBitmask < nc.childrenBitmask) {
		nc = ntmp;
		x = false; y = false; z = true;
	}

	// XY
	ntmp = mirror(true, true, false);
	if (ntmp.childrenBitmask < nc.childrenBitmask) {
		nc = ntmp;
		x = true; y = true; z = false;
	}

	// XZ
	ntmp = mirror(true, false, true);
	if (ntmp.childrenBitmask < nc.childrenBitmask) {
		nc = ntmp;
		x = true; y = false; z = true;
	}

	// YZ
	ntmp = mirror(false, true, true);
	if (ntmp.childrenBitmask < nc.childrenBitmask) {
		nc = ntmp;
		x = false; y = true; z = true;
	}

	// XYZ
	ntmp = mirror(true, true, true);
	if (ntmp.childrenBitmask < nc.childrenBitmask) {
		nc = ntmp;
		x = true; y = true; z = true;
	}
	return nc;
}

#define BYTETOBINARY(byte)  \
  (byte & 0x80 ? 1 : 0), \
  (byte & 0x40 ? 1 : 0), \
  (byte & 0x20 ? 1 : 0), \
  (byte & 0x10 ? 1 : 0), \
  (byte & 0x08 ? 1 : 0), \
  (byte & 0x04 ? 1 : 0), \
  (byte & 0x02 ? 1 : 0), \
  (byte & 0x01 ? 1 : 0) 

void GeomOctree::Node::print() const {
	printf("%d%d%d%d%d%d%d%d ", BYTETOBINARY(childrenBitmask));
	for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) printf("[%s] ", children[i] != nullNode ? std::to_string(children[i]).c_str() : "NULL");
	printf("\n");
}
```

### src/voxelator/scene.cpp
```
#include "scene.hpp"
#include <sstream>
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <common/format.hpp>
#include <common/paths.hpp>
#include <cstdint>
#include <map>
#include <iostream>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

#include <ctime>

#ifndef _MSC_VER
#	include <string.h>
#	define strtok_s strtok_r
#endif

#define ETA(x) ((clock() - x) / float(CLOCKS_PER_SEC))

Scene::Scene() {
	_bbox.to_empty();

	TMaterial defaultMat;
	sprintf(defaultMat.name, "Voxelator Default Mat");
	defaultMat.ambientColor = glm::vec3(0.1, 0.1, 0.1);
	defaultMat.diffuseColor = glm::vec3(0.9, 0.9, 0.9);
	defaultMat.specColor = glm::vec3(0.2, 0.2, 0.2);
	_materials.push_back(defaultMat);
}
void Scene::loadObj(std::string fileName, bool tryLoadBinCache, bool loadMaterials, bool loadNormals, bool recomputeNormals) {
	
	printf("* Loading '%s'...\n", fileName.c_str());

	if (tryLoadBinCache) {
		std::string binCacheFilename = fileName + ".bincache";
		std::ifstream ifsBinCache(binCacheFilename);
		if (ifsBinCache.good()) {
			printf("\t- Binary cache found! Loading '%s'... ", binCacheFilename.c_str());
			loadBinObj(binCacheFilename);
			printf("OK!\n");
			printf("\t- Loaded %s triangles (%zu)\n", vox::human_readable_quantity(_indexedTris.size()).c_str(), _indexedTris.size());
			printf("\t- Bbox: [%.3f %.3f %.3f]  [%.3f %.3f %.3f]\n", _bbox[0][0], _bbox[0][1], _bbox[0][2], _bbox[1][0], _bbox[1][1], _bbox[1][2]);
			return;
		}
	}
	printf("\t- Reading ASCII OBJ (reading lines)...");
	FILE * file = fopen(fileName.c_str(), "r");
	if (file == NULL) {
		std::cout << "ERROR: Scene:loadOBJ: Can't open file " << fileName << std::endl;
		exit(1);
	}

	// Size estimation
#if 0
	fseek(file, 0L, SEEK_END);
	size_t totalSize = ftell(file);
	fseek(file, 0L, SEEK_SET);
	_indexedTris.reserve(totalSize / 10000);
	_vertices.reserve(totalSize / 10000);
	_normals.reserve(totalSize / 10000);
#endif

	clock_t tini = clock();
	_bbox.to_empty();

	std::uint32_t currentMat = 0; // default material
	size_t nLine = 0, recomputedNormals = 0;
	const int len = 10000;
	char str[len];
	while (fgets(str, len, file) != NULL) {
		nLine++;
		if (nLine % 1000000L == 0) {
			printf(".%zuM.", nLine / 1000000L); fflush(stdout);
			//printf("\b\b\b\b\b%3d %%", int(100 * (float(ftell(file)) / float(totalSize)))); fflush(stdout);
			//printf("\t[%.2fs] Loaded %iM lines (%.2fM tris, %.2fM verts)\n", ETA(tini), nRead / 1000000, _nTris/1000000.0f, _vertices.size()/1000000.0f);
			//printf("\tBbox: [%.4f, %.4f, %.4f]  [%.4f, %.4f, %.4f]\n\n", _bbMin.x, _bbMin.y, _bbMin.z, _bbMax.x, _bbMax.y, _bbMax.z);
		}

		switch (str[0]) {
		case 'v': { //vertex data
			switch (str[1]) {
			case 'n': // normal
			{
				if (loadNormals) {
					glm::vec3 v;
					sscanf(str + 2, "%f %f %f", &v[0], &v[1], &v[2]);
					_normals.push_back(v);
				}
				break;
			}
			case 't': // tex coord
			{
				// Not dealing with this
				break;
			}
			default: //vertex
			{
				glm::vec3 p;
				int k = sscanf(str + 1, "%f %f %f", &p[0], &p[1], &p[2]);
				if (k != 3) {
					printf("Read in line %zu a bad vertex format\n", nLine);
					break;
				}
				_bbox.merge(p);
				_vertices.push_back(p);
				break;
			}
			}
			break;
		}
		case 'f': { // face
			std::vector<std::string> triples;
			char *next_token;
			char *pch = strtok_s(str + 1, " ", &next_token);
			while (pch) {
				std::string s(pch);
				triples.push_back(s);
				pch = strtok_s(NULL, " ", &next_token);
			}
			// throw away last symbol (\n)
			triples.back().resize(triples.back().size() - 1);

			TIndexedTri itri;
			itri.material = currentMat;
			bool noNormal = false;
			for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
				int idxV = 0, idxN = 0;
				int n = sscanf(triples[i].c_str(), "%d//%d", &idxV, &idxN);
				// negative indices
				if (idxV < 0) idxV = int(_vertices.size()) + idxV + 1;
				if (idxN < 0) idxN = int(_normals.size()) + idxN + 1;
				if (n == 0) {
					std::cout << "ERROR:Scene:loadObj: problem parsing face instruction" << std::endl;
				}
				else if (n == 1) { // just vertex info
					itri.verticesIdx[i] = idxV - 1;
					noNormal = true;
				}
				else if (n == 2) { // vertex and normal info
					itri.verticesIdx[i] = idxV - 1;
					if (!recomputeNormals) itri.normalsIdx[i] = idxN - 1;
				}
			}

			if (loadNormals && (noNormal || recomputeNormals)) {
				glm::vec3 e1 = _vertices[itri.verticesIdx[1]] - _vertices[itri.verticesIdx[0]];
				glm::vec3 e2 = _vertices[itri.verticesIdx[2]] - _vertices[itri.verticesIdx[0]];
				glm::vec3 n = glm::cross(e1, e2);
				itri.normalsIdx[0] = itri.normalsIdx[1] = itri.normalsIdx[2] = (std::uint32_t)_normals.size();
				_normals.push_back(n);
				recomputedNormals++;
			}
			_indexedTris.push_back(itri);

			if (triples.size() > 4) { // if it has 4 indices (a quad)
				for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
					int idxV = 0, idxN = 0;
					int n = sscanf(triples[(i + 2) % 4].c_str(), "%d//%d", &idxV, &idxN);
					// negative indices
					if (idxV < 0) idxV = int(_vertices.size()) + idxV + 1;
					if (idxN < 0) idxN = int(_normals.size()) + idxN + 1;
					if (n == 0) {
						std::cout << "ERROR:Scene:loadObj: problem parsing face instruction" << std::endl;
					}
					else if (n == 1) { // just vertex info
						itri.verticesIdx[i] = idxV - 1;
						noNormal = true;
					}
					else if (n == 2) { // vertex and normal info
						itri.verticesIdx[i] = idxV - 1;
						if (!recomputeNormals) itri.normalsIdx[i] = idxN - 1;
					}
				}

				if (loadNormals && (noNormal || recomputeNormals)) {
					glm::vec3 e1 = _vertices[itri.verticesIdx[1]] - _vertices[itri.verticesIdx[0]];
					glm::vec3 e2 = _vertices[itri.verticesIdx[2]] - _vertices[itri.verticesIdx[0]];
					glm::vec3 n = glm::cross(e1, e2);
					itri.normalsIdx[0] = itri.normalsIdx[1] = itri.normalsIdx[2] = (std::uint32_t)_normals.size();
					_normals.push_back(n);
					recomputedNormals++;
				}
				_indexedTris.push_back(itri);
			}

			break;
		}
		case 'm': { // mtllib
			if (loadMaterials) {
				std::string path = vox::path_directory(fileName);
				std::string mtlFileName(str + 7);
				// throw away linebreak character
				mtlFileName.resize(mtlFileName.size() - 1);
				mtlFileName.erase(std::remove(mtlFileName.begin(), mtlFileName.end(), '\r'), mtlFileName.end());
				loadObjMtlLib(path + "/" + mtlFileName);
			}
			break;
		}
		case 'u': { // usemtl
			if (loadMaterials) {
				std::string mtlName(str + 7);
				mtlName.resize(mtlName.size() - 1);
				bool found = false;
				for (std::uint32_t i = 0; i < (std::uint32_t)_materials.size(); i++) {
					if (_materials[i].name == mtlName) {
						currentMat = i;
						found = true;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (!found) std::cout << "WARNING:Scene:loadObj(): Material " << mtlName << " not found in library" << std::endl;
			}
		}
		}
	}

	printf(" OK! [%.2f s]    \n", ETA(tini));
	printf("\t- Loaded %zu triangles (%zu materials) in %.2fM obj lines\n", getNTriangles(), recomputedNormals, nLine / 1000000.0f);
	printf("\t- Bbox: [%.3f %.3f %.3f]  [%.3f %.3f %.3f]\n", _bbox[0][0], _bbox[0][1], _bbox[0][2], _bbox[1][0], _bbox[1][1], _bbox[1][2]);
	fflush(stdout);

	_vertices.shrink_to_fit();
	_normals.shrink_to_fit();
	_materials.shrink_to_fit();
	_indexedTris.shrink_to_fit();

	printf("\t- Saving '%s' binary cache... ", (fileName + ".bincache").c_str());
	saveBinObj(fileName + ".bincache");
	printf("OK!\n");

	fclose(file);
}

#if 1
void Scene::loadTriSoup(std::string filename, bool memoryLoad)
{
	printf("* Loading '%s'...", filename.c_str()); fflush(stdout);

	_streamingTSPFile = std::ifstream(filename, std::ios::in | std::ios::binary);
	if (!_streamingTSPFile.is_open()) {
		printf("ERROR: Couldn't open '%s'\n", filename.c_str());
		exit(1);
	}
	_streamFromTSPFile = !memoryLoad;
	_bbox.to_empty();
	size_t elemsRead = 0;
	_materialMapper.clear();

	while (!_streamingTSPFile.eof()) {
		TriSoupElement tse;
		_streamingTSPFile.read((char *)(&tse), sizeof(TriSoupElement));
		_bbox.merge(tse.vertices[0]);
		_bbox.merge(tse.vertices[1]);
		_bbox.merge(tse.vertices[2]);
		_numTrisTSP++;
		TIndexedTri itri;
		if (memoryLoad) {
			itri.verticesIdx[0] = (std::uint32_t)_vertices.size();
			itri.verticesIdx[1] = itri.verticesIdx[0] + 1;
			itri.verticesIdx[2] = itri.verticesIdx[0] + 2;
			_vertices.push_back(tse.vertices[0]);
			_vertices.push_back(tse.vertices[1]);
			_vertices.push_back(tse.vertices[2]);
			_indexedTris.push_back(itri);
		}

		//material
		glm::vec3 col = tse.colors[0];
		if (col != tse.colors[1] || col != tse.colors[2])
			printf("Scene::loadTriSoup: WARNING! Color per vertex not supported. Using [0]\n");

		auto it = _materialMapper.find(col);
		if (it == _materialMapper.end()) {
			_materialMapper[col] = (std::uint32_t)_materials.size();
			if(memoryLoad) itri.material = (std::uint32_t)_materials.size();
			TMaterial mat;
			mat.diffuseColor = col;
			_materials.push_back(mat);
		}
		else {
			if (memoryLoad) itri.material = (*it).second;
		}
		if (getNTriangles() % 1000000 == 0) printf(".%iM.", int(getNTriangles() / 1000000)); fflush(stdout);

	}

	if(memoryLoad)
		_streamingTSPFile.close();
	else {
		_streamingTSPFile.clear();
		_streamingTSPFile.seekg(0);
	}

	printf(" OK!\n");
	printf("\t- Loaded %s triangles (%zu) (%zu materials)\n", vox::human_readable_quantity(getNTriangles()).c_str(), getNTriangles(), _materials.size());
	printf("\t- Bbox: [%.3f %.3f %.3f]  [%.3f %.3f %.3f]\n", _bbox[0][0], _bbox[0][1], _bbox[0][2], _bbox[1][0], _bbox[1][1], _bbox[1][2]);
}
#else

void Scene::loadTriSoup(std::string filename, bool memoryLoad)
{
	printf("* Loading '%s'...", filename.c_str()); fflush(stdout);

	FILE * file = fopen(filename.c_str(), "rb");
	if (file == NULL) {
		printf("ERROR: Couldn't open '%s'\n", filename.c_str());
		exit(1);
	}
	_bbox.to_empty();
	TriSoupElement * buffer = new TriSoupElement[1000000];
	size_t elemsRead = 0;

	std::map<glm::vec3, unsigned int, Vec3Less> uniqueMats;

	while((elemsRead = fread((void *)buffer, sizeof(TriSoupElement), 1000000, file)) > 0) {
		for (int i = 0; i < elemsRead; ++i) {
			_bbox.merge(buffer[i].vertices[0]);
			_bbox.merge(buffer[i].vertices[1]);
			_bbox.merge(buffer[i].vertices[2]);

			TIndexedTri itri;
			if (memoryLoad) {
				itri.verticesIdx[0] = (std::uint32_t)_vertices.size();
				itri.verticesIdx[1] = itri.verticesIdx[0] + 1;
				itri.verticesIdx[2] = itri.verticesIdx[0] + 2;
				_vertices.push_back(buffer[i].vertices[0]);
				_vertices.push_back(buffer[i].vertices[1]);
				_vertices.push_back(buffer[i].vertices[2]);
			}
			//material
			glm::vec3 col = buffer[i].colors[0];
			if (col != buffer[i].colors[1] || col != buffer[i].colors[2]) {
				col = (buffer[i].colors[0] + buffer[i].colors[1] + buffer[i].colors[2]) / 3.0f;
				printf("Scene::loadTriSoup: WARNING! Blending colors in a face\n");
			}
			auto it = uniqueMats.find(col);
			if (it == uniqueMats.end()) {
				uniqueMats[col] = (unsigned int)_materials.size();
				itri.material = (unsigned int)_materials.size();
				TMaterial mat;
				mat.diffuseColor = col;
				_materials.push_back(mat);
			} else {
				itri.material = (*it).second;
			}

			if(memoryLoad) _indexedTris.push_back(itri);
		}
		if(getNTriangles()%1000000==0) printf(".%iM.", int(getNTriangles() / 1000000)); fflush(stdout);
	}
	

	delete [] buffer;
	fclose(file);

	printf(" OK!\n");
	printf("\t- Loaded %s triangles (%zu) (%zu materials)\n", vox::human_readable_quantity(_indexedTris.size()).c_str(), _indexedTris.size(), _materials.size());
	printf("\t- Bbox: [%.3f %.3f %.3f]  [%.3f %.3f %.3f]\n", _bbox[0][0], _bbox[0][1], _bbox[0][2], _bbox[1][0], _bbox[1][1], _bbox[1][2]);
}

#endif

bool Scene::loadObjMtlLib(std::string fileName) {

	std::ifstream ifs;
	ifs.open(fileName);
	printf(" [Loading %s... ", fileName.c_str());
	if (!ifs.is_open()) {
		printf("WARNING: Couldn't open file.\n");
		return false;
	}

	std::string line, cmd, tmp;
	TMaterial * mat = NULL;
	std::size_t n = 0;
	while (!ifs.eof()) {
		std::getline(ifs, line);
		if (line.empty() || line.find_first_of("#") != std::string::npos)
			continue;
		std::stringstream ss(line);
		ss >> cmd;
		if (cmd == "newmtl") {
			if (mat) _materials.push_back(*mat);
			mat = new TMaterial();
			ss >> tmp;
			strcpy(mat->name, tmp.c_str());
		}
		else if (cmd == "Ka") {
			glm::vec3 c;
			ss >> c[0] >> c[1] >> c[2];
			mat->ambientColor = c;
		}
		else if (cmd == "Kd") {
			glm::vec3 c;
			ss >> c[0] >> c[1] >> c[2];
			mat->diffuseColor = c;
		}
		else if (cmd == "Ks") {
			glm::vec3 c;
			ss >> c[0] >> c[1] >> c[2];
			mat->specColor = c;
		}
		else if (cmd == "Ns") {
			ss >> mat->specCoef;
		}
		else if (cmd == "d" || cmd == "Ts") {
			ss >> mat->transparency;
		}
	}
	if (mat)  _materials.push_back(*mat);
	printf("OK! (%zu materials loaded)] ", _materials.size());
	ifs.close();
	return true;
}

void Scene::saveBinObj(std::string filename) {

	BinObjHeader header;
	header.nVertices = _vertices.size();
	header.nNormals = _normals.size();
	header.nMaterials = _materials.size();
	header.nIndexedTris = _indexedTris.size();
	header.bboxMin[0] = _bbox[0][0];
	header.bboxMin[1] = _bbox[0][1];
	header.bboxMin[2] = _bbox[0][2];
	header.bboxMax[0] = _bbox[1][0];
	header.bboxMax[1] = _bbox[1][1];
	header.bboxMax[2] = _bbox[1][2];
	
	std::ofstream file(filename, std::ios::out | std::ios::binary);
	file.write((char *)(&header), sizeof(BinObjHeader));
	
	file.write((char*)&_vertices[0], _vertices.size() * sizeof(glm::vec3));
	file.write((char*)&_normals[0], _normals.size() * sizeof(glm::vec3));
	file.write((char*)&_materials[0], _materials.size() * sizeof(TMaterial));
	file.write((char*)&_indexedTris[0], _indexedTris.size() * sizeof(TIndexedTri));
	
	file.close();
}

void Scene::loadBinObj(std::string filename) {

	std::ifstream file(filename, std::ios::in | std::ios::binary);
	BinObjHeader header;
	file.read((char *)(&header), sizeof(BinObjHeader));
	
	memcpy(&_bbox[0], &header.bboxMin, 3 * sizeof(float));
	memcpy(&_bbox[1], &header.bboxMax, 3 * sizeof(float));

	_vertices.resize(header.nVertices);
	if (_vertices.size()>0)
		file.read((char *)(&_vertices[0]), header.nVertices*sizeof(glm::vec3));

	_normals.resize(header.nNormals);
	if(_normals.size()>0)
		file.read((char *)(&_normals[0]), header.nNormals*sizeof(glm::vec3));

	_materials.resize(header.nMaterials);
	if (_materials.size()>0)
		file.read((char *)(&_materials[0]), header.nMaterials*sizeof(TMaterial));

	_indexedTris.resize(header.nIndexedTris);
	if (_indexedTris.size()>0)
		file.read((char *)(&_indexedTris[0]), header.nIndexedTris*sizeof(TIndexedTri));

	file.close();
}
```

### src/voxelator/scene.hpp
```
#pragma once

#include <vector>
#include <cstdint>
#include <map>
#include <string>
#include <fstream>

#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <common/aabb.hpp>

// Lexicographic ordering so glm::vec3 can key a std::map
struct Vec3Less {
	bool operator()(const glm::vec3& a, const glm::vec3& b) const {
		if (a.x != b.x) return a.x < b.x;
		if (a.y != b.y) return a.y < b.y;
		return a.z < b.z;
	}
};

class Scene {
public:
	typedef struct _Material {
		char name[256];
		glm::vec3 diffuseColor;
		glm::vec3 specColor;
		glm::vec3 ambientColor;
		float specCoef;
		float transparency;
	} TMaterial;

	typedef struct _IndexedTri {
		std::uint32_t verticesIdx[3];
		std::uint32_t normalsIdx[3];
		std::uint32_t material;
	} TIndexedTri;

public:
	Scene();

	void loadObj(std::string fileName, bool tryLoadBinCache = true, bool loadMaterials = true, bool loadNormals = false, bool recomputeNormals = false);
	void loadTriSoup(std::string filename, bool memoryLoad);

	inline std::vector<glm::vec3> * getVertices() { return &_vertices; }
	inline std::vector<glm::vec3> * getNormals() { return &_normals; }
	inline std::vector<TMaterial> * getMaterials() { return &_materials; }
	inline std::vector<TIndexedTri> * getIndexedGeom() { return &_indexedTris; }

	inline std::size_t getNTriangles() { return _indexedTris.size(); }
	inline std::size_t getNVertices() {
		return _vertices.size();
	}

	inline void getBounds(glm::vec3 & min, glm::vec3 & max) const { min = _bbox[0]; max = _bbox[1]; }
	inline vox::AABB3f getAABB() const { return _bbox; }

	inline const glm::vec3 & getTriangleVertice(std::size_t idTri, int n) {
		return  _vertices[_indexedTris[idTri].verticesIdx[n]];

	}
	inline void getTriangleVertices(std::size_t idTri, glm::vec3 & v0, glm::vec3 & v1, glm::vec3 & v2) {
		v0 = getTriangleVertice(idTri, 0);
		v1 = getTriangleVertice(idTri, 1);
		v2 = getTriangleVertice(idTri, 2);
	}
	inline void getTriangleNormals(std::size_t idTri, glm::vec3 & n0, glm::vec3 & n1, glm::vec3 & n2) {
		n0 = _normals[_indexedTris[idTri].normalsIdx[0]];
		n1 = _normals[_indexedTris[idTri].normalsIdx[1]];
		n2 = _normals[_indexedTris[idTri].normalsIdx[2]];
	}
	inline void getTriangleColor(std::size_t idTri, glm::vec3 & c) {
		c = _materials[getTriangleMaterialId(idTri)].diffuseColor;
	}
	inline size_t getTriangleMaterialId(std::size_t idTri) {
		return (idTri < _indexedTris.size()) ? _indexedTris[idTri].material : 0;
	}

	void saveBinObj(std::string filename);
	void loadBinObj(std::string filename);

	// Streaming
	inline void streamingGetNextTri(glm::vec3 &v0, glm::vec3 &v1, glm::vec3 &v2, std::uint32_t &matIdx) {
		v0 = _vertices[_indexedTris[_streamingPos].verticesIdx[0]];
		v1 = _vertices[_indexedTris[_streamingPos].verticesIdx[1]];
		v2 = _vertices[_indexedTris[_streamingPos].verticesIdx[2]];
		matIdx = _indexedTris[_streamingPos].material;
		_streamingPos++;
	}
	inline void streamingRewind() {
		_streamingPos = 0;
	}
	inline bool streamingEnd() { return (_streamingPos >= _indexedTris.size()); }
	inline std::uint32_t streamingGetPos() { return _streamingPos; }

private:
	std::vector <glm::vec3> _vertices;
	std::vector <glm::vec3> _normals;
	std::vector < TMaterial > _materials;
	std::vector < TIndexedTri > _indexedTris;
	std::map<glm::vec3, std::uint32_t, Vec3Less> _materialMapper;

	bool loadObjMtlLib(std::string fileName);

	vox::AABB3f _bbox;

	std::uint32_t _streamingPos{ 0 };
	std::ifstream _streamingTSPFile;
	bool _streamFromTSPFile;
	size_t _numTrisTSP;

private:
	typedef struct {
		size_t nVertices;
		size_t nNormals;
		size_t nMaterials;
		size_t nIndexedTris;
		float bboxMin[3];
		float bboxMax[3];
	} BinObjHeader;

	typedef struct {
		glm::vec3   vertices[3]; // position
		glm::vec3 normals[3]; // normal
		glm::vec3       colors[3]; // color
	} TriSoupElement;
};
```

### src/voxelator/test_triangle_box.cpp
```
/// TRIANGLE - BOX Intersection Code ------------------------------------
/// by Tomas Akenine-M�ller
/// see http://fileadmin.cs.lth.se/cs/Personal/Tomas_Akenine-Moller/code/
```
